ב״ה
סֵדֶר הַסְּלִחוֹת Seder HaSelichot The Sephardic Order of Penitential Prayers
The Selichot service is recited during the period before Rosh Hashanah and through Yom Kippur.
Following the Sephardic rite, these penitential prayers call upon God’s mercy through
the Thirteen Attributes of Compassion, the merit of the Patriarchs, and heartfelt confession.
EnglishTransliterationעברית
1 Kamti Be'ashmoret קַֽמְתִּי בְּאַשְׁמֹֽרֶת
יש נוהגים לומר קודם "אשרי" פיוט זה אשר חברו רבי משה אבן עזרא זצ"ל.
Some have the custom to recite this piyyut before "Ashrei," composed by Rabbi Moshe ibn Ezra, of blessed memory.
I arose in the night watch, to seek forgiveness for my iniquity. And my soul is darkened, because of my abundant willful sins. Have mercy on Your congregation, the flock of Your pasture. Praiseworthy are those who dwell in Your House; they will ever praise You, Selah.
Kametiy beashemoret, levakesh al avoniy. Venafeshiy shecharechoret, mipeney rov zedoniy: Rachem al adatekha, tzon mareiytekha. Asherey yoshevey veytekha, od yehalelukha selah:
קַֽמְתִּי בְּאַשְׁמֹֽרֶת, לְבַקֵּשׁ עַל עֲוֹנִי. וְנַפְשִׁי שְׁחַרְחֹֽרֶת, מִפְּנֵי רֹב זְדוֹנִי: רַחֵם עַל עֲדָתֶֽךָ, צֹאן מַרְעִיתֶֽךָ. אַשְׁרֵי יֽוֹשְׁבֵֽי בֵיתֶֽךָ, עוֹד יְהַלְלֽוּךָ סֶּֽלָה:
From the throne of Your glorious splendor, shine upon the treasured people. Return the captivity of Your holy ones, and grace as of old Your treasured ones. Display Your wonders of kindness, and build the city of praise. Praiseworthy are those who dwell in Your House; they will ever praise You, Selah.
Mikise kevod hodekha, baser le'am segulah. Shuvah shevut kadeshekha, vechon kekedem segulah: Hafele-na chasadeykha, uveneh iyr tehilah. Asherey yoshevey veytekha, od yehalelukha selah:
מִכִּסֵּא כְּבוֹד הוֹדֶֽךָ, בַּשֵּׂר לְעַם סְגֻלָּה. שֽׁוּבָה שְׁבוּת קָדְשֶֽׁךָ, וְחֹן כְּקֶֽדֶם סְגֻלָּה: הַפְלֶא־נָא חֲסָדֶֽיךָ, וּבְנֵה עִיר תְּהִלָּה. אַשְׁרֵי יֽוֹשְׁבֵֽי בֵיתֶֽךָ, עוֹד יְהַלְלֽוּךָ סֶּֽלָה:
2 Ashrei אַשְׁרֵי יֽוֹשְׁבֵי בֵיתֶךָ
May Your kindness, O Lord, be upon us, as we have hoped in You.
Yehiy-chasedekha Adonai aleynu kaasher ichalenu lakh:
יְהִֽי־חַסְדְּךָ יְהֹוָה עָלֵינוּ כַּֽאֲשֶׁר יִחַלְנוּ לָֽךְ:
Praiseworthy are those who dwell in Your House; they will ever praise You, Selah. Praiseworthy is the people for whom this is so; praiseworthy is the people whose God is the Lord.
Asherey yoshevey veytekha od yehalelukha selah: Asherey haam shekakhah lo asherey haam sheadonai elohayv:
אַשְׁרֵי יֽוֹשְׁבֵי בֵיתֶךָ עוֹד יְֽהַֽלְלוּךָ סֶּֽלָה: אַשְׁרֵי הָעָם שֶׁכָּכָה לּוֹ אַשְׁרֵי הָעָם שֶֽׁיְהֹוָה אֱלֹהָֽיו:
A psalm of David. I will exalt You, my God the King, and bless Your Name forever. Every day I will bless You and praise Your Name forever. Great is the Lord and exceedingly praised; His greatness is beyond investigation. Each generation will praise Your works to the next, and declare Your mighty acts. The splendor of Your glorious majesty, and Your wondrous deeds I will speak of. They will tell of Your awesome power, and Your greatness I will recount. They will pour forth the fame of Your abundant goodness, and sing of Your righteousness. Gracious and merciful is the Lord, slow to anger and great in kindness. The Lord is good to all, and His mercy is upon all His works. All Your works shall thank You, Lord, and Your pious ones shall bless You. They shall speak of the glory of Your kingdom and tell of Your might, to make known to people His mighty acts, and the glorious splendor of His kingdom. Your kingdom is an everlasting kingdom, and Your dominion is throughout all generations. The Lord supports all who fall and straightens all who are bent. The eyes of all look hopefully to You, and You give them their food in its proper time. You open Your hand and satisfy the desire of every living thing. The Lord is righteous in all His ways and kind in all His deeds. The Lord is close to all who call upon Him, to all who call upon Him in truth. He fulfills the desire of those who fear Him; He hears their cry and saves them. The Lord guards all who love Him, and all the wicked He will destroy. My mouth shall speak the praise of the Lord; let all flesh bless His holy Name forever.
Tehilah ledavid aromimekha Elohai hamelekh vaavarekhah shimekha leolam vaed: Bekhal-yom avarekheka vaahalelah shimekha leolam vaed: Gadol Adonai umehulal meod veligedulato eyn cheker: Dor ledor yeshabach maashoykha ugevuroteykha yagiydu: Hadar kevod hodekha vediverey nifeleoteykha ashoychah: Veezuz noreoteykha yomeru ugedulatekha asaperenah: Zekher rav-tuvekha yabiyu vetzidekatekha yeranenu: Chanun verachum Adonai erekh apaim ugedal-chased: Tov-Adonai lakol verachamayv al-kal-maashoyv: Yodukha Adonai kal-maashoykha vachasiydeykha yevarekhukhah: Kevod malekhutekha yomeru ugevuratekha yedaberu: Lehodiya liveney haadam gevurotayv ukhevod hadar malekhuto: Malekhutekha malekhut kal-olamiym umemeshaletekha bekhal-dor vador: Somekh Adonai lekhal-hanofeliym vezokef lekhal-hakefufiym: Eyney-khol eleykha yesaberu veatah noten-lahem et-akhelam beito: Potecha et-yadekha umasebiya lekhal-chay ratzon: Tzadiyk Adonai bekhal-derakhayv vechasiyd bekhal-maashoyv: Karov Adonai lekhal-koreayv lekhol asher ikerauhu veemet: Retzon-yereayv yaashoh veet-shaveatam ishema veyoshiyem: Shomer Adonai et-kal-ohavayv veet kal-hareshaiym yashemiyd: Tehilat Adonai yedaber piy viyvarekh kal-basar shem kadesho leolam vaed: Vaanachenu nevarekh yah meatah vead-olam haleluyah:
תְּהִלָּה לְדָוִד אֲרֽוֹמִמְךָ אֱלוֹהַי הַמֶּלֶךְ וַֽאֲבָֽרְכָה שִׁמְךָ לְעוֹלָם וָעֶֽד: בְּכָל־יוֹם אֲבָֽרְכֶךָּ וַאֲהַֽלְלָה שִׁמְךָ לְעוֹלָם וָעֶֽד: גָּדוֹל יְהֹוָה וּמְהֻלָּל מְאֹד וְלִגְדֻלָּתוֹ אֵין חֵֽקֶר: דּוֹר לְדוֹר יְשַׁבַּח מַֽעֲשֶֹיךָ וּגְבוּרֹתֶיךָ יַגִּֽידוּ: הֲדַר כְּבוֹד הוֹדֶךָ וְדִבְרֵי נִפְלְאֹתֶיךָ אָשִֹֽיחָה: וֶֽעֱזוּז נֽוֹרְאֹתֶיךָ יֹאמֵרוּ וּגְדֻלָּֽתְךָ אֲסַפְּרֶֽנָּה: זֵכֶר רַב־טֽוּבְךָ יַבִּיעוּ וְצִדְקָֽתְךָ יְרַנֵּֽנוּ: חַנּוּן וְרַחוּם יְהֹוָה אֶרֶךְ אַפַּיִם וּגְדָל־חָֽסֶד: טֽוֹב־יְהֹוָה לַכֹּל וְרַֽחֲמָיו עַל־כָּֽל־מַֽעֲשָֹֽיו: יוֹדוּךָ יְהֹוָה כָּֽל־מַֽעֲשֶֹיךָ וַֽחֲסִידֶיךָ יְבָֽרְכֽוּכָה: כְּבוֹד מַלְכֽוּתְךָ יֹאמֵרוּ וּגְבוּרָֽתְךָ יְדַבֵּֽרוּ: לְהוֹדִיעַ | לִבְנֵי הָֽאָדָם גְּבֽוּרֹתָיו וּכְבוֹד הֲדַר מַלְכוּתֽוֹ: מַלְכוּתְךָֽ מַלְכוּת כָּל־עֹֽלָמִים וּמֶֽמְשַׁלְתְּךָ בְּכָל־דּוֹר וָדֹֽר: סוֹמֵךְ יְהֹוָה לְכָל־הַנֹּֽפְלִים וְזוֹקֵף לְכָל־הַכְּפוּפִֽים: עֵֽינֵי־כֹל אֵלֶיךָ יְשַׂבֵּרוּ וְאַתָּה נֽוֹתֵן־לָהֶם אֶת־אָכְלָם בְּעִתּֽוֹ: פּוֹתֵחַ אֶת־יָדֶךָ וּמַשְׂבִּיעַ לְכָל־חַי רָצֽוֹן: צַדִּיק יְהֹוָה בְּכָל־דְּרָכָיו וְחָסִיד בְּכָל־מַֽעֲשָֹֽיו: קָרוֹב יְהֹוָה לְכָל־קֹֽרְאָיו לְכֹל אֲשֶׁר יִקְרָאֻהוּ בֶֽאֱמֶֽת: רְצֽוֹן־יְרֵאָיו יַֽעֲשֶֹה וְֽאֶת־שַׁוְעָתָם יִשְׁמַע וְיֽוֹשִׁיעֵֽם: שׁוֹמֵר יְהֹוָה אֶת־כָּל־אֹֽהֲבָיו וְאֵת כָּל־הָֽרְשָׁעִים יַשְׁמִֽיד: תְּהִלַּת יְהֹוָה יְֽדַבֶּר פִּי וִֽיבָרֵךְ כָּל־בָּשָׂר שֵׁם קָדְשׁוֹ לְעוֹלָם וָעֶֽד: וַֽאֲנַחְנוּ | נְבָרֵךְ יָהּ מֵֽעַתָּה וְעַד־עוֹלָם הַֽלְלוּיָֽהּ:
נוהגים לומר "חצי קדיש":
It is customary to recite the Half Kaddish:
May His great Name be exalted and sanctified in the world He created according to His will. May He establish His kingdom, cause His salvation to sprout, and bring near His anointed, in your lifetime and in your days, speedily and soon; and say Amen. May His great Name be blessed forever and ever. Blessed, praised, glorified, exalted, extolled, honored, elevated, and lauded be the Name of the Holy One, blessed is He, above all blessings, songs, praises, and consolations uttered in the world; and say Amen.
Itegadal veitekadash shemeh raba. Amen. Bealema diy vera kireuteh, veyameliykh malekhuteh, veyatzemach purekaneh, viykarev meshiycheh. Amen. Bechayeykhon uveyomeykhon uvechayey dekhal-beyt iserael, baagala uvizeman kariyv, veimeru amen. Amen. Yehe shemeh raba mevarakh lealam ulealemey alemaya itebarakh, veishetabach, veitepaar, veiteromam, veitenase, veitehadar, veitealeh, veitehalal, shemeh dekudesha beriykh hu. Amen. Leela min kal-birekhata, shiyrata, tishebechata venechamata, daamiyran bealema, veimeru amen. Amen.
יִתְגַּדַּל וְיִתְקַדַּשׁ שְׁמֵהּ רַבָּא. אמן. בְּעָֽלְמָא דִי בְרָא כִּרְעוּתֵהּ, וְיַמְלִיךְ מַלְכוּתֵהּ, וְיַצְמַח פּוּרְקָנֵהּ, וִיקָרֵב מְשִׁיחֵהּ. אמן. בְּחַיֵּיכוֹן וּבְיוֹמֵיכוֹן וּבְחַיֵּי דְכָל־בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל, בַּעֲגָלָא וּבִזְמַן קָרִיב, וְאִמְרוּ אָמֵן. אמן. יְהֵא שְׁמֵהּ רַבָּא מְבָרַךְ לְעָלַם וּלְעָֽלְמֵי עָֽלְמַיָּֽא יִתְבָּרַךְ, וְיִשְׁתַּבַּח, וְיִתְפָּאַר, וְיִתְרוֹמַם, וְיִתְנַשֵּׂא, וְיִתְהַדָּר, וְיִתְעַלֶּה, וְיִתְהַלָּל, שְׁמֵהּ דְּקוּדְשָׁא בְּרִיךְ הוּא. אמן. לְעֵֽלָּא מִן כָּל־בִּרְכָתָא, שִׁירָתָא, תִּשְׁבְּחָתָא וְנֶחָמָתָא, דַּאֲמִירָן בְּעָֽלְמָא, וְאִמְרוּ אָמֵן. אמן.
3 Ben Adam בֶּן־אָדָם מַה־לְּךָ נִרְדָּם
O son of man, why do you slumber? Arise, call out in supplication. Pour forth your words, seek forgiveness from the Lord of lords. Wash and purify yourself, and do not delay, before the days turn away. And hurry, run for help, before Him who dwells on high. And from transgression and wickedness, flee. O please, heed those who know Your Name, Israel the faithful. Yours, O Lord, is the righteousness, and ours the shame.
Ben-adam mah-lekha niredam, kum kera betachanuniym. Shefokh siychah, derosh seliychah, meadon haadoniym. Rechatz utehar, veal teachar, beterem yamiym poniym. Umeherah, rutz leezerah, lifeney shokhen meoniym. Umipesha, vegam resha, berach, ufechad measoniym. Ana sheeh, shimekha yodeey, iserael neemaniym. Lekha Adonai hatzedakah, velanu boshet hapaniym.
בֶּן־אָדָם מַה־לְּךָ נִרְדָּם, קוּם קְרָא בְּתַחֲנוּנִים. שְׁפֹךְ שִׂיחָה, דְּרֹשׁ סְלִיחָה, מֵאֲדוֹן הָאֲדוֹנִים. רְחַץ וּטְהַר, וְאַל תְּאַחַר, בְּטֶֽרֶם יָמִֽים פּוֹנִים. וּמְהֵרָה, רוּץ לְעֶזְרָה, לִפְנֵי שׁוֹכֵֽן מְעוֹנִים. וּמִפֶּֽשַׁע, וְגַם רֶֽשַׁע, בְּרַח, וּפְחַד מֵאֲסוֹנִים. אָֽנָּֽא שְׁעֵה, שִׁמְךָ יֽוֹדְעֵֽי, יִשְׂרָאֵל נֶאֱמָנִים. לְךָ יְהֹוָה הַצְּדָקָה, וְלָֽנוּ בֹּֽשֶׁת הַפָּנִים.
Stand like a man and be strong, to confess your sins. O God, seek earnestly, with gravity. For nothing is ever hidden from Him; every word spoken is read before Him. The Merciful One, He will have mercy upon us, as a father has mercy upon children. Yours, O Lord, is the righteousness, and ours the shame.
Amod kegever, vehitegaber, lehitevadot al chataiym. Yah, el, derosh, bekhoved rosh, lekhaper al peshaiym. Kiy leolam, lo neelam, mimenu, nifelaiym. Vekhal-maamar, asher yeamar, lefanayv hem nikeraiym. Hamerachem, hu yerachem, aleynu, kerachem av al baniym. Lekha Adonai hatzedakah, velanu boshet hapaniym.
עֲמֹד כְּגֶֽבֶר, וְהִתְגַּבֵּר, לְהִתְוַדּוֹת עַל חֲטָאִים. יָהּ, אֵל, דְּרֹשׁ, בְּכֹֽבֶד רֹאשׁ, לְכַפֵּר עַל פְּשָׁעִים. כִּי לְעוֹלָם, לֹא נֶעְלַם, מִמֶּֽנּוּ, נִפְלָאִים. וְכָל־מַאֲמָר, אֲשֶׁר יֵאָמַר, לְפָנָיו הֵם נִקְרָאִים. הַמְרַחֵם, הוּא יְרַחֵם, עָלֵֽינוּ, כְּרַחֵם אָב עַל בָּנִים. לְךָ יְהֹוָה הַצְּדָקָה, וְלָֽנוּ בֹּֽשֶׁת הַפָּנִים.
Yours, O Lord, is the righteousness, and ours the shame. How shall we complain, and what shall we say? Our burden of sin is so heavy.
Lekha Adonai hatzedakah, velanu boshet hapaniym: Mah-niteonen umah-nomar, mah-nedaber umah-nitzetadak. Nachepesah derakheynu venachekorah, venashuvah eleykha.
לְךָ יְהֹוָה הַצְּדָקָה, וְלָֽנוּ בֹּֽשֶׁת הַפָּנִים: מַה־נִּתְאוֹנֵן וּמַה־נֹּאמַר, מַה־נְּדַבֵּר וּמַה־נִּצְטַדָּק. נַחְפְּשָׂה דְרָכֵֽינוּ וְנַחְקֹֽרָה, וְנָשֽׁוּבָה אֵלֶֽיךָ.
For Your right hand is outstretched to receive those who return.
Kiy yemiynekha feshutah, lekabel shaviym.
כִּי יְמִֽינְךָֽ פְשׁוּטָה, לְקַבֵּל שָׁבִים.
Those who return to You with all their heart, You accept their cry with Your mercies.
Shaviym eleykha bekhal-lev, shaveatam tekabel berachameykha.
שָׁבִים אֵלֶֽיךָ בְּכָל־לֵב, שַׁוְעָתָם תְּקַבֵּל בְּרַחֲמֶֽיךָ.
In Your abundant mercy we have come before You. Like the poor and the destitute we knock upon Your doors.
Berachameykha harabiym banu lefaneykha, kedaliym ukherashiym dafakenu delateykha.
בְּרַחֲמֶֽיךָ הָרַבִּים בָּֽאנוּ לְפָנֶֽיךָ, כְּדַלִּים וּכְרָשִׁים דָּפַֽקְנוּ דְלָתֶֽיךָ.
At Your doors we knock, O merciful and gracious One; do not send us away empty from before You.
Delateykha dafakenu rachum vechanun, al teshiyvenu reykam milefaneykha.
דְּלָתֶֽיךָ דָפַֽקְנוּ רַחוּם וְחַנּוּן, אַל תְּשִׁיבֵֽנוּ רֵיקָם מִלְּפָנֶֽיךָ.
From before You, our King, do not send us away empty, for You hear prayer.
Milefaneykha malekenu reykam al teshiyvenu, kiy atah shomea tefilah.
מִלְּפָנֶֽיךָ מַלְכֵּֽנוּ רֵיקָם אַל תְּשִׁיבֵֽנוּ, כִּי אַתָּה שׁוֹמֵֽעַ תְּפִלָּה.
O You who hear prayer, to You all flesh shall come.
Shomea tefilah, adeykha kal-basar yavou.
שׁוֹמֵֽעַ תְּפִלָּה, עֲדֶֽיךָ כָּל־בָּשָׂר יָבֹֽאוּ.
O You who hear supplication, to You all spirits shall come.
Shomea techinah, eleykha kal-haruchot yavou.
שׁוֹמֵֽעַ תְּחִנָּה, אֵלֶֽיךָ כָּל־הָרוּחוֹת יָבֹֽאוּ.
Let all spirits come before You, and every soul.
Yavou eleykha haruchot, vekhal-haneshamah.
יָבֹֽאוּ אֵלֶֽיךָ הָרוּחוֹת, וְכָל־הַנְּשָׁמָה.
The soul is Yours and the body is Your handiwork; have compassion on Your labor.
Haneshamah lakh vehaguf paolakh, chusah al amalakh.
הַנְּשָׁמָה לָךְ וְהַגּוּף פָּעֳלָךְ, חֽוּסָה עַל עֲמָלָךְ.
The soul is Yours and the body is Your handiwork; O Rock to whom none compares, have compassion on Your labor.
Haneshamah lakh vehaguf paolakh, tzur asher eyn domeh lakh, chusah al amalakh.
הַנְּשָׁמָה לָךְ וְהַגּוּף פָּעֳלָךְ, צוּר אֲשֶׁר אֵין דּֽוֹמֶה לָּֽךְ, חֽוּסָה עַל עֲמָלָךְ.
The soul and the body are Your handiwork. O Lord, act for the sake of Your Name.
Haneshamah vehaguf paolakh, Adonai aseh lemaan shemekha.
הַנְּשָׁמָה וְהַגּוּף פָּעֳלָךְ, יְהֹוָה עֲשֵׂה לְמַֽעַן שִׁמְךָ.
We have come for the sake of Your Name. O Lord, act for the sake of Your Name.
Atanu al shimekha, Adonai aseh lemaan shemekha.
אָתָֽאנוּ עַל שִׁמְךָ, יְהֹוָה עֲשֵׂה לְמַֽעַן שִׁמְךָ.
For the sake of Your Name, for You are a gracious and merciful King.
Baavur shimekha, kiy el melekh chanun verachum shemekha.
בַּעֲבוּר שִׁמְךָ, כִּי אֵל מֶֽלֶךְ חַנּוּן וְרַחוּם שְׁמֶֽךָ.
Your Name is called upon us, O Lord our God.
Shimekha nikera aleynu, Adonai eloheynu.
שִׁמְךָ נִקְרָא עָלֵֽינוּ, יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ.
Your Name is called in our midst; do not forsake us, O Lord our God.
Shimekha nikera bekirebenu, al taniychenu Adonai eloheynu.
שִׁמְךָ נִקְרָא בְּקִרְבֵּֽנוּ, אַל תַּנִּיחֵֽנוּ יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ.
Our God, we are ashamed of our deeds and humiliated by our iniquities.
Eloheynu boshenu vemaaseynu, venikhelamenu baavonoteynu.
אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ בֹּֽשְׁנוּ בְמַעֲשֵֽׂינוּ, וְנִכְלַֽמְנוּ בַּעֲוֹנוֹתֵֽינוּ.
We have no mouth to respond and no forehead to lift our head.
Eyn lanu peh lehashiyv, velo metzach lehariym rosh.
אֵין לָֽנוּ פֶה לְהָשִׁיב, וְלֹא מֵֽצַח לְהָרִים רֹאשׁ.
For our backslidings are many; we have sinned against You.
Kiy rabu meshuvoteynu, lekha chatanu.
כִּי רַבּֽוּ מְשׁוּבוֹתֵֽינוּ, לְךָ חָטָֽאנוּ.
We have sinned with our ancestors; we have acted perversely, we have acted wickedly.
Chatanu im avoteynu, heeviynu hireshaenu.
חָטָֽאנוּ עִם אֲבוֹתֵֽינוּ, הֶעֱוִֽינוּ הִרְשַֽׁעְנוּ.
What can we say before You, O Lord our God? What can we speak, and how can we justify ourselves?
Mah-nomar lefaneykha Adonai eloheynu, mah-nedaber umah-nitzetadak.
מַה־נֹּאמַר לְפָנֶֽיךָ יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ, מַה־נְּדַבֵּר וּמַה־נִּצְטַדָּק.
What can we say before You who dwells on high? And what can we tell before You who abides in the heavens? Do You not know all things, hidden and revealed?
Mah-nomar lefaneykha yoshev marom, umah-nesaper lefaneykha shokhen shechakiym.
מַה־נֹּאמַר לְפָנֶֽיךָ יוֹשֵׁב מָרוֹם, וּמַה־נְּסַפֵּר לְפָנֶֽיךָ שׁוֹכֵן שְׁחָקִים.
Indeed the hidden and the revealed, You know.
Halo hanisetarot vehanigelot, atah yodea.
הֲלֹא הַנִּסְתָּרוֹת וְהַנִּגְלוֹת, אַתָּה יוֹדֵֽעַ.
You know the mysteries of the universe, and the hidden secrets of all living.
Atah yodea razey olam, vetaalumot siterey khal-chay.
אַתָּה יוֹדֵֽעַ רָזֵי עוֹלָם, וְתַעֲלוּמוֹת סִתְרֵי כָל־חָי.
You search all the chambers of the innermost parts; You test the conscience and the heart.
Atah chofes kal-chaderey baten, roeh khelayot valev.
אַתָּה חוֹפֵשׂ כָּל־חַדְרֵי בָֽטֶן, רוֹאֶה כְלָיוֹת וָלֵב.
Nothing is hidden from You, and nothing is concealed from Your eyes.
Eyn davar neelam mimakh, veeyn nisetar mineged eyneykha.
אֵין דָּבָר נֶעְלָם מִמָּֽךְ, וְאֵין נִסְתָּר מִנֶּֽגֶד עֵינֶֽיךָ.
If our iniquities testify against us, O Lord, act for the sake of Your Name.
Im avoneynu anu vanu, Adonai aseh lemaan shemekha.
אִם עֲוֹנֵֽינוּ עָֽנוּ בָֽנוּ, יְהֹוָה עֲשֵׂה לְמַֽעַן שְׁמֶֽךָ.
If You preserved iniquities, O Lord, who could stand?
Im avonot tishemar-yah, Adonai miy yaamod.
אִם עֲוֹנוֹת תִּשְׁמָר־יָהּ, יְהֹוָה מִי יַעֲמֹד.
For with You there is forgiveness, that You may be feared.
Kiy imekha haseliychah, lemaan tivare.
כִּי עִמְּךָ הַסְּלִיחָה, לְמַֽעַן תִּוָּרֵא.
For with You is the source of life; in Your light we see light.
Kiy imekha mekor chaiym, beorekha nireeh-or.
כִּי עִמְּךָ מְקוֹר חַיִּים, בְּאֽוֹרְךָֽ נִרְאֶה־אוֹר.
For not on account of our righteous deeds do we cast our supplications before You, but on account of Your abundant mercies.
Kiy lo al-tzidekoteynu anachenu mapiyliym tachanuneynu lefaneykha, kiy al-rachameykha harabiym.
כִּי לֹא עַל־צִדְקוֹתֵֽינוּ אֲנַֽחְנוּ מַפִּילִים תַּחֲנוּנֵֽינוּ לְפָנֶֽיךָ, כִּי עַל־רַחֲמֶֽיךָ הָרַבִּים.
4 Lema'ankha Elohai לְמַעַנְךָ אֱלֹהַי
יש נוהגים לומר פיוט זה בעשרת ימי תשובה, אשר חברו רבי דויד אבן בקודה ז"ל. וכן הוא מנהג יוצאי בבל ודמשק ומרוקו. ומנהג יוצאי חאלב לאומרו בכל ימי הסליחות, וכן מנהג מרן הראש"ל שליט"א.
Some have the custom to recite this piyyut during the Ten Days of Repentance, composed by Rabbi David ibn Bakuda, of blessed memory. This is the custom of those from Babylonia, Damascus, and Morocco. The custom of those from Aleppo is to recite it throughout all the days of Selichot, and this is also the custom of Maran the Rishon LeTzion, may he live long.
For Your sake, my God, and not for our sake; see, we stand before You destitute, without deeds or righteousness. What shall we say before You who dwells on high? And what shall we tell You who abides in the heavens? Do You not know all that is hidden and revealed?
Lemaanekha Elohai, retzeh am lekha shichar. Lechalot paneykha, bemaamad hashachar. Adonay hakeshiyvah vaaseh, al teachar.
לְמַעַנְךָ אֱלֹהַי, רְצֵה עַם לְךָ שִׁחַר. לְחַלּוֹת פָּנֶֽיךָ, בְּמַעֲמַד הַשַּֽׁחַר. אֲדֹנָי הַקְשִֽׁיבָה וַעֲשֵׂה, אַל תְּאַחַר.
For Your sake, my God, draw up from the depths of the sea those sated with bitterness and wormwood, in the house of their wandering and captivity. And look upon their oppression and their affliction, and do not turn to their rebellion. And incline Your ear to their cry, in the dawn prayer. O Lord, heed and act, do not delay.
Lemaanekha Elohai, deleh mimetzulot yam. Seveey rosh velaan, beveyt nodam veshivyam*. Veshur lachetzam veaneyam, veal tefen lemereyam. Vehat ozen leshaveam, bitefilat hashachar. Adonay hakeshiyvah vaaseh, al teachar.
לְמַעַנְךָ אֱלֹהַי, דְּלֵה מִמְּצוּלוֹת יָם. שְׂבֵֽעֵי רֹֽאשׁ וְלַֽעַן, בְּבֵית נוֹדָם וְשִׁבְיָם. וְשׁוּר לַחְצָם וְעָנְיָם, וְאַל תֵּֽפֶן לְמֶרְיָם. וְהַט אֹֽזֶן לְשַׁוְעָם, בִּתְפִלַּת הַשַּֽׁחַר. אֲדֹנָי הַקְשִֽׁיבָה וַעֲשֵׂה, אַל תְּאַחַר.
For Your sake, my God, perform a sign for good. And grace afflicted souls, thirsting for waters of salvation. And gather the dispersed, scattered in every direction, whose skin has darkened from the abundance of troubles. O Lord, heed and act, do not delay.
Lemaanekha Elohai, vaaseh letovah ot. Vechon nefashot atzuvot, lemey yesha tzemeot. Vekabetz nidachiym, pezuriym bekhal-peot. Asher merov telaot, oram meod shachar. Adonay hakeshiyvah vaaseh, al teachar.
לְמַעַנְךָ אֱלֹהַי, וַעֲשֵׂה לְטֽוֹבָה אוֹת. וְחֹן נְפָשׁוֹת עֲצוּבוֹת, לְמֵי יֶֽשַׁע צְמֵאוֹת. וְקַבֵּץ נִדָּחִים, פְּזוּרִים בְּכָל־פֵּאוֹת. אֲשֶׁר מֵרֹב תְּלָאוֹת, עוֹרָם מְאֹד שָׁחַֽר. אֲדֹנָי הַקְשִֽׁיבָה וַעֲשֵׂה, אַל תְּאַחַר.
For Your sake, my God, let Your mercies be stirred. And from the dwelling of Your heavens, hear the voice of Your people. Those who bear Your anger, Your wrath, and Your fury; and from bearing Your terror, their hearts are deeply anguished. O Lord, heed and act, do not delay.
Lemaanekha Elohai, yehemu rachameykha. Umimeon shameykha, shema kol amekha. Soveley apekha, kitzepekha vezaamekha. Umineso eymeykha, libam meod secharechar. Adonay hakeshiyvah vaaseh, al teachar.
לְמַעַנְךָ אֱלֹהַי, יֶהֱמוּ רַחֲמֶֽיךָ. וּמִמְּעוֹן שָׁמֶֽיךָ, שְׁמַע קוֹל עַמֶּֽךָ. סֽוֹבְלֵֽי אַפְּךָ, קִצְפְּךָ וְזַעֲמֶֽךָ. וּמִנְּשׂוֹא אֵימֶֽיךָ, לִבָּם מְאֹד סְחַרְחַר. אֲדֹנָי הַקְשִֽׁיבָה וַעֲשֵׂה, אַל תְּאַחַר.
For Your sake, my God, have mercy upon Your lowly ones. And gladden them from their sorrow, and with Your counsel comfort them. And display Your wondrous kindness, and have pity and mercy. Those borne from the womb, and from the earliest dawn. O Lord, heed and act, do not delay.
Lemaanekha Elohai, daleykha terachem. Vesamechem miygonam, uvaatzatekha tanechem. Vehafele chasadeykha, vechusah na verachem. Amusiym mibeten, umerechem mishechar. Adonay hakeshiyvah vaaseh, al teachar.
לְמַעַנְךָ אֱלֹהַי, דַּלֶּֽיךָ תְּרַחֵם. וְשַׂמְּחֵם מִיגוֹנָם, וּבַעֲצָֽתְךָֽ תַּנְחֵם. וְהַפְלֵֽא חֲסָדֶֽיךָ, וְחֽוּסָה נָּא וְרַחֵֽם. עֲמוּסִים מִבֶּֽטֶן, וּמֵרֶֽחֶם מִשְׁחָר. אֲדֹנָי הַקְשִֽׁיבָה וַעֲשֵׂה, אַל תְּאַחַר.
5 Adonai Shim'ah אֲדֹנָי | שְׁמָעָה
O Lord, hear! O Lord, forgive! O Lord, heed and act, do not delay! For Your own sake, my God, for Your Name is called upon Your city and upon Your people.
Adonay shemaah adonay selachah adonay hakeshiyvah vaaseh al-teachar lemaanekha Elohai kiy-shimekha nikera al-iyrekha veal-amekha: Hashiyvenu Adonai eleykha venashuvah chadesh yameynu kekedem:
אֲדֹנָי | שְׁמָעָה אֲדֹנָי | סְלָחָה אֲדֹנָי הַֽקְשִׁיבָה וַֽעֲשֵׂה אַל־תְּאַחַר לְמַֽעַנְךָ אֱלֹהַי כִּֽי־שִׁמְךָ נִקְרָא עַל־עִֽירְךָ וְעַל־עַמֶּֽךָ: הֲשִׁיבֵנוּ יְהֹוָה | אֵלֶיךָ וְֽנָשׁוּבָה חַדֵּשׁ יָמֵינוּ כְּקֶֽדֶם:
The Thirteen Attributes of Mercy (י״ג מידות) are recited aloud by the congregation together.
6 Shevet Yehuda שֵֽׁבֶט יְהוּדָה
נוהגים לעמוד ב"שבט יהודה".
It is customary to stand for "Shevet Yehuda."
The tribe of Judah is in distress and anguish; does a lion roar in the forest without prey? Parents and children await Your salvation, the poor and the destitute. Stand in the breach lest we become a mockery. Why, O Lord, do You stand afar? Your dove has reached the gates of death; O You who sits upon the Cherubim, appear! Grant us strength and courage; O our Rock, have mercy and bring our deliverance near.
Shevet yehudah bedochak uvetzaar, haisheag areyeh bayaar. Mekaviym yeshuatekha avot uvaniym, haaniiym vehaeveyoniym. Amod baperetz bal niheyeh lisechok, lamah Adonai taamod berachok. Yonatekha ad shaarey mavet higiyah, yoshev hakeruviym hofiyah. Havah lanu ezerat mitzar, hayad Adonai tiketzar. Chadesh yameynu begalut yashan, urah lamah tiyshan. Zekhor baneykha beeretz lo lahem, vezar lo ikerav aleyhem. Ketz hanechetam galeh legalemudah, isemach har tziyon tagelenah benot yehudah. Shaveatenu taaleh lishemey meromiym, el melekh yoshev al kise rachamiym.
שֵֽׁבֶט יְהוּדָה בְּדֹֽחַק וּבְצַֽעַר, הֲיִשְׁאַג אַרְיֵה בַּיַּֽעַר. מְקַוִּים יְשׁוּעָֽתְךָֽ אָבוֹת וּבָנִים, הָעֲנִיִּים וְהָאֶבְיוֹנִים. עֲמֹד בַּפֶּֽרֶץ בַּל נִהְיֶה לִשְׂחֹק, לָמָֽה יְהֹוָה תַּעֲמֹד בְּרָחוֹק. יוֹנָֽתְךָֽ עַד שַׁעֲרֵי מָֽוֶת הִגִּֽיעָה, יוֹשֵׁב הַכְּרוּבִים הוֹפִֽיעָה. הָֽבָה לָּֽנוּ עֶזְרַת מִצָּר, הֲיַד יְהֹוָה תִּקְצָר. חַדֵּשׁ יָמֵֽינוּ בְּגָלוּת יָשָׁן, עֽוּרָה לָֽמָּה תִישָׁן. זְכֹר בָּנֶֽיךָ בְּאֶֽרֶץ לֹא לָהֶם, וְזָר לֹא יִקְרַב אֲלֵיהֶם. קֵץ הַנֶּחְתָּם גַּלֵּה לְגַלְמוּדָה, יִשְׂמַח הַר צִיּוֹן תָּגֵֽלְנָה בְּנוֹת יְהוּדָה. שַׁוְעָתֵֽנוּ תַּעֲלֶה לִשְׁמֵי מְרוֹמִים, אֶל מֶֽלֶךְ יוֹשֵׁב עַל כִּסֵּא רַחֲמִים.
The Thirteen Attributes of Mercy (י״ג מידות) are recited aloud by the congregation together.
God, King, who sits upon a throne of mercy, and acts with loving-kindness, forgiving the iniquities of His people, removing them one by one. Increasing forgiveness for sinners and pardon for transgressors. Dealing righteously with all flesh and spirit; not according to their evil does He requite them. God, You have taught us to recite the thirteen attributes; remember for us today the covenant of the thirteen attributes, as You made known of old to the humble one, and so it is written in Your Torah: And the Lord descended in the cloud and stood with him there, and proclaimed by the Name of the Lord. And there it is said:
El melekh yoshev al kise rachamiym, umitenaheg bachasiydut, mochel avonot amo, maaviyr rishon rishon. Marebeh mechiylah lachataiym, useliychah laposheiym. Oseh tzedakot im kal-basar verucha, lo kheraatam lahem gomel. El, horetanu lomar midot shelosh-esereh, zekhor lanu hayom beriyt shelosh-esereh, kemo shehodaeta leanav mikedem, vekhen katuv betoratakh: Vayered Adonai beanan vaiteyatzev imo sham vaikera veshem Adonai : Vesham neemar:
אֵֽל מֶֽלֶךְ יוֹשֵׁב עַל כִּסֵּא רַחֲמִים, וּמִתְנַהֵג בַּחֲסִידוּת, מוֹחֵל עֲוֹנוֹת עַמּוֹ, מַעֲבִיר רִאשׁוֹן רִאשׁוֹן. מַרְבֶּה מְחִילָה לַחַטָּאִֽים, וּסְלִיחָה לַפּֽוֹשְׁעִֽים. עוֹשֵׂה צְדָקוֹת עִם כָּל־בָּשָׂר וְרֽוּחַ, לֹא כְרָעָתָם לָהֶם גּוֹמֵל. אֵֽל, הוֹרֵתָֽנוּ לוֹמַר מִדּוֹת שְׁלֹשׁ־עֶשְׂרֵה, זְכֹר לָֽנוּ הַיּוֹם בְּרִית שְׁלֹשׁ־עֶשְׂרֵה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוֹדַֽעְתָּ לֶעָנָו מִקֶּֽדֶם, וְכֵן כָּתוּב בְּתוֹרָתָךְ: וַיֵּרֶד יְהוָֹה בֶּֽעָנָן וַיִּתְיַצֵּב עִמּוֹ שָׁם וַיִּקְרָא בְשֵׁם יְהוָֹֽה : וְשָׁם נֶאֱמַר:
And the Lord passed before him and proclaimed: The Lord, the Lord, God, compassionate and gracious, slow to anger, and abundant in kindness and truth. Preserving kindness for thousands of generations, forgiving iniquity, transgression, and sin, and He cleanses.
Vayaavor Adonai al-panayv vaikera Adonai Adonai el rachum vechanun erekh apaim verav-chesed veemet: Notzer chesed laalafiym nose avon vafesha vechataah venakeh.
וַיַּֽעֲבֹר יְהֹוָה | עַל־פָּנָיו וַיִּקְרָא יְהֹוָה | יְהֹוָה אֵל רַחוּם וְחַנּוּן אֶרֶךְ אַפַּיִם וְרַב־חֶסֶד וֶֽאֱמֶֽת: נֹצֵר חֶסֶד לָֽאֲלָפִים נֹשֵׂא עָוֹן וָפֶשַׁע וְחַטָּאָה וְנַקֵּֽה.
And forgive our iniquity and our sin, and take us as Your heritage.
Vesalacheta laavonenu ulechatatenu unechaletanu:
וְסָֽלַחְתָּ לַֽעֲוֹנֵנוּ וּלְחַטָּאתֵנוּ וּנְחַלְתָּֽנוּ:
7 Rachamana רַחֲמָנָא
The leader recites each line and the congregation responds: בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר — "For the sake of 'And He passed before him.'"
O Merciful One, remember for us the covenant of Abraham the beloved, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, idekar lan keyameh deaveraham rechiyma, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, אִדְכַּר לָן קְיָמֵהּ דְּאַבְרָהָם רְחִימָֽא, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, remember for us the covenant of Isaac who was bound, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, idekar lan keyameh deitzechak akeyda, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, אִדְכַּר לָן קְיָמֵהּ דְּיִצְחָק עֲקֵידָֽא, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, remember for us the covenant of Jacob the wholehearted, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, idekar lan keyameh deyaakov sheleyma, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, אִדְכַּר לָן קְיָמֵהּ דְּיַעֲקֹב שְׁלֵימָֽא, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, remember for us the covenant of Moses the prophet, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, idekar lan keyameh demosheh neviyah, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, אִדְכַּר לָן קְיָמֵהּ דְּמֹשֶׁה נְבִיאָֽה, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, remember for us the covenant of Aaron the priest, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, idekar lan keyameh deaharon kahana, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, אִדְכַּר לָן קְיָמֵהּ דְּאַהֲרֹן כַּהֲנָֽא, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, remember for us the merit of Joseph the righteous, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, idekar lan zekhuteh deyosef tzadiyka, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, אִדְכַּר לָן זְכוּתֵהּ דְּיוֹסֵף צַדִּיקָֽא, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, remember the covenant of David the anointed king, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, idekar lan keyameh dedavid maleka meshiycha, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, אִדְכַּר לָן קְיָמֵהּ דְּדָוִד מַלְכָּֽא מְשִׁיחָֽא, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, remember the covenant of Pinchas the zealot, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, idekar lan keyameh defiynechas kanaah, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, אִדְכַּר לָן קְיָמֵהּ דְּפִֽינְחָֽס קַנָּאָה, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, remember the prayer of Solomon the king, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, idekar lan tzeloteh dishelomoh maleka, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, אִדְכַּר לָן צְלוֹתֵהּ דִּשְׁלֹמֹה מַלְכָּֽא, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, raise Your right hand and cause Your salvation to sprout, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, areym yemiynakh veatzemach purekanakh, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, אָרֵים יְמִינָךְ וְאַצְמַח פֻּרְקָנָךְ, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, with downcast faces we come to call before You; have mercy upon us, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, bekhisufey apiyn ateyna lemikerey kamakh rachem alan, bediyl vayaavor:
רַחֲמָנָא, בְּכִסּוּפֵי אַפִּין אָתֵֽינָא לְמִקְרֵי קַמָּךְ רַחֵם עֲלָן, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר:
O Merciful One, reveal Your might and redeem us, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, galey gevuretakh uferok lan, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, גַּלֵּי גְבֻרְתָּךְ וּפְרֹק לָן, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, bring forth our judgment to the light, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, diynan apeyk linehora, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, דִּינָן אַפֵּיק לִנְהוֹרָא, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, decree upon us a verdict of good life, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, diyna dechayey tavey gezor alan, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, דִּינָא דְחַיֵּי טָבֵי גְּזֹר עֲלָן, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, bestow Your splendor upon us, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, haderakh shaviy alan, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, הַדְרָךְ שַׁוִּי עֲלָן, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, do not exact punishment from us according to our evil deeds, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, vela titepera keovadana viyshiyn minan, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, וְלָא תִתְפְּרַע כְּעוֹבָדָֽנָא בִישִׁין מִנָּן, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, let Your radiance shine upon us, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, ziyvakh asherey alan, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, זִיוָךְ אַשְׁרֵי עֲלָן, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, search our merits for us, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, zakhevan chapes-lan, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, זַכְוָן חַפֶּשׂ־לָן, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, consider good deeds for us, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, chashov alan tavevan, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, חֲשׁוֹב עֲלָן טַבְוָן, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, bring abundant good upon us, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, tavevan sagiyan ayeytiy alan, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, טַבְוָן סַגִּיאָן אַיְיתִי עֲלָן, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, let Your mercies flow upon us, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, itegalegelun rachamakh alan, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, יִתְגַּלְגְּלוּן רַחֲמָךְ עֲלָן, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
בעשרת ימי תשובה מוסיפים:
During the Ten Days of Repentance, the following is added:
O Merciful One, inscribe us in the Book of Life, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, kateviynan besifera dechayey, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, כַּתְבִינָן בְּסִפְרָא דְחַיֵּי, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, inscribe us in the Book of Mercy, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, kateviynan besifera derachamey, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, כַּתְבִינָן בְּסִפְרָא דְרַחֲמֵי, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, inscribe us in the Book of the Righteous and Pious, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, kateviynan besifera detzadiykey vachasiydey, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, כַּתְבִינָן בְּסִפְרָא דְצַדִּיקֵי וַחֲסִידֵי, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, inscribe us in the Book of the Upright and Pure, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, kateviynan besifera diysharey utemiymey, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, כַּתְבִינָן בְּסִפְרָא דִישָׁרֵי וּתְמִימֵי, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, inscribe us in the Book of Good Livelihood, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, kateviynan besifera defarenasata taveta umezoney tavey, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, כַּתְבִינָן בְּסִפְרָא דְפַרְנָסָתָא טַבְתָּא וּמְזוֹנֵי טָבֵי, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
עד כאן מוסיפים בעשרת ימי תשובה.
End of the addition for the Ten Days of Repentance.
O Merciful One, subdue wrath and anger from us, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, kevosh chemeta verugeza minan, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, כְּבֹשׁ חֶמְתָּא וְרֻגְזָא מִנָּן, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, do not make a total end of us, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, la taebeyd gemiyra lan, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, לָא תַעְבֵּיד גְּמִֽירָא לָן, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, forgive and pardon our sins and transgressions, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, mechol ushevok lechoviyn velaavayan, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, מְחֹל וּשְׁבֹק לְחוֹבִין וְלַעֲוָיָן, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, let the light of Your goodness shine upon us, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, nehor tuvakh anehar alan, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, נְהוֹר טוּבָךְ אַנְהַר עֲלָן, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, support and sustain us, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, seiyd usemiykh hevey lan, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, סְעִיד וּסְמִיךְ הֱוֵי לָן, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, perform a good sign with us, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, aveyd imana ata letav, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, עֲבֵיד עִמָּֽנָא אָתָֽא לְטַב, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, open the heavens to our prayers, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, petach shemaya litzelotiyn, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, פְּתַח שְׁמַיָּא לִצְלוֹתִין, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, accept our prayer with favor, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, tzelotana kabel beraava, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, צְלוֹתָנָא קַבֵּל בְּרַעֲוָא, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, accept our prayers and petitions in this time of distress, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, kabel tzelotiyn uvautiyn beidan aketiyn, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, קַבֵּל צְלוֹתִין וּבָעוּתִין בְּעִדָּן עַקְתִּין, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, have mercy on our souls, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, rachem al nishematiyn, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, רַחֵם עַל נִשְׁמָתִין, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, bring a good year upon us, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, shata taveta ayeytiy alan, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, שַׁתָּא טַבְתָּא אַיְיתִי עֲלָן, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, turn from Your anger, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, tuv merugezakh, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, תּוּב מֵרֻגְזָךְ, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
O Merciful One, let us not return empty from before You, for the sake of 'And He passed over.'
Rachamana, vela nehedar reykam min kamakh, bediyl vayaavor.
רַחֲמָנָא, וְלָא נֶהְדָּר רֵיקָם מִן קַמָּךְ, בְּדִיל וַיַּעֲבֹר.
And the Lord passed before him and proclaimed: The Lord, the Lord, God, compassionate and gracious, slow to anger, and abundant in kindness and truth. Preserving kindness for thousands of generations, forgiving iniquity, transgression, and sin, and He cleanses.
Vayaavor Adonai al-panayv vaikera Adonai Adonai el rachum vechanun erekh apaim verav-chesed veemet: Notzer chesed laalafiym nose avon vafesha vechataah venakeh.
וַיַּֽעֲבֹר יְהֹוָה | עַל־פָּנָיו וַיִּקְרָא יְהֹוָה | יְהֹוָה אֵל רַחוּם וְחַנּוּן אֶרֶךְ אַפַּיִם וְרַב־חֶסֶד וֶֽאֱמֶֽת: נֹצֵר חֶסֶד לָֽאֲלָפִים נֹשֵׂא עָוֹן וָפֶשַׁע וְחַטָּאָה וְנַקֵּֽה.
And forgive our iniquity and our sin, and take us as Your heritage.
Vesalacheta laavonenu ulechatatenu unechaletanu:
וְסָֽלַחְתָּ לַֽעֲוֹנֵנוּ וּלְחַטָּאתֵנוּ וּנְחַלְתָּֽנוּ:
8 Anshei Emunah אַנְשֵׁי אֱמוּנָה אָבָֽדוּ
Men of faith have perished, who came with the strength of their deeds. Warriors who stood in the breach and turned away evil decrees. They were for us a wall and a shelter in the day of fury. They quenched anger with their whispers and restrained wrath with their prayers.
Aneshey emunah avadu, baiym bekhocha maaseyhem. Giboriym laamod baperetz, dochiym et hagezerot. Hayu lanu lechomah, ulemacheseh beyom zaam. Zoakhiym af belachasham, chemah atzeru beshaveam. Terem keraukha aniytam, yodeiym laator uleratzot. Keav richameta lemaanam, lo heshiyvota peneihem reykam. Merov avoneynu avadenum, neesefu menu bachataeynu. Sau hemah limenuchot, azevu otanu laanachot. Pasu goderey gader, tzumetu meshiyvey chemah. Kamiym baperetz ain, reuim leratzotekha afesu. Shotatenu beareba pinot, terufah lo matzanu. Shavenu eleykha bevoshet paneynu, leshacharakh el beet seliychatenu.
אַנְשֵׁי אֱמוּנָה אָבָֽדוּ, בָּאִים בְּכֹֽחַ מַעֲשֵׂיהֶֽם. גִּבּוֹרִים לַעֲמֹד בַּפֶּֽרֶץ, דּוֹחִים אֶת הַגְּזֵרוֹת. הָֽיוּ לָֽנוּ לְחוֹמָה, וּלְמַחְסֶה בְּיוֹם זַֽעַם. זוֹעֲכִים אַף בְּלַחֲשָׁם, חֵמָה עָֽצְרֽוּ בְּשַׁוְּעָם. טֶֽרֶם קְרָאֽוּךָ עֲנִיתָם, יֽוֹדְעִֽים לַעֲתֹר וּלְרַצּוֹת. כְּאָב רִחַֽמְתָּ לְמַעֲנָם, לֹא הֱשִׁיבֽוֹתָ פְנֵיהֶם רֵיקָם. מֵרֹב עֲוֹנֵֽינוּ אֲבַדְנוּם, נֶאֶסְפוּ מֶֽנּוּ בַּחֲטָאֵֽינוּ. סָֽעוּ הֵֽמָּה לִמְנוּחוֹת, עָֽזְבֽוּ אוֹתָֽנוּ לַאֲנָחוֹת. פַּֽסּוּ גּֽוֹדְרֵֽי גָדֵֽר, צֻמְּתוּ מְשִׁיבֵי חֵמָה. קָמִים בַּפֶּֽרֶץ אַֽיִן, רְאוּיִם לְרַצּֽוֹתְךָֽ אָפֵֽסוּ. שׁוֹטַֽטְנוּ בְּאַרְבַּֽע פִּנּוֹת, תְּרוּפָה לֹא מָצָֽאנוּ. שַֽׁבְנוּ אֵלֶֽיךָ בְּבֹֽשֶׁת פָּנֵֽינוּ, לְשַׁחֲרָךְ אֵֽל בְּעֵת סְלִיחָתֵֽנוּ.
The Thirteen Attributes of Mercy (י״ג מידות) are recited aloud by the congregation together.
God, King, who sits upon a throne of mercy, and acts with loving-kindness, forgiving the iniquities of His people, removing them one by one. Increasing forgiveness for sinners and pardon for transgressors. Dealing righteously with all flesh and spirit; not according to their evil does He requite them. God, You have taught us to recite the thirteen attributes; remember for us today the covenant of the thirteen attributes, as You made known of old to the humble one, and so it is written in Your Torah: And the Lord descended in the cloud and stood with him there, and proclaimed by the Name of the Lord. And there it is said:
El melekh yoshev al kise rachamiym, umitenaheg bachasiydut, mochel avonot amo, maaviyr rishon rishon. Marebeh mechiylah lachataiym, useliychah laposheiym. Oseh tzedakot im kal-basar verucha, lo kheraatam lahem gomel. El, horetanu lomar midot shelosh-esereh, zekhor lanu hayom beriyt shelosh-esereh, kemo shehodaeta leanav mikedem, vekhen katuv betoratakh: Vayered Adonai beanan vaiteyatzev imo sham vaikera veshem Adonai : Vesham neemar:
אֵֽל מֶֽלֶךְ יוֹשֵׁב עַל כִּסֵּא רַחֲמִים, וּמִתְנַהֵג בַּחֲסִידוּת, מוֹחֵל עֲוֹנוֹת עַמּוֹ, מַעֲבִיר רִאשׁוֹן רִאשׁוֹן. מַרְבֶּה מְחִילָה לַחַטָּאִֽים, וּסְלִיחָה לַפּֽוֹשְׁעִֽים. עוֹשֵׂה צְדָקוֹת עִם כָּל־בָּשָׂר וְרֽוּחַ, לֹא כְרָעָתָם לָהֶם גּוֹמֵל. אֵֽל, הוֹרֵתָֽנוּ לוֹמַר מִדּוֹת שְׁלֹשׁ־עֶשְׂרֵה, זְכֹר לָֽנוּ הַיּוֹם בְּרִית שְׁלֹשׁ־עֶשְׂרֵה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוֹדַֽעְתָּ לֶעָנָו מִקֶּֽדֶם, וְכֵן כָּתוּב בְּתוֹרָתָךְ: וַיֵּרֶד יְהוָֹה בֶּֽעָנָן וַיִּתְיַצֵּב עִמּוֹ שָׁם וַיִּקְרָא בְשֵׁם יְהוָֹֽה : וְשָׁם נֶאֱמַר:
And the Lord passed before him and proclaimed: The Lord, the Lord, God, compassionate and gracious, slow to anger, and abundant in kindness and truth. Preserving kindness for thousands of generations, forgiving iniquity, transgression, and sin, and He cleanses.
Vayaavor Adonai al-panayv vaikera Adonai Adonai el rachum vechanun erekh apaim verav-chesed veemet: Notzer chesed laalafiym nose avon vafesha vechataah venakeh.
וַיַּֽעֲבֹר יְהֹוָה | עַל־פָּנָיו וַיִּקְרָא יְהֹוָה | יְהֹוָה אֵל רַחוּם וְחַנּוּן אֶרֶךְ אַפַּיִם וְרַב־חֶסֶד וֶֽאֱמֶֽת: נֹצֵר חֶסֶד לָֽאֲלָפִים נֹשֵׂא עָוֹן וָפֶשַׁע וְחַטָּאָה וְנַקֵּֽה.
And forgive our iniquity and our sin, and take us as Your heritage.
Vesalacheta laavonenu ulechatatenu unechaletanu:
וְסָֽלַחְתָּ לַֽעֲוֹנֵנוּ וּלְחַטָּאתֵנוּ וּנְחַלְתָּֽנוּ:
9 Tamahnu Mera'ot תָּמַֽהְנוּ מֵרָעוֹת
We are astonished by evils; our strength has been sapped by troubles. We have bowed down exceedingly; we have been brought low to the dust. O Merciful One, such is our nature; stiff-necked and rebellious are we. We cried out that we have sinned, but our hearts are crooked. O Most High, Your mercies are eternal; forgiveness is with You. Let Your staff of kindness prevail.
Tamahenu meraot, tashash kochenu mitzarot. Shachenu ad limeod, shafalenu ad afar. Rachum kakh hiy midatenu, keshey oref umameriym anachenu. Tzaaknu befiynu chatanu, petaletol veikesh libenu. Eleyon rachameykha meolam, seliychah imekha hiy. Nicham al haraah, mateh kelapey chesed. Lo titealam beitot kael, kiy vetzarah gedolah anachenu. Ivada leeyney hakol, tuvekha vechasedekha imanu. Chatom peh-satan veal yasetiyn aleynu, zeom bo veidom. Veyaamod malach meliytz tov letzadekenu, hu yagiyd yasherenu. Derakheykha rachum vechanun giliyta leneeman bait, bevakesho az milefaneykha. Emunatekha hodaeta lo.
תָּמַֽהְנוּ מֵרָעוֹת, תָּשַׁשׁ כֹּחֵֽנוּ מִצָּרוֹת. שַֽׁחְנוּ עַד לִמְאֹד, שָׁפַֽלְנוּ עַד עָפָר. רַחוּם כָּךְ הִיא מִדָּתֵֽנוּ, קְשֵׁי עֹֽרֶף וּמַמְרִים אֲנָֽחְנוּ. צָעַֽקְנוּ בְּפִֽינוּ חָטָֽאנוּ, פְּתַלְתֹּל וְעִקֵּשׁ לִבֵּֽנוּ. עֶלְיוֹן רַחֲמֶֽיךָ מֵעוֹלָם, סְלִיחָה עִמְּךָ הִיא. נִחָם עַל הָרָעָה, מַטֵּה כְּלַפֵּי חֶֽסֶד. לֹא תִתְעַלַּם בְּעִתּוֹת כָּאֵֽל, כִּי בְצָרָה גְדוֹלָה אֲנָֽחְנוּ. יִוָּדַע לְעֵינֵי הַכֹּל, טֽוּבְךָֽ וְחַסְדְּךָ עִמָּֽנוּ. חֲתֹם פֶּה־שָׂטָן וְאַל יַשְׂטִין עָלֵֽינוּ, זְעוֹם בּוֹ וְיִדּוֹם. וְיַעֲמֹד (מלאך) מֵלִיץ טוֹב לְצַדְּקֵֽנוּ, הוּא יַגִּיד יָשְׁרֵֽנוּ. דְּרָכֶֽיךָ רַחוּם וְחַנּוּן גִּלִּֽיתָ לְנֶאֱמַן בַּֽיִת, בְּבַקְשׁוֹ אָז מִלְּפָנֶֽיךָ. אֱמוּנָֽתְךָֽ הוֹדַֽעְתָּ לּוֹ.
The Thirteen Attributes of Mercy (י״ג מידות) are recited aloud by the congregation together.
God, King, who sits upon a throne of mercy, and acts with loving-kindness, forgiving the iniquities of His people, removing them one by one. Increasing forgiveness for sinners and pardon for transgressors. Dealing righteously with all flesh and spirit; not according to their evil does He requite them. God, You have taught us to recite the thirteen attributes; remember for us today the covenant of the thirteen attributes, as You made known of old to the humble one, and so it is written in Your Torah: And the Lord descended in the cloud and stood with him there, and proclaimed by the Name of the Lord. And there it is said:
El melekh yoshev al kise rachamiym, umitenaheg bachasiydut, mochel avonot amo, maaviyr rishon rishon. Marebeh mechiylah lachataiym, useliychah laposheiym. Oseh tzedakot im kal-basar verucha, lo kheraatam lahem gomel. El, horetanu lomar midot shelosh-esereh, zekhor lanu hayom beriyt shelosh-esereh, kemo shehodaeta leanav mikedem, vekhen katuv betoratakh: Vayered Adonai beanan vaiteyatzev imo sham vaikera veshem Adonai : Vesham neemar:
אֵֽל מֶֽלֶךְ יוֹשֵׁב עַל כִּסֵּא רַחֲמִים, וּמִתְנַהֵג בַּחֲסִידוּת, מוֹחֵל עֲוֹנוֹת עַמּוֹ, מַעֲבִיר רִאשׁוֹן רִאשׁוֹן. מַרְבֶּה מְחִילָה לַחַטָּאִֽים, וּסְלִיחָה לַפּֽוֹשְׁעִֽים. עוֹשֵׂה צְדָקוֹת עִם כָּל־בָּשָׂר וְרֽוּחַ, לֹא כְרָעָתָם לָהֶם גּוֹמֵל. אֵֽל, הוֹרֵתָֽנוּ לוֹמַר מִדּוֹת שְׁלֹשׁ־עֶשְׂרֵה, זְכֹר לָֽנוּ הַיּוֹם בְּרִית שְׁלֹשׁ־עֶשְׂרֵה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוֹדַֽעְתָּ לֶעָנָו מִקֶּֽדֶם, וְכֵן כָּתוּב בְּתוֹרָתָךְ: וַיֵּרֶד יְהוָֹה בֶּֽעָנָן וַיִּתְיַצֵּב עִמּוֹ שָׁם וַיִּקְרָא בְשֵׁם יְהוָֹֽה : וְשָׁם נֶאֱמַר:
And the Lord passed before him and proclaimed: The Lord, the Lord, God, compassionate and gracious, slow to anger, and abundant in kindness and truth. Preserving kindness for thousands of generations, forgiving iniquity, transgression, and sin, and He cleanses.
Vayaavor Adonai al-panayv vaikera Adonai Adonai el rachum vechanun erekh apaim verav-chesed veemet: Notzer chesed laalafiym nose avon vafesha vechataah venakeh.
וַיַּֽעֲבֹר יְהֹוָה | עַל־פָּנָיו וַיִּקְרָא יְהֹוָה | יְהֹוָה אֵל רַחוּם וְחַנּוּן אֶרֶךְ אַפַּיִם וְרַב־חֶסֶד וֶֽאֱמֶֽת: נֹצֵר חֶסֶד לָֽאֲלָפִים נֹשֵׂא עָוֹן וָפֶשַׁע וְחַטָּאָה וְנַקֵּֽה.
And forgive our iniquity and our sin, and take us as Your heritage.
Vesalacheta laavonenu ulechatatenu unechaletanu:
וְסָֽלַחְתָּ לַֽעֲוֹנֵנוּ וּלְחַטָּאתֵנוּ וּנְחַלְתָּֽנוּ:
We are ashamed of our deeds and humiliated by our iniquities. We have no mouth to respond and no forehead to lift our face. What shall we say, O Lord our God? What shall we speak, and how shall we justify ourselves? Let us search our ways and examine them, and return to You, for Your right hand is outstretched to receive those who return.
Anachenu boshenu vemaaseynu, venikhelamenu baavonoteynu. Eyn lanu peh lehashiyv, velo metzach lehariym rosh.
אֲנַֽחְנוּ בֹּֽשְׁנוּ בְמַעֲשֵֽׂינוּ, וְנִכְלַֽמְנוּ בַּעֲוֹנוֹתֵֽינוּ. אֵין לָֽנוּ פֶה לְהָשִׁיב, וְלֹא מֵֽצַח לְהָרִים רֹאשׁ.
10 Al Ta'as Immanu Kalah אַל תַּעַשׂ עִמָּנוּ כָלָה
Our God and God of our fathers: Do not make an end of us; let Your hand grasp judgment. When rebuke comes before You, do not blot our name from Your book. When You approach to examine chastisement, let Your mercies precede Your wrath. When You behold the paucity of our deeds, bring near righteousness from Yourself. Guide us, when we cry out to You; command our salvation through an intercessor. And restore the captivity of the tents of the upright; see that its openings lie desolate. Remember what You declared: a testimony that shall not be forgotten from the mouth of his seed. Loose the sealed testimony; set Your secret among those who learn of You. The navel of the moon-shaped bowl—pray, let not the mixture be lacking. Yah, take note of Israel who have known You; cast down the nations that have not known You. For You will restore to the stronghold the captives, prisoners of hope.
Eloheynu velohey avoteynu, al taas imanu kalah, tochez yadekha bamishepat. Bevo tokhechah negedekha, shemenu misiferekha al temach. Gishetekha lachakor musar, rachameykha yekademu ragezekha. Dalut maasiym beshurekha, karev tzedek meeleykha. Horenu, bezaakenu lakh, tzav yeshuatenu bemafegiya. Vetashiyv shevut aholey tam, petachayv reeh kiy shamemu. Zekhor naameta, edut lo tishakhach mipiy zar-o. Chotam teudah tatiyr, sodekha siym belimudeykha. Tabur agan hasahar, na al yechesar hamazeg. Yah, da et iserael asher yedaukha, mager et hagoim asher lo yedaukha. Kiy tashiyv levitzaron, lekhudiym asiyrey hatikevah.
אֱלֹהֵינוּ וֵאלֹהֵי אֲבוֹתֵינוּ. אַל תַּעַשׂ עִמָּנוּ כָלָה, תֹּאחֵז יָֽדְךָֽ בַּמִּשְׁפָּט. בְּבֹא תוֹכֵחָה נֶגְדֶּֽךָ, שְׁמֵֽנוּ מִסִּפְרְךָ אַל תֶּֽמַח. גִּשְׁתְּךָ לַחֲקֹר מוּסָר, רַחֲמֶֽיךָ יְקַדְּמוּ רָגְזֶֽךָ. דַּלּוּת מַעֲשִׂים בְּשׁוּרֶֽךָ, קָרֵב צֶֽדֶק מֵאֵלֶֽיךָ. הוֹרֵֽנוּ, בְּזַעֲקֵֽנוּ לָךְ, צַו יְשׁוּעָתֵֽנוּ בְּמַפְגִּֽיעַ. וְתָשִׁיב שְׁבוּת אָֽהֳלֵֽי תָם, פְּתָחָיו רְאֵה כִּי שָׁמֵֽמוּ. זְכֹֽר נָאַֽמְתָּ, עֵדוּת לֹא תִשָּׁכַח מִפִּי זַרְעוֹ. חוֹתָם תְּעוּדָה תַּתִּיר, סֽוֹדְךָֽ שִׂים בְּלִמּוּדֶֽיךָ. טַבּוּר אַגַּן הַסַּֽהַר, נָא אַל יֶחְסַר הַמָּֽזֶג. יָהּ, דַּע אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל אֲשֶׁר יְדָעֽוּךָ, מַגֵּר אֶת הַגּוֹיִם אֲשֶׁר לֹא יְדָעֽוּךָ. כִּי תָשִׁיב לְבִצָּרוֹן, לְכוּדִים אֲסִירֵי הַתִּקְוָה.
11 Vidui אֶתְוַדֶּה עַל עֲבֵרוֹת
Master of the Universe, I confess my transgressions, light and severe, in the night at the end of the watches.
Ribono shel olam, etevadeh al averot kalot vachamurot, balayelah lesof ashemurot.
רִבּוֹנוֹ שֶׁל עוֹלָם, אֶתְוַדֶּה עַל עֲבֵרוֹת קַלּוֹת וַחֲמוּרוֹת, בַּלַּֽיְלָה לְסוֹף אַשְׁמוּרוֹת.
My evil inclination conspires wickedly. 'Watchman, what of the night? Watchman, what of the night?'
Itzeriy aliylot beresha lehiteolel, shomer mah-milayelah shomer mah-mileyl.
יִצְרִי עֲלִילוֹת בְּרֶֽשַׁע לְהִתְעוֹלֵל, שׁוֹמֵר מַה־מִּלַּֽיְלָה שׁוֹמֵר מַה־מִּלֵּיל.
My heart and my eyes lead me to sin. Woe to a heart that does not repent; woe to eyes that do not weep.
Libiy veeynay lachato yeotu, veyom valayelah lo ishebotu.
לִבִּי וְעֵינַי לַחֲטֹא יֵאֽוֹתוּ, וְיוֹם וָלַֽיְלָה לֹא יִשְׁבֹּֽתוּ.
And my other limbs conspire to do evil in Your sight, all the day and all the night.
Ushear evaray laasot hara beeyneykha itelachashu, kal-hayom vekhal-halayelah tamiyd lo yecheshu.
וּשְׁאָר אֵבָרַי לַעֲשׂוֹת הָרַע בְּעֵינֶֽיךָ יִתְלַחֲשֽׁוּ, כָּל־הַיּוֹם וְכָל־הַלַּֽיְלָה תָּמִיד לֹא יֶחֱשׁוּ.
And because I have transgressed the positive and negative commandments, who can count the sins I have committed?
Veyaan asher avaretiy al mitzeot aseh veal mitzeot lo taaseh, miy iten veasecheh vekhal-layelah mitatiy, bedimeatiy aresiy ameseh.
וְיַֽעַן אֲשֶׁר עָבַֽרְתִּי עַל מִצְוֹת עֲשֵׂה וְעַל מִצְוֹת לֹא תַעֲשֶׂה, מִי יִתֵּן וְאַשְׂחֶה בְכָל־לַֽיְלָה מִטָּֽתִי, בְּדִמְעָתִי עַרְשִׂי אַמְסֶה.
Perhaps He will hear the voice of my bitter weeping, on the day I cry out in the night.
Ulay ishema kol bikheiy nora aliylah, yom tzaaketiy balayelah.
אוּלַי יִשְׁמַע קוֹל בִּכְיִי נוֹרָא עֲלִילָה, יוֹם צָעַֽקְתִּי בַּלָּֽיְלָה.
I call out by day and raise my voice, over my sin and treachery, and at night there is no silence for me.
Ekera yomam veariym koliy, al pisheiy umaaliy, velayelah velo dumiyah liy.
אֶקְרָא יוֹמָם וְאָרִים קוֹלִי, עַל פִּשְׁעִי וּמַעֲלִי, וְלַֽיְלָה וְלֹא דוּמִיָּֽה לִי.
Some of my sins I recall before Him who sees the future; at night I arise to give thanks.
Et miketzat chataay aniy mazekiyr lifeney tzofeh atiydot, layelah akum lehodot.
אֶת מִקְצַת חֲטָאַי אֲנִי מַזְכִּיר לִפְנֵי צוֹפֶה עֲתִידוֹת, לַֽיְלָה אָקוּם לְהוֹדוֹת.
And I confess my transgressions and willful sins; this night belongs to the Lord.
Veodeh aley feshaay uzedonay, halayelah hazeh ladonai.
וְאוֹדֶֽה עֲלֵי פְשָׁעַי וּזְדוֹנַי, הַלַּֽיְלָה הַזֶּה לַיהֹוָה.
Please, O Lord our God and God of our fathers, may our prayer come before You. Do not hide from our supplication, for we are not so brazen as to say we are righteous and have not sinned. Indeed, we and our ancestors have sinned.
Ana Adonai eloheynu velohey avoteynu, tavo lefaneykha tefilatenu, veal titealam malekenu mitechinatenu, sheeyn anachenu azey faniym ukeshey oref, lomar lefaneykha Adonai eloheynu velohey avoteynu tzadiykiym anachenu velo-chatanu, aval chatanu, aviynu, pashaenu, anachenu vaavoteynu veaneshey veytenu.
אָֽנָּֽא יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ וֵאלֹהֵי אֲבוֹתֵֽינוּ, תָּבֹא לְפָנֶֽיךָ תְּפִלָּתֵֽנוּ, וְאַל תִּתְעַלַּם מַלְכֵּֽנוּ מִתְּחִנָּתֵֽנוּ, שֶׁאֵין אֲנַֽחְנוּ עַזֵּי פָנִים וּקְשֵׁי עֹֽרֶף, לוֹמַר לְפָנֶֽיךָ יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ וֵאלֹהֵי אֲבוֹתֵֽינוּ צַדִּיקִים אֲנַֽחְנוּ וְלֹא־חָטָֽאנוּ, אֲבָל חָטָֽאנוּ, עָוִֽינוּ, פָּשַֽׁעְנוּ, אֲנַֽחְנוּ וַאֲבוֹתֵֽינוּ וְאַנְשֵׁי בֵיתֵֽנוּ.
My sin I make known to You, and my iniquity I have not hidden. I said: I will confess my transgressions to the Lord, and You forgave the guilt of my sin.
Chatatiy odiyakha vaavoniy lo-khisiytiy, amaretiy odeh aley feshaay ladonai, veatah nasata avon chatatiy selah: Mekhaseh feshaayv lo yatzeliycha, umodeh veozev yerucham:
חַטָּאתִי אוֹדִיעֲךָ וַֽעֲוֹנִי לֹֽא־כִסִּיתִי, אָמַרְתִּי אוֹדֶה עֲלֵי פְשָׁעַֽי לַֽיהֹוָה, וְאַתָּה נָשָׂאתָ עֲוֹן חַטָּאתִי סֶֽלָה: מְכַסֶּה פְשָׁעָֽיו לֹא יַצְלִיחַ, וּמוֹדֶה וְעֹזֵב יְרֻחָֽם:
We have been guilty. We have eaten forbidden foods. We have betrayed. We have neglected Your Torah days and nights. We have robbed. We have stolen. We have been arrogant. We have spoken defamation and evil speech. We have spoken one thing with the mouth and another in the heart. We have caused perversion. We have entertained evil thoughts. And we have acted wickedly. And we have multiplied crime and wickedness without limit. And we have appointed ourselves toward a matter of transgression. We have been presumptuous. We have gone astray after our heart and our eyes. We have done violence. We have coveted. We have desecrated Sabbaths and Your holy festivals. We have patched on falsehood and deceit. We have counseled evil counsels beyond searching. We have lied. We have been angry. We have mocked. We have scoffed. We have rebelled. We have defied Your words. We have rebelled against the kingdom of heaven; we have rebelled against the kingdom of the house of David; we have rejected the Temple—and all three of them we seek. We have reviled. We have committed adultery. We have sworn in vain and falsely. We have vowed and not paid. We have been wayward. We have been rebellious and defiant. We have committed iniquity. We have transgressed positive commandments and negative commandments. We have transgressed sins of excision and death penalties of the court. We have transgressed desecration of the Name. We have willfully sinned. We have blemished the sign of the holy covenant. We have oppressed. We have distressed father and mother, parents and teachers. We have been stiff-necked. We have damaged the channels of abundance. We have acted wickedly. We have been evil toward heaven and toward creatures. We have corrupted. We have lied. We have wasted holy seed in vain; we have abominated. We have gone astray and have led others astray. And we have turned aside from Your commandments and from Your good ordinances, and it has not availed us; and You are righteous concerning all that has come upon us, for You have acted truthfully, and we have acted wickedly.
Ashamenu. Akhalenu maakhalot asurot. Bagadenu. Bitalenu toratekha yamiym veleylot. Gazalenu. Ganavenu. Gaiynu. Dibarenu dofiy velashon hara. Dibarenu echad bapeh veechad balev. Heeviynu. Hireharenu hirehuriym raiym. Vehireshaenu. Vehirebiynu pesha varesha liveliy chok. Viyadenu atzemenu lidevar averah. Zadenu. Zaniynu achar libenu veeyneynu. Chamasenu. Chamadenu. Chilalenu shabatot umoadey kadeshekha. Tafalenu sheker umiremah. Yaatzenu etzot raot ad eyn cheker. Kizavenu. Kaasenu. Latzenu. Lotzatzenu. Maradenu. Mariynu devareykha. Maradenu bemalekhut shamaim, maradenu bemalekhut beyt david, maasenu beveyt hamikedash, ushelashetam anachenu mevakeshiym. Niatzenu. Niafenu. Nishebaenu lashave velasheker. Nadarenu velo shilamenu. Sararenu. Soreriym umoriym haiynu. Aviynu. Avarenu al mitzeot aseh veal mitzeot lo taaseh. Avarenu al keriytot umiytot beyt diyn. Avarenu al chilul hashem. Pashaenu. Pagamenu beot beriyt kodesh. Tzararenu. Tziarenu av vaem, horiym umoriym. Kishiynu oref. Kilekalenu tzinorot hashefa. Rashaenu. Raiym lashamayim velaberiyot haiynu. Shichatenu. Shikarenu. Shichatenu zera kodesh levatalah tiavenu. Taiynu vetietaenu. Vesarenu mimitzeoteykha umimishepateykha hatoviym velo-shavah lanu, veatah tzadiyk al kal-haba aleynu, kiy-emet asiyta vaanachenu hireshaenu:
אָשַֽׁמְנוּ. אָכַֽלְנוּ מַאֲכָלוֹת אֲסוּרוֹת. בָּגַֽדְנוּ. בִּטַּֽלְנוּ תוֹרָתֶֽךָ יָמִים וְלֵילוֹת. גָּזַֽלְנוּ. גָּנַֽבְנוּ. גָּאִֽינוּ. דִּבַּֽרְנוּ דֹֽפִי וְלָשׁוֹן הָרָע. דִּבַּֽרְנוּ אֶחָד בַּפֶּה וְאֶחָד בַּלֵּב. הֶעֱוִֽינוּ. הִרְהַֽרְנוּ הִרְהוּרִים רָעִים. וְהִרְשַֽׁעְנוּ. וְהִרְבִּֽינוּ פֶּֽשַׁע וָרֶֽשַׁע לִבְלִי חֹק. וִיעַֽדְנוּ עַצְמֵֽנוּ לִדְבַר עֲבֵרָה. זַֽדְנוּ. זָנִֽינוּ אַחַר לִבֵּֽנוּ וְעֵינֵֽינוּ. חָמַֽסְנוּ. חָמַֽדְנוּ. חִלַּֽלְנוּ שַׁבָּתוֹת וּמוֹעֲדֵי קָדְשֶֽׁךָ. טָפַֽלְנוּ שֶֽׁקֶר וּמִרְמָה. יָעַֽצְנוּ עֵצוֹת רָעוֹת עַד אֵין חֵֽקֶר. כִּזַּֽבְנוּ. כָּעַֽסְנוּ. לַֽצְנוּ. לוֹצַֽצְנוּ. מָרַֽדְנוּ. מָרִֽינוּ דְבָרֶֽיךָ. מָרַֽדְנוּ בְּמַלְכוּת שָׁמַֽיִם, מָרַֽדְנוּ בְּמַלְכוּת בֵּית דָּוִד, מָאַֽסְנוּ בְּבֵית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ, וּשְׁלָשְׁתָּם אֲנַֽחְנוּ מְבַקְשִׁים. נִאַֽצְנוּ. נִאַֽפְנוּ. נִשְׁבַּֽעְנוּ לַשָּֽׁוְא וְלַשֶּֽׁקֶר. נָדַֽרְנוּ וְלֹא שִׁלַּֽמְנוּ. סָרַֽרְנוּ. סֽוֹרְרִֽים וּמוֹרִים הָיִֽינוּ. עָוִֽינוּ. עָבַֽרְנוּ עַל מִצְוֹת עֲשֵׂה וְעַל מִצְוֹת לֹא תַעֲשֶׂה. עָבַֽרְנוּ עַל כְּרִיתוֹת וּמִיתוֹת בֵּית דִּין. עָבַֽרְנוּ עַל חִלּוּל הַשֵּׁם. פָּשַֽׁעְנוּ. פָּגַֽמְנוּ בְּאוֹת בְּרִית קֹֽדֶשׁ. צָרַֽרְנוּ. צִעַֽרְנוּ אָב וָאֵם, הוֹרִים וּמוֹרִים. קִשִּֽׁינוּ עֹֽרֶף. קִלְקַֽלְנוּ צִנּוֹרוֹת הַשֶּֽׁפַע. רָשַֽׁעְנוּ. רָעִים לַשָּׁמַֽיִם וְלַבְּרִיּוֹת הָיִֽינוּ. שִׁחַֽתְנוּ. שִׁקַּֽרְנוּ. שִׁחַֽתְנוּ זֶֽרַע קֹֽדֶשׁ לְבַטָּלָה תִּעַֽבְנוּ. תָּעִינוּ וְתִעְתַּֽעְנוּ. וְסַֽרְנוּ מִמִּצְוֹתֶֽיךָ וּמִמִּשְׁפָּטֶֽיךָ הַטּוֹבִים וְלֹא־שָֽׁוָה לָֽנוּ, וְאַתָּה צַדִּיק עַל כָּל־הַבָּא עָלֵינוּ, כִּֽי־אֱמֶת עָשִׂיתָ וַֽאֲנַחְנוּ הִרְשָֽׁעְנוּ:
We are more guilty than any people; we are more ashamed than any nation. Joy has gone forth from us; our heart is faint in our sins. Our desire has been ruined, and our glory has been stripped away. The abode of our Sanctuary is desolate through our iniquities. Our citadel has become a waste; the beauty of our land is for strangers, our strength for foreigners. Before our eyes they have oppressed our toil, scraped and peeled away from us. They have set their yoke upon us; we have borne it upon our shoulder. Servants have ruled over us; there is none to redeem from their hand. Many troubles have surrounded us; we have called to You, O Lord our God. You have withdrawn from us through our iniquities; we have turned away from following You; we have strayed like sheep and are lost. And still we have not returned from our wandering.
Ashamenu mikal-am, boshenu mikal-goy. Galah mimenu masos, daveh libenu bachataeynu. Hachebal eveyenu, venifera peerenu. Zevul mikedashenu, charev baavoneynu. Tiyratenu hayetah leshamah, yefiy adematenu lezariym, kochenu lenakheriym. Leeyneynu asheku amalenu, memurakh umorat mimenu. Natenu ulam aleynu, savalenu al shikhemenu. Avadiym mashelu vanu, porek eyn miyadam. Tzarot rabot sevavunu, keranukha Adonai eloheynu. Rachaketa mimenu baavoneynu, shavenu meachareykha, taiynu khatzon veavadenu. Vaadain lo shavenu miteiyatenu.
אָשַֽׁמְנוּ מִכָּל־עָם, בֹּֽשְׁנוּ מִכָּל־גּוֹי. גָּלָה מִמֶּֽנּוּ מָשׂוֹשׂ, דָּוֶֽה לִבֵּֽנוּ בַּחֲטָאֵֽינוּ. הָחְבַּל אֶוְיֵֽנוּ, וְנִפְרָע פְּאֵרֵֽנוּ. זְבוּל מִקְדָּשֵֽׁנוּ, חָרֵב בַּעֲוֹנֵֽינוּ. טִירָתֵֽנוּ הָֽיְתָֽה לְשַׁמָּה, יְפִי אַדְמָתֵֽנוּ לְזָרִים, כֹּחֵֽנוּ לְנָכְרִים. לְעֵינֵֽינוּ עָֽשְׁקֽוּ עֲמָלֵֽנוּ, מְמֻרָּךְ וּמוֹרָט מִמֶּֽנּוּ. נָֽתְנֽוּ עֻלָּם עָלֵֽינוּ, סָבַֽלְנוּ עַל שִׁכְמֵֽנוּ. עֲבָדִים מָֽשְׁלוּ בָֽנוּ, פּוֹרֵק אֵין מִיָּדָם. צָרוֹת רַבּוֹת סְבָבֽוּנוּ, קְרָאנֽוּךָ יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ. רָחַֽקְתָּ מִמֶּֽנּוּ בַּעֲוֹנֵֽינוּ, שַֽׁבְנוּ מֵאַחֲרֶֽיךָ, תָּעִֽינוּ כַצֹּאן וְאָבַֽדְנוּ. וַעֲדַֽיִן לֹא שַֽׁבְנוּ מִתְּעִיָּתֵֽנוּ.
12 Shema Yisrael שְׁמַע יִשְׂרָאֵל
To the Lord our God belong compassion and forgiveness, for we have sinned against Him.
Ladonay eloheynu harachamiym vehaseliychot, kiy chatanu lo.
לַאדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ הָרַחֲמִים וְהַסְּלִיחוֹת, כִּי חָטָֽאנוּ לוֹ.
To the Lord our God belong compassion and forgiveness, for we have rebelled against Him.
Ladonay eloheynu harachamiym vehaselichot, kiy maradenu bo:
לַֽאדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ הָרַֽחֲמִים וְהַסְּלִחוֹת, כִּי מָרַדְנוּ בּֽוֹ:
Please do not place upon us a sin for which we have been foolish and for which we have sinned.
Al-na tashet aleynu chatat, asher noalenu vaasher chatanu:
אַל־נָא תָשֵׁת עָלֵינוּ חַטָּאת, אֲשֶׁר נוֹאַלְנוּ וַֽאֲשֶׁר חָטָֽאנוּ:
We have sinned, our Rock; forgive us, our Creator.
Chatanu tzurenu, selach lanu yotzerenu.
חָטָֽאנוּ צוּרֵֽנוּ, סְלַח לָֽנוּ יֽוֹצְרֵֽנוּ.
Hear, O Israel: the Lord our God, the Lord is One!
Shema iserael Adonai eloheynu Adonai echad:
שְׁמַע יִשְׂרָאֵל יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ יְהֹוָה | אֶחָֽד:
The Lord, He is God. The Lord, He is God.
Adonai hu haelohiym. Adonai hu haelohiym:
יְהֹוָה הוּא הָֽאֱלֹהִים. יְהֹוָה הוּא הָֽאֱלֹהִֽים:
The Lord, He is God. The Lord, He is God.
Adonai hu haelohiym. Adonai hu haelohiym:
יְהֹוָה הוּא הָֽאֱלֹהִים. יְהֹוָה הוּא הָֽאֱלֹהִֽים:
In the following verses, the leader declares each heavenly proclamation and the congregation responds with "יְהֹוָה הוּא הָֽאֱלֹהִים" — "The Lord, He is God."
The angels on high declare: the Lord is our Master. The chosen treasured ones respond and say: the Lord is our God.
Ereeley maelah omeriym Adonai adonenu. Bechiyrey segulah oniym veomeriym Adonai hu haelohiym. Adonai hu haelohiym:
אֶרְאֵלֵי מַֽעְלָה אֽוֹמְרִֽים יְהֹוָה אֲדוֹנֵֽנוּ. בְּחִירֵי סְגֻלָּה עוֹנִים וְאֽוֹמְרִֽים יְהֹוָה הוּא הָֽאֱלֹהִים. יְהֹוָה הוּא הָֽאֱלֹהִֽים:
The celestial spheres on high declare: the Lord is our Master. The distinguished treasured ones respond and say: the Lord is our God.
Galegaley maelah omeriym Adonai adonenu, deguley segulah oniym veomeriym Adonai hu haelohiym. Adonai hu haelohiym:
גַּלְגַּלֵּי מַֽעְלָה אֽוֹמְרִֽים יְהֹוָה אֲדוֹנֵֽנוּ, דְּגוּלֵי סְגֻלָּה עוֹנִים וְאֽוֹמְרִֽים יְהֹוָה הוּא הָֽאֱלֹהִים. יְהֹוָה הוּא הָֽאֱלֹהִֽים:
The multitudes on high declare: the Lord is our Master. Israel in their distress and exile respond and say: the Lord is our God.
Hamoney maelah omeriym Adonai adonenu, iserael betzaratam uvegalutam oniym veomeriym Adonai hu haelohiym. Adonai hu haelohiym:
הֲמוֹנֵי מַֽעְלָה אֽוֹמְרִֽים יְהֹוָה אֲדוֹנֵֽנוּ, יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּצָרָתָם וּבְגָלוּתָם עוֹנִים וְאֽוֹמְרִֽים יְהֹוָה הוּא הָֽאֱלֹהִים. יְהֹוָה הוּא הָֽאֱלֹהִֽים:
The Lord is King, the Lord was King, the Lord shall reign forever and ever.
Adonai melekh, Adonai malakh, Adonai imelokh leolam vaed:
יְהֹוָה מֶֽלֶךְ, יְהֹוָה מָלָֽךְ, יְהֹוָה | יִמְלֹךְ לְעֹלָם וָעֶֽד:
The Lord is King, the Lord was King, the Lord shall reign forever and ever.
Adonai melekh, Adonai malakh, Adonai imelokh leolam vaed:
יְהֹוָה מֶֽלֶךְ, יְהֹוָה מָלָֽךְ, יְהֹוָה | יִמְלֹךְ לְעֹלָם וָעֶֽד:
Before the heavens and the earth were stretched out, the Lord was King. And before the luminaries shone, the Lord was King. And when all shall end, the Lord shall reign. The Lord was King, the Lord is King, the Lord shall reign forever and ever.
Beterem shechakiym vaarakiym nimetachu, Adonai melekh. Vead lo meorot zarachu, Adonai malakh. Vehaaretz kabeged tiveleh, veshamaim keashan nimelachu. Adonai imelokh leolam vaed: Adonai melekh, Adonai malakh, Adonai imelokh leolam vaed:
בְּטֶֽרֶם שְׁחָקִים וַאֲרָקִים נִמְתָּֽחוּ, יְהֹוָה מֶֽלֶךְ. וְעַד לֹא מְאוֹרוֹת זָרָֽחוּ, יְהֹוָה מָלָֽךְ. וְהָאָֽרֶץ כַּבֶּֽגֶד תִּבְלֶה, וְשָׁמַֽיִם כְּעָשָׁן נִמְלָֽחוּ. יְהֹוָה | יִמְלֹךְ לְעֹלָם וָעֶֽד: יְהֹוָה מֶֽלֶךְ, יְהֹוָה מָלָֽךְ, יְהֹוָה | יִמְלֹךְ לְעֹלָם וָעֶֽד:
And before He made the earth and its open places, the Lord was King. And when He established creatures upon the earth, the Lord was King. The Lord was King, the Lord is King, the Lord shall reign forever and ever.
Vead lo-asah eretz vechutzot, Adonai melekh. Uvahakhiyno yetzuriym aley aratzot, Adonai malakh. Veet yekabetz nefutziym meareba tefutzot, Adonai imelokh leolam vaed: Adonai melekh, Adonai malakh, Adonai imelokh leolam vaed:
וְעַד לֹא־עָשָׂה אֶֽרֶץ וְחוּצוֹת, יְהֹוָה מֶֽלֶךְ. וּבַהֲכִינוֹ יְצוּרִים עֲלֵי אֲרָצוֹת, יְהֹוָה מָלָֽךְ. וְעֵת יְקַבֵּץ נְפוּצִים מֵאַרְבַּֽע תְּפוּצוֹת, יְהֹוָה | יִמְלֹךְ לְעֹלָם וָעֶֽד: יְהֹוָה מֶֽלֶךְ, יְהֹוָה מָלָֽךְ, יְהֹוָה | יִמְלֹךְ לְעֹלָם וָעֶֽד:
He is unique in 'I Am that I Am.' He was, He is, and He shall be. He puts to death and gives life; He brings low and also lifts up. The Lord was King, the Lord is King, the Lord shall reign forever and ever.
Meyuchad beeheyeh asher eheyeh, hu hayah vehu hoveh vehu iheyeh, hu memiyt umechayeh, lefanayv lo notzar el, veacharayv lo iheyeh.
מְיוּחָד בְּאֶהְיֶה אֲשֶׁר אֶהְיֶה, הוּא הָיָה וְהוּא הֹוֶה וְהוּא יִהְיֶה, הוּא מֵמִית וּמְחַיֶּה, לְפָנָיו לֹא נוֹצַר אֵל, וְאַחֲרָיו לֹא יִהְיֶה.
יפסיק מעט לפני שיתחיל "אחד".
Pause briefly before beginning "Echad" (One).
One is our God, great is our Master, holy and awesome is His Name. For great above the heavens is Your kindness, and until the skies is Your truth.
Echad eloheynu, gadol adonenu, kadosh venora shemo. Kiy-gadol meal-shamaim chasedekha, vead-shechakiym amitekha: Kiy-gadol atah veoseh nifelaot, atah elohiym levadekha: Shefokh chamatekha el-hagoim asher lo-yedaukha, veal mamelakhot asher beshimekha lo karau:
אֶחָד אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ, גָּדוֹל אֲדוֹנֵֽנוּ, קָדוֹשׁ וְנוֹרָא שְׁמוֹ. כִּֽי־גָדוֹל מֵֽעַל־שָׁמַיִם חַסְדֶּךָ, וְֽעַד־שְׁחָקִים אֲמִתֶּֽךָ: כִּֽי־גָדוֹל אַתָּֽה וְעֹשֵׂה נִפְלָאוֹת, אַתָּה אֱלֹהִים לְבַדֶּֽךָ: שְׁפֹךְ חֲמָֽתְךָ אֶֽל־הַגּוֹיִם אֲשֶׁר לֹֽא־יְדָעוּךָ, וְעַל מַמְלָכוֹת אֲשֶׁר בְּשִׁמְךָ לֹא קָרָֽאוּ:
Act for the holiness of Your Name, and not for us. Not for us, O Lord, not for us, but for Your Name give glory, for Your kindness and for Your truth. Why should the nations say, Where now is their God? But our God is in the heavens. One is our God in the heavens. Our testimony twice every day. He is living and enduring; He is full of mercies; He is full of merits. Whatever He has desired He has done in heaven and on earth; there is none who may say to Him, What are You doing? and none who may say to Him, What are You accomplishing? for all is the work of His hands.
Likedushat shimekha aseh, velo lanu. Lo lanu Adonai lo lanu, kiy-leshimekha ten kavod, al-chasedekha al-amitekha. Lamah yomeru hagoim, ayeh-na eloheyhem. Veloheynu bashamayim. Echad eloheynu bashamayim. Edutenu bekhal-yom paamaim. Chay vekayam hu, male rachamiym hu, male zakiyut hu. Kal-asher chafetz asah bashamayim uvaaretz, eyn miy yomar lo mah-taaseh, veeyn miy yomar lo mah-tifeal, kiy hakol maaseh yadayv.
לִקְדֻשַּׁת שִׁמְךָ עֲשֵׂה, וְלֹא לָֽנוּ. לֹא לָֽנוּ יְהֹוָה לֹא לָֽנוּ, כִּי־לְשִׁמְךָ תֵּן כָּבוֹד, עַל־חַסְדְּךָ עַל־אֲמִתֶּֽךָ. לָֽמָּה יֹאמְרוּ הַגּוֹיִם, אַיֵּה־נָא אֱלֹהֵיהֶם. וֵאלֹהֵֽינוּ בַּשָּׁמַֽיִם. אֶחָד אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ בַּשָּׁמַֽיִם. עֵדוּתֵֽנוּ בְּכָל־יוֹם פַּעֲמַֽיִם. חַי וְקַיָּם הוּא, מָלֵא רַחֲמִים הוּא, מָלֵא זַכִּיּוּת הוּא. כָּל־אֲשֶׁר חָפֵץ עָשָׂה בַּשָּׁמַֽיִם וּבָאָֽרֶץ, אֵין מִי יֹֽאמַר לוֹ מַה־תַּעֲשֶׂה, וְאֵין מִי יֹֽאמַר לוֹ מַה־תִּפְעַל, כִּי הַכֹּל מַעֲשֵׂה יָדָיו.
13 Eloheinu Shebashamayim אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ שֶׁבַּשָּׁמַֽיִם
Our God in heaven, hear our voice and accept our prayer with favor. Amen
Eloheynu shebashamayim, shema kolenu vekabel tefilatenu beratzon. amen
אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ שֶׁבַּשָּׁמַֽיִם, שְׁמַע קוֹלֵֽנוּ וְקַבֵּל תְּפִלָּתֵֽנוּ בְּרָצוֹן. אָמֵן
Our God in heaven, do not destroy us in the length of our exile. Amen
Eloheynu shebashamayim, al teabedenu beorekh galutenu. amen
אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ שֶׁבַּשָּׁמַֽיִם, אַל תְּאַבְּדֵֽנוּ בְּאֹֽרֶךְ גָּלוּתֵֽנוּ. אָמֵן
Destroy and annihilate all who rise against us for evil. Amen
Abed vehashemed kal-hakamiym aleynu leraah. amen
אַבֵּד וְהַשְׁמֵד כָּל־הַקָּמִים עָלֵֽינוּ לְרָעָה. אָמֵן
Our God in heaven, remember Your covenant and do not forget us. Amen
Eloheynu shebashamayim, beriytekha zekhor veal tishekachenu. amen
אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ שֶׁבַּשָּׁמַֽיִם, בְּרִֽיתְךָֽ זְכֹר וְאַל תִּשְׁכָּחֵֽנוּ. אָמֵן
Bless our bread and our water. Amen
Barekh et lachemenu veet meymeynu. amen
בָּרֵךְ אֶת לַחְמֵֽנוּ וְאֶת מֵימֵֽינוּ. אָמֵן
Bring us tidings of good. Amen
Baserenu besorot tovot. amen
בַּשְּׂרֵֽנוּ בְּשׂוֹרוֹת טוֹבוֹת. אָמֵן
Annul from upon us and from upon Your people Israel all evil decrees. Amen
Batel mealeynu umeal amekha iserael bekhal-makom shehem, kal-gezerot kashot veraot. amen
בַּטֵּל מֵעָלֵֽינוּ וּמֵעַל עַמְּךָ יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּכָל־מָקוֹם שֶׁהֵם, כָּל־גְּזֵרוֹת קָשׁוֹת וְרָעוֹת. אָמֵן
Our God in heaven, decree upon us good decrees. Amen
Eloheynu shebashamayim, gezor aleynu gezerot tovot. amen
אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ שֶׁבַּשָּׁמַֽיִם, גְּזֹר עָלֵֽינוּ גְּזֵרוֹת טוֹבוֹת. אָמֵן
Reveal the glory of Your kingdom upon us speedily. Amen
Galeh kevod malekhutekha aleynu meherah. amen
גַּלֵּה כְּבוֹד מַלְכֽוּתְךָֽ עָלֵֽינוּ מְהֵרָה. אָמֵן
Our God in heaven, we have sought You; be found by us. Amen
Eloheynu shebashamayim, derashenukha himatze lanu. amen
אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ שֶׁבַּשָּׁמַֽיִם, דְּרַשְׁנֽוּךָ הִמָּֽצֵא לָֽנוּ. אָמֵן
Avenge our blood from our oppressors. Amen
Derosh dameynu miyad kameynu. amen
דְּרֹשׁ דָּמֵֽינוּ מִיַּד קָמֵֽינוּ. אָמֵן
Our God in heaven, answer us today and every day in our prayer. Amen
Eloheynu shebashamayim, heater-lanu hayom uvekhal-yom vayom bitefilatenu. amen
אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ שֶׁבַּשָּׁמַֽיִם, הֵעָֽתֶר־לָֽנוּ הַיּוֹם וּבְכָל־יוֹם וָיוֹם בִּתְפִלָּתֵֽנוּ. אָמֵן
Return us in complete repentance before You. Amen
Hachaziyrenu biteshuvah shelemah lefaneykha. amen
הַחֲזִירֵֽנוּ בִּתְשׁוּבָה שְׁלֵמָה לְפָנֶֽיךָ. אָמֵן
Our God in heaven, do not put us to shame for our hope. Amen
Eloheynu shebashamayim, veal teviyshenu misivrenu. amen
אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ שֶׁבַּשָּׁמַֽיִם, וְאַל תְּבִישֵֽׁנוּ מִשִּׂבְרֵֽנוּ. אָמֵן
We shall call and You shall answer us. Amen
Venikera veatah taanenu. amen
וְנִקְרָא וְאַתָּה תַעֲנֵֽנוּ. אָמֵן
Our God in heaven, remember us with a favorable memory before You. Amen
Eloheynu shebashamayim, zakherenu bezikheron tov milefaneykha. amen
אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ שֶׁבַּשָּׁמַֽיִם, זָכְרֵֽנוּ בְּזִכְרוֹן טוֹב מִלְּפָנֶֽיךָ. אָמֵן
Vindicate us in our judgment. Amen
Zakenu bediynenu. amen
זַכֵּֽנוּ בְּדִינֵֽנוּ. אָמֵן
Vindicate us in Your justice. Amen
Zakenu bemishepateykha. amen
זַכֵּֽנוּ בְּמִשְׁפָּטֶֽיךָ. אָמֵן
Our God in heaven, have compassion upon us, our children, and our infants. Amen
Eloheynu shebashamayim, chamol aleynu veal tapeynu veal olaleynu. amen
אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ שֶׁבַּשָּׁמַֽיִם, חֲמֹל עָלֵֽינוּ וְעַל טַפֵּֽינוּ וְעַל עוֹלָלֵֽינוּ. אָמֵן
Have pity and mercy upon us; save us for the sake of Your Name. Amen
Chus verachem aleynu. amen
חוּס וְרַחֵם עָלֵֽינוּ. אָמֵן
Our God in heaven, purify us from the impurity of our sins. Amen
Eloheynu shebashamayim, taharenu mitumeot avoneynu. amen
אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ שֶׁבַּשָּׁמַֽיִם, טַהֲרֵֽנוּ מִטֻּמְאוֹת עֲוֹנֵֽינוּ. אָמֵן
Purify us from our impurities. Amen
Taharenu mitumeoteynu. amen
טַהֲרֵֽנוּ מִטֻּמְאוֹתֵֽינוּ. אָמֵן
Our God in heaven, let Your mercies be aroused upon us. Amen
Eloheynu shebashamayim, yehemu-na rachameykha aleynu. amen
אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ שֶׁבַּשָּׁמַֽיִם, יֶֽהֱמוּ־נָֽא רַחֲמֶֽיךָ עָלֵֽינוּ. אָמֵן
בעשרת ימי תשובה מוסיפים:
During the Ten Days of Repentance, the following is added:
Our God in heaven, inscribe us in the Book of Good Life. Amen
Eloheynu shebashamayim, katevenu besefer chaiym toviym. amen
אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ שֶׁבַּשָּׁמַֽיִם, כָּתְבֵֽנוּ בְּסֵֽפֶר חַיִּים טוֹבִים. אָמֵן
Inscribe us in the Book of the Righteous and Pious. Amen
Katevenu besefer tzadiykiym vachasiydiym. amen
כָּתְבֵֽנוּ בְּסֵֽפֶר צַדִּיקִים וַחֲסִידִים. אָמֵן
Inscribe us in the Book of the Upright and Pure. Amen
Katevenu besefer yeshariym utemiymiym. amen
כָּתְבֵֽנוּ בְּסֵֽפֶר יְשָׁרִים וּתְמִימִים. אָמֵן
Inscribe us in the Book of Merits. Amen
Katevenu besefer zakhiyot. amen
כָּתְבֵֽנוּ בְּסֵֽפֶר זָכִיּוֹת. אָמֵן
Inscribe us in the Book of Sustenance and Good Livelihood. Amen
Katevenu besefer mezonot ufarenasah tovah. amen
כָּתְבֵֽנוּ בְּסֵֽפֶר מְזוֹנוֹת וּפַרְנָסָה טוֹבָה. אָמֵן
Inscribe us in the Book of Forgiveness, Pardon, and Atonement. Amen
Katevenu besefer mechiylah useliychah vekhaparah. amen
כָּתְבֵֽנוּ בְּסֵֽפֶר מְחִילָה וּסְלִיחָה וְכַפָּרָה. אָמֵן
Inscribe us in the Book of Redemption and Salvation. Amen
Katevenu besefer geulah viyshuah. amen
כָּתְבֵֽנוּ בְּסֵֽפֶר גְּאֻלָּה וִישׁוּעָה. אָמֵן
עד כאן מוסיפים בעשרת ימי תשובה.
End of the addition for the Ten Days of Repentance.
Our God in heaven, subdue those who oppress us. Amen
Eloheynu shebashamayim, kevosh et-koveshenu. amen
אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ שֶׁבַּשָּׁמַֽיִם, כְּבֹשׁ אֶת־כּֽוֹבְשֵֽׁנוּ. אָמֵן

Nekom nikemat dam avadeykha hashafukh. amen
נְקֹם נִקְמַת דַּם עֲבָדֶֽיךָ הַשָּׁפוּךְ. אָמֵן

Semokh et sukat david hanofelet. amen
סְמֹךְ אֶת סֻכַּת דָּוִד הַנּוֹפֶֽלֶת. אָמֵן

Anenu beyom kareenu lakh. amen

Tzadekenu bemishepateykha. . amen

Eloheynu shebashamayim, karev lanu ketz hageulah. amen

Karevenu laavodatekha veireatekha. amen

Kera roa gezar diynenu. amen

Kabel berachamiym uveratzon et tefilatenu. amen
קַבֵּל בְּרַחֲמִים וּבְרָצוֹן אֶת תְּפִלָּתֵֽנוּ. אָמֵן

Eloheynu shebashamayim, riyvah riyvenu ugealenu. amen
אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ שֶׁבַּשָּׁמַֽיִם, רִיבָֽה רִיבֵֽנוּ וּגְאָלֵֽנוּ. אָמֵן
See the affliction of Your people Israel. Amen
Reeh bavoniy amekha iserael. amen

Refa kal-choley amekha iserael. amen

Reeh bedochak hashaah vehaparenasah. amen
רְאֵה בְּדֹֽחַק הַשָּׁעָה וְהַפַּרְנָסָה. אָמֵן

Eloheynu shebashamayim, sheeh et shaveatenu. amen
אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ שֶׁבַּשָּׁמַֽיִם, שְׁעֵה אֶת שַׁוְעָתֵֽנוּ. אָמֵן
Grant peace among us. Amen
Shiyt shalom beyneynu. amen

Shiyt shalevah bearemenoteynu. amen

Eloheynu shebashamayim, ten shalom baaretz. amen
