Grant dew and rain for a blessing in their season upon the land. Amen
Ten tal umatar liverakhah beitam baaretz. amen
תֵּן טַל וּמָטָר לִבְרָכָה בְּעִתָּם בָּאָֽרֶץ. אָמֵן
Grant seed to the sower and bread to the eater. Amen
Ten zera lazorea velechem laokhel. amen
תֵּן זֶֽרַע לַזּוֹרֵֽעַ וְלֶֽחֶם לָאוֹכֵֽל. אָמֵן
Grant bread for the mouths of children, to satisfy. Amen
Ten lechem lefiy hataf lasova. amen
תֵּן לֶֽחֶם לְפִי הַטָּף לָשֹֽׂבַע. אָמֵן
May our prayer be established as incense before You. Amen
Tikon tefilatenu ketoret lefaneykha. amen
תִּכּוֹן תְּפִלָּתֵֽנוּ קְטֹֽרֶת לְפָנֶֽיךָ. אָמֵן
Our God in heaven, perform a sign for good with us, a sign for salvation Amen and mercy. Amen May our foes see and be ashamed, Amen may our enemies behold and be humiliated, Amen for You, O Lord, have helped us and comforted us.
Eloheynu shebashamayim, aseh imanu ot letovah, aseh imanu ot liyshuah, amen aseh imanu ot lerachamiym. amen Ireu soneeynu veyevoshu, amen yechezu oyeveynu veikalemu, amen kiy atah Adonai azaretanu venichametanu.
אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ שֶׁבַּשָּׁמַֽיִם, עֲשֵׂה עִמָּֽנוּ אוֹת לְטוֹבָה, עֲשֵׂה עִמָּֽנוּ אוֹת לִישׁוּעָה, אָמֵן עֲשֵׂה עִמָּֽנוּ אוֹת לְרַחֲמִים. אָמֵן יִרְאוּ שֽׂוֹנְאֵֽינוּ וְיֵבֽוֹשׁוּ, אָמֵן יֶחֱזוּ אֽוֹיְבֵֽינוּ וְיִכָּֽלְמֽוּ, אָמֵן כִּי אַתָּה יְהֹוָה עֲזַרְתָּֽנוּ וְנִחַמְתָּֽנוּ.
In Your anger, remember mercy. In Your anger, remember love. In Your anger, remember the binding of Isaac.
Berogez rachem tizekor, berogez ahavah tizekor, berogez akedah tizekor, berogez temiymut tizekor. Habet laberiyt veal tafer beriytekha itanu, anenu baet uvaonah hazot.
בְּרֹֽגֶז רַחֵם תִּזְכֹּר, בְּרֹֽגֶז אַהֲבָה תִּזְכֹּר, בְּרֹֽגֶז עֲקֵדָה תִּזְכֹּר, בְּרֹֽגֶז תְּמִימוּת תִּזְכֹּר. הַבֵּט לַבְּרִית וְאַל תָּפֵר בְּרִֽיתְךָֽ אִתָּֽנוּ, עֲנֵֽנוּ בָּעֵת וּבָעוֹנָה הַזֹּאת.
14 Anenu עֲנֵֽנוּ אָבִֽינוּ
יש נוהגים לומר פיוט זה, כפתיחה ל"עננו". וכן מנהג יוצאי בבל וחאלב, אבל מנהג מרן הראש"ל שליט"א שלא לאומרו. וכן מנהג יוצאי מרוקו ומנהג ירושלים.
Some have the custom to recite this piyyut as an introduction to "Anenu." This is the custom of those from Babylonia and Aleppo, but the custom of Maran the Rishon LeTzion, may he live long, is not to recite it. This is also the custom of those from Morocco and Jerusalem.
O God, You know my folly and my guilts; from You none of my willful sins are hidden. When I bring to heart the greatness of my backslidings, I am poured out like water and all my bones are out of joint; hearken to the voice of my supplications. In wrath, remember mercy for him who knocks at the gates of Your compassion, who stands like a pauper begging, seeking Your forgiveness. My eyes forestalled the night watches to recount Your greatness; I will declare Your arm to a generation, Your might to all that will come. Unveil my eyes that I may behold wonders from Your Torah; let those who await the end of wonders sing to Your Name; restore to their dwelling those scattered in every corner, for You are the Lord, God of hosts. God of hosts, restore us; answer us with awesome deeds in righteousness; look upon the face of Your anointed and see our shield; and let all who take refuge in You rejoice, forever sing for joy. Answer us, our Father, answer us.
Elohiym atah yadaeta leivaletiy veashemotay, mimekha lo nikhechadu kal-zedonotay, behaalotiy al levaviy godel meshuvotay, kamaim nishepakhetiy vehiteparedu kal-atzemotay, hakeshiyvah vekol tachanunotay. Rachem berogez tizekor ledofek shaarey chemelatekha, haomed kedal shoel mevakesh seliychatekha, kidemu eynay ashemurot lesaper gedulatekha, agiyd zeroakha ledor lekhal-yavo gevuratekha. Gal eynay veabiytah mitoratekha nifelaot, yezameru leshimekha meyachaley ketz pelaot, teshovev lineveyhem pezuriym bekhal-peot, kiy atah Adonai elohiym tzevaot. Elohiym tzevaot hashiyvenu, noraot betzedek taanenu, habet peney meshiychekha ureeh maginenu, veisemechu khal-chosey vakh leolam yeranenu, anenu aviynu anenu.
אֱלֹהִים אַתָּה יָדַֽעְתָּ לְאִוַּלְתִּי וְאַשְׁמוֹתַי, מִמְּךָ לֹא נִכְחֲדֽוּ כָּל־זְדוֹנוֹתַי, בְּהַעֲלוֹתִי עַל לְבָבִי גֹּֽדֶל מְשׁוּבוֹתַי, כַּמַּֽיִם נִשְׁפַּֽכְתִּי וְהִתְפָּֽרְדֽוּ כָּל־עַצְמוֹתָי, הַקְשִֽׁיבָה בְקוֹל תַּחֲנוּנוֹתָי. רַחֵם בְּרֹֽגֶז תִּזְכֹּר לְדוֹפֵק שַׁעֲרֵי חֶמְלָתֶֽךָ, הָעוֹמֵד כְּדַל שׁוֹאֵל מְבַקֵּשׁ סְלִיחָתֶֽךָ, קִדְּמוּ עֵינַי אַשְׁמוּרוֹת לְסַפֵּר גְּדֻלָּתֶֽךָ, אַגִּיד זְרוֹעֲךָ לְדוֹר לְכָל־יָבוֹא גְּבוּרָתֶֽךָ. גַּל עֵינַי וְאַבִּיטָה מִתּוֹרָֽתְךָֽ נִפְלָאוֹת, יְזַמְּרוּ לְשִׁמְךָ מְיַחֲלֵי קֵץ פְּלָאוֹת, תְּשׁוֹבֵב לִנְוֵיהֶם פְּזוּרִים בְּכָל־פֵּאוֹת, כִּי אַתָּה יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהִים צְבָאוֹת. אֱלֹהִים צְבָאוֹת הֲשִׁיבֵֽנוּ, נוֹרָאוֹת בְּצֶֽדֶק תַּעֲנֵֽנוּ, הַבֵּט פְּנֵי מְשִׁיחֶֽךָ וּרְאֵה מָגִנֵּֽנוּ, וְיִשְׂמְחוּ כָל־חוֹסֵי בָךְ לְעוֹלָם יְרַנֵּֽנוּ, עֲנֵֽנוּ אָבִֽינוּ עֲנֵֽנוּ.
כשאומר "עננו" יזהר מאד שלא להדגיש הנו"ן, שלא ישָמע מלשון עִנּוּי חלילה.
When reciting "Anenu" (Answer us), one must be very careful not to stress the letter nun, lest it sound like the word for affliction, God forbid.
Answer us, our Father, answer us. Answer us, our Creator, answer us. Answer us, our Redeemer, answer us. Answer us, You who seek us, answer us. Answer us, O glory and majesty, answer us. Answer us, O ancient One in consolations, answer us. Answer us, O pure and upright, answer us. Answer us, O living and enduring, answer us. Answer us, O pure of eyes, answer us. Answer us, O Dweller in the heavens, answer us. Answer us, O mighty in strength, answer us. Answer us, O God who does not desire wickedness, answer us. Answer us, O King of kings of kings, answer us. Answer us, O awesome and exalted, answer us. Answer us, O Supporter of the falling, answer us. Answer us, O Helper of the poor, answer us. Answer us, O Redeemer and Savior, answer us. Answer us, O Righteous who justifies, answer us. Answer us, O Near to all who call upon Him in truth, answer us. Answer us, O high and lifted up, answer us. Answer us, O Dweller in the skies, answer us. Answer us, O Supporter of the sincere, answer us.
Anenu aviynu anenu. Anenu voreenu anenu. Anenu goalenu anenu. Anenu doreshenu anenu. Anenu hod vehadar anenu. Anenu vatiyk benechamot anenu. Anenu zakh veyashar anenu. Anenu chay vekayam anenu. Anenu tehor eynaim anenu. Anenu yoshev shamaim anenu. Anenu kabiyr kocha anenu. Anenu lo el chafetz beresha anenu. Anenu melekh malekhey hamelakhiym anenu. Anenu nora venisegav anenu. Anenu somekh nofeliym anenu. Anenu ozer daliym anenu. Anenu podeh umatziyl anenu. Anenu tzadiyk umatzediyk anenu. Anenu karov lekhal-koreayv beemet anenu. Anenu ram venisa anenu. Anenu shokhen shechakiym anenu. Anenu tomekh temiymiym anenu.
עֲנֵֽנוּ אָבִֽינוּ עֲנֵֽנוּ. עֲנֵֽנוּ בֽוֹרְאֵֽנוּ עֲנֵֽנוּ. עֲנֵֽנוּ גוֹאֲלֵֽנוּ עֲנֵֽנוּ. עֲנֵֽנוּ דֽוֹרְשֵֽׁנוּ עֲנֵֽנוּ. עֲנֵֽנוּ הוֹד וְהָדָר עֲנֵֽנוּ. עֲנֵֽנוּ וָתִיק בְּנֶחָמוֹת עֲנֵֽנוּ. עֲנֵֽנוּ זַךְ וְיָשָׁר עֲנֵֽנוּ. עֲנֵֽנוּ חַי וְקַיָּם עֲנֵֽנוּ. עֲנֵֽנוּ טְהוֹר עֵינַֽיִם עֲנֵֽנוּ. עֲנֵֽנוּ יוֹשֵׁב שָׁמַֽיִם עֲנֵֽנוּ. עֲנֵֽנוּ כַּבִּיר כֹּֽחַ עֲנֵֽנוּ. עֲנֵֽנוּ לֹא אֵל חָפֵץ בְּרֶֽשַׁע עֲנֵֽנוּ. עֲנֵֽנוּ מֶֽלֶךְ מַלְכֵי הַמְּלָכִים עֲנֵֽנוּ. עֲנֵֽנוּ נוֹרָא וְנִשְׂגָּב עֲנֵֽנוּ. עֲנֵֽנוּ סוֹמֵךְ נֽוֹפְלִֽים עֲנֵֽנוּ. עֲנֵֽנוּ עוֹזֵר דַּלִּים עֲנֵֽנוּ. עֲנֵֽנוּ פּוֹדֶה וּמַצִּיל עֲנֵֽנוּ. עֲנֵֽנוּ צַדִּיק וּמַצְדִּיק עֲנֵֽנוּ. עֲנֵֽנוּ קָרוֹב לְכָל־קֽוֹרְאָֽיו בֶּאֱמֶת עֲנֵֽנוּ. עֲנֵֽנוּ רָם וְנִשָּׂא עֲנֵֽנוּ. עֲנֵֽנוּ שׁוֹכֵן שְׁחָקִים עֲנֵֽנוּ. עֲנֵֽנוּ תּוֹמֵךְ תְּמִימִים עֲנֵֽנוּ.
Answer us, O God of Abraham, answer us. Answer us, O Fear of Isaac, answer us. Answer us, O Mighty One of Jacob, answer us. Answer us, O Mercy of the Matriarchs, answer us. Answer us, O He who answers in a time of distress, answer us. Answer us, O He who answers in a time of favor, answer us.
Anenu elohey averaham anenu. Anenu ufachad itzechak anenu. Anenu aviyr yaakov anenu. Anenu magen david anenu. Anenu haoneh veet ratzon anenu. Anenu haoneh veet tzarah anenu. Anenu haoneh veet rachamiym anenu. Anenu elohey hamerekavah anenu. Anenu rachum vechanun anenu.
עֲנֵֽנוּ אֱלֹהֵי אַבְרָהָם עֲנֵֽנוּ. עֲנֵֽנוּ וּפַֽחַד יִצְחָק עֲנֵֽנוּ. עֲנֵֽנוּ אֲבִיר יַעֲקֹב עֲנֵֽנוּ. עֲנֵֽנוּ מָגֵן דָּוִד עֲנֵֽנוּ. עֲנֵֽנוּ הָעוֹנֶה בְעֵת רָצוֹן עֲנֵֽנוּ. עֲנֵֽנוּ הָעוֹנֶה בְעֵת צָרָה עֲנֵֽנוּ. עֲנֵֽנוּ הָעוֹנֶה בְעֵת רַחֲמִים עֲנֵֽנוּ. עֲנֵֽנוּ אֱלֹהֵי הַמֶּרְכָּבָה עֲנֵֽנוּ. עֲנֵֽנוּ רַחוּם וְחַנּוּן עֲנֵֽנוּ.
15 Adon HaSelichot אֲדֽוֹן הַסְּלִיחֽוֹת
Compassionate and gracious One, I have sinned before You. O Lord, full of mercy, have mercy upon me and accept my supplications.
Rachum vechanun chatanu lefaneykha rachem aleynu.
רַחוּם וְחַנּוּן חָטָֽאנוּ לְפָנֶֽיךָ רַחֵם עָלֵֽינוּ.
Master of forgiveness, Examiner of hearts, Revealer of depths, Speaker of righteousness: we have sinned before You, have mercy upon us. Glorious in wonders, Ancient in consolations, Rememberer of the patriarchal covenant, Searcher of hearts: we have sinned before You, have mercy upon us.
Adon haseliychot, bochen levavot, goleh amukot, dover tzedakot, chatanu lefaneykha rachem aleynu. Hadur benifelaot, vatiyk benechamot, zokher beriyt avot, choker kelayot, chatanu lefaneykha rachem aleynu. Tov umetiyv laberiyot, yodea kal-nisetarot, kovesh avonot, lovesh tzedakot, chatanu lefaneykha rachem aleynu. Male zakhiyot, nora tehilot, solecha avonot, oneh beet tzarot, chatanu lefaneykha rachem aleynu. Poel yeshuot, tzofeh atiydot, kore hadorot, rokhev aravot, shomea tefilot, temiym deot, chatanu lefaneykha rachem aleynu.
אֲדֽוֹן הַסְּלִיחֽוֹת, בּוֹחֵֽן לְבָבֽוֹת, גּוֹלֶֽה עֲמוּקֽוֹת, דּוֹבֵֽר צְדָקֽוֹת, חָטָֽאנוּ לְפָנֶֽיךָ רַחֵֽם עָלֵֽינוּ. הָדֽוּר בְּנִפְלָאֽוֹת, וָתִֽיק בְּנֶחָמֽוֹת, זוֹכֵֽר בְּרִֽית אָבֽוֹת, חוֹקֵֽר כְּלָיֽוֹת, חָטָֽאנוּ לְפָנֶֽיךָ רַחֵֽם עָלֵֽינוּ. טוֹב וּמֵטִֽיב לַבְּרִיּֽוֹת, יוֹדֵֽעַ כָּל־נִסְתָּרֽוֹת, כּוֹבֵֽשׁ עֲוֹנֽוֹת, לוֹבֵֽשׁ צְדָקֽוֹת, חָטָֽאנוּ לְפָנֶֽיךָ רַחֵֽם עָלֵֽינוּ. מָלֵֽא זָכִיּֽוֹת, נוֹרָֽא תְהִלּֽוֹת, סוֹלֵֽחַ עֲוֹנֽוֹת, עוֹנֶֽה בְּעֵֽת צָרֽוֹת, חָטָֽאנוּ לְפָנֶֽיךָ רַחֵֽם עָלֵֽינוּ. פּוֹעֵֽל יְשׁוּעֽוֹת, צוֹפֶֽה עֲתִידֽוֹת, קוֹרֵֽא הַדּוֹרֽוֹת, רוֹכֵֽב עֲרָבֽוֹת, שׁוֹמֵֽעַ תְּפִלּֽוֹת, תְּמִֽים דֵּעֽוֹת, חָטָֽאנוּ לְפָנֶֽיךָ רַחֵֽם עָלֵֽינוּ.
Compassionate God is Your Name; gracious God is Your Name. See the affliction of Your people; have mercy on Your world.
El rachum shimekha, el chanun shimekha, el erekh apaim shimekha, male rachamiym shimekha, banu nikera shimekha, Adonai aseh lemaan shemekha.
אֵל רַחוּם שִׁמְךָ, אֵל חַנּוּן שִׁמְךָ, אֵל אֶֽרֶךְ אַפַּֽיִם שִׁמְךָ, מָלֵא רַחֲמִים שִׁמְךָ, בָּֽנוּ נִקְרָא שִׁמְךָ, יְהֹוָה עֲשֵׂה לְמַֽעַן שְׁמֶֽךָ.
16 El Rachum Shimkha אֵל רַחוּם שְׁמָךְ
יש נוהגים להוסיף בקשה זו בעשרת ימי תשובה. וכן מנהג יוצאי בבל ודמשק. ומנהג יוצאי חאלב לאומרו כל ימי הסליחות.
Some have the custom to add this supplication during the Ten Days of Repentance. This is the custom of those from Babylonia and Damascus. The custom of those from Aleppo is to recite it throughout all the days of Selichot.
Compassionate God is Your Name; gracious God is Your Name. See the affliction of Your people; have mercy on Your world.
El rachum shemakh, el chanun shemakh. Reeh bavoniy amakh, rachem al olamakh.
אֵל רַחוּם שְׁמָךְ, אֵל חַנּוּן שְׁמָךְ. רְאֵה בָּעֳנִֽי עַמָּךְ, רַחֵם עַל עוֹלָמָךְ.
Mighty God is Your Name, blessed God is Your Name. Act for the sake of Your great Name. See the affliction of Your people; have mercy on Your world.
El adiyr shemakh, el barukh shemakh, el gadol shemakh, el dagul shemakh. Reeh bavoniy amakh, rachem al olamakh.
אֵל אַדִּיר שְׁמָךְ, אֵל בָּרוּךְ שְׁמָךְ, אֵל גָּדוֹל שְׁמָךְ, אֵל דָּגוּל שְׁמָךְ. רְאֵה בָּעֳנִֽי עַמָּךְ, רַחֵם עַל עוֹלָמָךְ.
Glorious God is Your Name, ancient God is Your Name, pure God is Your Name, gracious God is Your Name. See the affliction of Your people; have mercy on Your world.
El hadur shemakh, el vatiyk shemakh, el zakay shemakh, el chonen shemakh. Reeh bavoniy amakh, rachem al olamakh.
אֵל הָדוּר שְׁמָךְ, אֵל וָתִיק שְׁמָךְ, אֵל זַכַּֽאי שְׁמָךְ, אֵל חוֹנֵן שְׁמָךְ. רְאֵה בָּעֳנִֽי עַמָּךְ, רַחֵם עַל עוֹלָמָךְ.
Pure God is Your Name, unique God is Your Name, mighty God is Your Name, eternal God is Your Name. See the affliction of Your people; have mercy on Your world.
El tahor shemakh, el yachiyd shemakh, el kabiyr shemakh, el laad shemakh. Reeh bavoniy amakh, rachem al olamakh.
אֵל טָהוֹר שְׁמָךְ, אֵל יָחִיד שְׁמָךְ, אֵל כַּבִּיר שְׁמָךְ, אֵל לָעַד שְׁמָךְ. רְאֵה בָּעֳנִֽי עַמָּךְ, רַחֵם עַל עוֹלָמָךְ.
King God is Your Name, awesome God is Your Name, supporting God is Your Name, helping God is Your Name. See the affliction of Your people; have mercy on Your world.
El melekh shemakh, el nora shemakh, el somekh shemakh, el ozer shemakh. Reeh bavoniy amakh, rachem al olamakh.
אֵל מֶֽלֶךְ שְׁמָךְ, אֵל נוֹרָא שְׁמָךְ, אֵל סוֹמֵךְ שְׁמָךְ, אֵל עוֹזֵר שְׁמָךְ. רְאֵה בָּעֳנִֽי עַמָּךְ, רַחֵם עַל עוֹלָמָךְ.
Redeeming God is Your Name, righteous God is Your Name, holy God is Your Name, merciful God is Your Name. See the affliction of Your people; have mercy on Your world.
El podeh shemakh, el tzadiyk shemakh, el kadosh shemakh, el rachaman shemakh. Reeh bavoniy amakh, rachem al olamakh.
אֵל פּוֹדֶה שְׁמָךְ, אֵל צַדִּיק שְׁמָךְ, אֵל קָדוֹשׁ שְׁמָךְ, אֵל רַחֲמָן שְׁמָךְ. רְאֵה בָּעֳנִֽי עַמָּךְ, רַחֵם עַל עוֹלָמָךְ.
Almighty God is Your Name, guarding God is Your Name, upholding God is Your Name, perfect God is Your Name. See the affliction of Your people; have mercy on Your world.
El shaday shemakh, el shomer shemakh, el tomekh shemakh, el tamiym shemakh. Reeh bavoniy amakh, rachem al olamakh.
אֵל שַׁדַּי שְׁמָךְ, אֵל שׁוֹמֵר שְׁמָךְ, אֵל תּוֹמֵךְ שְׁמָךְ, אֵל תָּמִים שְׁמָךְ. רְאֵה בָּעֳנִֽי עַמָּךְ, רַחֵם עַל עוֹלָמָךְ.
17 Adonai Chonnenu יֶהֶוֶהֶ חָנֵּֽנוּ וַהֲקִימֵֽנוּ
בעשרת ימי תשובה אומרים:
During the Ten Days of Repentance, the following is recited:
O Lord, be gracious to us and raise us up; and in the Book of Life, remember us and inscribe us.
Adonai chanenu vahakiymenu. Uvesefer chaiym zakherenu vekhatevenu.
יֶהֶוֶהֶ חָנֵּֽנוּ וַהֲקִימֵֽנוּ. וּבְסֵֽפֶר חַיִּים זָכְרֵֽנוּ וְכָתְבֵֽנוּ.
O Lord, on the day of salvation bring us tidings and have mercy; and in the Book of Life, remember us and inscribe us.
Yeheveh beyom yeshuatah baserenu verachamenu. Uvesefer chaiym zakherenu vekhatevenu.
יְהְוְהְ בְּיוֹם יְשׁוּעָֽתָה בַּשְּׂרֵֽנוּ וְרַחֲמֵֽנוּ. וּבְסֵֽפֶר חַיִּים זָכְרֵֽנוּ וְכָתְבֵֽנוּ.
O Lord, cause the multitude of Your mercies to flow upon us; and in the Book of Life, remember us and inscribe us.
Yohooho galegel hamon rachameykha aleynu. Uvesefer chaiym zakherenu vekhatevenu.
יֹהֹוֹהֹ גַּלְגֵּל הֲמוֹן רַחֲמֶֽיךָ עָלֵֽינוּ. וּבְסֵֽפֶר חַיִּים זָכְרֵֽנוּ וְכָתְבֵֽנוּ.
O Lord, let Your mercies be aroused upon us; and in the Book of Life, remember us and inscribe us.
Ihivihi yehemu-na rachameykha aleynu. Uvesefer chaiym zakherenu vekhatevenu.
יִהִוִהִ יֶֽהֱמוּ־נָֽא רַחֲמֶֽיךָ עָלֵֽינוּ. וּבְסֵֽפֶר חַיִּים זָכְרֵֽנוּ וְכָתְבֵֽנוּ.
O Lord, set us as a seal upon Your heart this day; and in the Book of Life, remember us and inscribe us.
Yuhuvuhu kachotam al lev hayom siymenu. Uvesefer chaiym zakherenu vekhatevenu.
יֻהֻוֻהֻ כַּחוֹתָם עַל לֵב הַיּוֹם שִׂימֵֽנוּ. וּבְסֵֽפֶר חַיִּים זָכְרֵֽנוּ וְכָתְבֵֽנוּ.
O Lord, champion our cause and fight our battles; and in the Book of Life, remember us and inscribe us.
Yuhuvuhu riyvah riyvenu ulecham lochameynu. Uvesefer chaiym zakherenu vekhatevenu.
יוּהוּווּהוּ רִיבָֽה רִיבֵֽנוּ וּלְחַם לוֹחֲמֵֽינוּ. וּבְסֵֽפֶר חַיִּים זָכְרֵֽנוּ וְכָתְבֵֽנוּ.
O Lord, bless our bread and our water; and in the Book of Life, remember us and inscribe us.
Adonai barekh et lachemenu veet meymeynu. Uvesefer chaiym zakherenu vekhatevenu.
יְהֹוָה בָּרֵךְ אֶת לַחְמֵֽנוּ וְאֶת מֵימֵֽינוּ. וּבְסֵֽפֶר חַיִּים זָכְרֵֽנוּ וְכָתְבֵֽנוּ.
עד כאן מוסיפים בעשרת ימי תשובה.
End of the addition for the Ten Days of Repentance.
18 Adonai Aseh Lema'an יְהֹוָה עֲשֵׂה לְמַֽעַן שְׁמֶֽךָ
O Lord, act for the sake of Your Name, and have mercy upon Israel Your people.
Adonai aseh lemaan shemekha, vechusah al iserael amekha.
יְהֹוָה עֲשֵׂה לְמַֽעַן שְׁמֶֽךָ, וְחֽוּסָה עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל עַמֶּֽךָ.
O Lord, act for the sake of Abraham, Your wholehearted one, and have mercy upon Israel Your people.
Adonai aseh lemaan averaham ezerach temiymekha, vechusah al iserael amekha.
יְהֹוָה עֲשֵׂה לְמַֽעַן אַבְרָהָם אֶזְרָח תְּמִימֶֽךָ, וְחֽוּסָה עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל עַמֶּֽךָ.
O Lord, act for the sake of Isaac, bound at Your altar, and have mercy upon Israel Your people.
Adonai aseh lemaan itzechak neekad beulamekha, vechusah al iserael amekha.
יְהֹוָה עֲשֵׂה לְמַֽעַן יִצְחָק נֶעֱקַד בְּאוּלַמֶּֽךָ, וְחֽוּסָה עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל עַמֶּֽךָ.
O Lord, act for the sake of Jacob, and have mercy upon Israel Your people.
Adonai aseh lemaan yaakov neenah vesulam mimeromeykha, vechusah al iserael amekha.
יְהֹוָה עֲשֵׂה לְמַֽעַן יַעֲקֹב נֶעֱנָה בְסֻלָּם מִמְּרוֹמֶֽיךָ, וְחֽוּסָה עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל עַמֶּֽךָ.
O Lord, act for the sake of Moses, faithful in all Your house, and have mercy upon Israel Your people.
Adonai aseh lemaan mosheh neeman bekhal-beytekha, vechusah al iserael amekha.
יְהֹוָה עֲשֵׂה לְמַֽעַן מֹשֶׁה נֶאֱמָן בְּכָל־בֵּיתֶֽךָ, וְחֽוּסָה עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל עַמֶּֽךָ.
O Lord, act for the sake of Aaron, and have mercy upon Israel Your people.
Adonai aseh lemaan aharon kihen beureykha vetumeykha, vechusah al iserael amekha.
יְהֹוָה עֲשֵׂה לְמַֽעַן אַהֲרֹן כִּהֵן בְּאוּרֶֽיךָ וְתֻמֶּֽיךָ, וְחֽוּסָה עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל עַמֶּֽךָ.
O Lord, act for the sake of the merit of Joseph, and have mercy upon Israel Your people.
Adonai aseh lemaan zekhut yosef asiyr tzadiykekha, vechusah al iserael amekha.
יְהֹוָה עֲשֵׂה לְמַֽעַן זְכוּת יוֹסֵף אָסִיר צַדִּיקֶֽךָ, וְחֽוּסָה עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל עַמֶּֽךָ.
O Lord, act for the sake of King David, and have mercy upon Israel Your people.
Adonai aseh lemaan melekh david neiym zemiyroteykha, vechusah al iserael amekha.
יְהֹוָה עֲשֵׂה לְמַֽעַן מֶֽלֶךְ דָּוִד נְעִים זְמִירוֹתֶֽיךָ, וְחֽוּסָה עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל עַמֶּֽךָ.
O Lord, act for the sake of Pinchas, zealous for Your Name, and have mercy upon Israel Your people.
Adonai aseh lemaan piynechas zakh kine lishemekha, vechusah al iserael amekha.
יְהֹוָה עֲשֵׂה לְמַֽעַן פִּֽינְחָֽס זַךְ קִנֵּא לִשְׁמֶֽךָ, וְחֽוּסָה עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל עַמֶּֽךָ.
O Lord, act for the sake of King Solomon, who built a House for Your Name, and have mercy upon Israel Your people.
Adonai aseh lemaan melekh shelomoh banah bait lishemekha, vechusah al iserael amekha.
יְהֹוָה עֲשֵׂה לְמַֽעַן מֶֽלֶךְ שְׁלֹמֹה בָּֽנָה בַּֽיִת לִשְׁמֶֽךָ, וְחֽוּסָה עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל עַמֶּֽךָ.
O Lord, act for the sake of those slain for Your holy Name, and have mercy upon Israel Your people.
Adonai aseh lemaan harugiym userufiym al ichud kedushat shemekha, vechusah al iserael amekha.
יְהֹוָה עֲשֵׂה לְמַֽעַן הֲרוּגִים וּשְׂרוּפִים עַל יִחוּד קְדֻשַּׁת שְׁמֶֽךָ, וְחֽוּסָה עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל עַמֶּֽךָ.
O Lord, act for the sake of Your Name, and have mercy upon Israel Your people.
Adonai aseh lemaan shemekha, vechusah al iserael amekha.

עֲשֵׂה לְמַֽעַן אַבְרָהָם יִצְחָק וְיַעֲקֹב. אָמֵן עֲשֵׂה לְמַֽעַן מֹשֶׁה וְאַהֲרֹן. אָמֵן עֲשֵׂה לְמַֽעַן יוֹסֵף דָּוִד וּשְׁלֹמֹה. אָמֵן עֲשֵׂה לְמַֽעַן יְרוּשָׁלַֽיִם עִיר הַקֹּֽדֶשׁ. אָמֵן עֲשֵׂה לְמַֽעַן צִיּוֹן מִשְׁכַּן כְּבוֹדָךְ. אָמֵן עֲשֵׂה לְמַֽעַן חָרְבַּן בֵּיתָךְ. אָמֵן עֲשֵׂה לְמַֽעַן שִׁמְמוּת הֵיכָלָךְ. אָמֵן עֲשֵׂה לְמַֽעַן יִשְׂרָאֵל הָעֲנִיִּים. אָמֵן עֲשֵׂה לְמַֽעַן יִשְׂרָאֵל הַדַּלִּים. אָמֵן עֲשֵׂה לְמַֽעַן יִשְׂרָאֵל הַשְּׁרוּיִים בְּצָרוֹת. אָמֵן עֲשֵׂה לְמַֽעַן יְתוֹמִים וְאַלְמָנוֹת. אָמֵן עֲשֵׂה לְמַֽעַן יֽוֹנְקֵֽי שָׁדַֽיִם. אָמֵן עֲשֵׂה לְמַֽעַן גְּמוּלֵי חָלָב. אָמֵן עֲשֵׂה לְמַֽעַן תִּינוֹקוֹת שֶׁל בֵּית רַבָּן שֶׁלֹּא חָֽטְאֽוּ. אָמֵן
בעשרת ימי תשובה מנהג יוצאי בבל להוסיף תחינה זו, המיוסדת על פי קטעים מפרקי היכלות דרבי ישמעאל.
During the Ten Days of Repentance, the custom of those from Babylonia is to add this supplication, based on passages from the Heikhalot of Rabbi Yishmael.
Act for the sake of Your holy and pure angels who minister before You, who seek mercy for Israel Your people. There is a company above called annulers of decree, voiders of oath, removers of jealousy, turners-back of wrath, reminders of love—the love of Abraham our father—before their Creator. When they see that He is angry with His children, they prostrate themselves before the throne of His glory, and entreat and say: Release, release, O Fashioner of the beginning; forgive, forgive, O Mighty One of Jacob; pardon, pardon, O Holy One of Israel—for You are majestic among kings. King majestic, King blessed, King great, King distinguished, King glorious, King ancient, King pure, King gracious, King clean, King upright, King mighty, King forever, King who has compassion, King awesome, King who supports, King who helps, King who redeems, King righteous, King clear, King near, King exalted, King who guards, King whole. Why enmity for You with the seed of Abraham who loves You? Why jealousy for You with the seed of Isaac, Your binding? Why rivalry for You with the seed of Jacob, Your sincere one?—for You have called them the possession of heaven and earth. At once horns of splendor go forth from beneath the Throne of Glory and proclaim and say: Happy are you who teach merit concerning My children; praise to you who draw down the merit of the fathers— for all the host of heaven and earth will give you glory and greatness.
Aseh lemaan maleakheykha hakedoshiym vehatehoriym hamesharetiym paneykha, hamevakeshiym rachamiym al iserael amekha. Kat yesh lemaelah nikeraiym mevateley gezerah, mefiyrey shevuah, maaviyrey kineah, meshiyvey chemah, mazekiyrey ahavah, ahavato shel averaham aviynu, lifeney voream. Keshehem roiym shehu vekhaas al banayv, mishetatechiym lifeney khise khevodo, umitechaneniym veomeriym. Hater hater yotzer bereshiyt, mechol mechol aviyr yaakov, selach selach kedosh iserael, kiy adiyr melakhiym atah. Melekh adiyr, melekh barukh, melekh gadol, melekh dagul, melekh hadur, melekh vatiyk, melekh zakh, melekh chanun, melekh tahor, melekh yashar, melekh kabiyr, melekh laad, melekh merachem, melekh nora, melekh somekh, melekh ozer, melekh podeh, melekh tzadiyk, melekh tzach, melekh karov, melekh ram, melekh shomer, melekh tam. Lamah lakh eyvah im zera averaham ohavekha, lamah lakh kineah im zera itzechak akedekha, lamah lakh tacherut im zera yaakov temiymekha, kiy kineyan shamaim vaaretz karata otam. Miyad yotzeot karenot hod mitachat kise hakavod umakheriyzot veomerot, ashereykhem melamedey zekhut al banay, shevach lakhem meshalesheley zekhut avot, kiy khal-tzeva hashamayim vehaaretz kavod ugedulah itenu lakhem.
עֲשֵׂה לְמַֽעַן מַלְאָכֶֽיךָ הַקְּדוֹשִׁים וְהַטְּהוֹרִים הַמְשָֽׁרְתִֽים פָּנֶֽיךָ, הַמְבַקְשִׁים רַחֲמִים עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל עַמֶּֽךָ. כַּת יֵשׁ לְמַֽעְלָה נִקְרָאִים מְבַטְּלֵי גְזֵרָה, מְפִירֵי שְׁבוּעָה, מַעֲבִירֵי קִנְאָה, מְשִׁיבֵי חֵמָה, מַזְכִּירֵי אַהֲבָה, אַהֲבָתוֹ שֶׁל אַבְרָהָם אָבִֽינוּ, לִפְנֵי בֽוֹרְאָֽם. כְּשֶׁהֵם רוֹאִים שֶׁהוּא בְכַֽעַס עַל בָּנָיו, מִשְׁתַּטְּחִים לִפְנֵי כִסֵּא כְבוֹדוֹ, וּמִתְחַנְּנִים וְאֽוֹמְרִֽים. הַתֵּר הַתֵּר יוֹצֵר בְּרֵאשִׁית, מְחֹל מְחֹל אֲבִיר יַעֲקֹב, סְלַח סְלַח קְדוֹשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵל, כִּי אַדִּיר מְלָכִים אָֽתָּה. מֶֽלֶךְ אַדִּיר, מֶֽלֶךְ בָּרוּךְ, מֶֽלֶךְ גָּדוֹל, מֶֽלֶךְ דָּגוּל, מֶֽלֶךְ הָדוּר, מֶֽלֶךְ וָתִיק, מֶֽלֶךְ זַךְ, מֶֽלֶךְ חַנּוּן, מֶֽלֶךְ טָהוֹר, מֶֽלֶךְ יָשָׁר, מֶֽלֶךְ כַּבִּיר, מֶֽלֶךְ לָעַד, מֶֽלֶךְ מְרַחֵם, מֶֽלֶךְ נוֹרָא, מֶֽלֶךְ סוֹמֵךְ, מֶֽלֶךְ עוֹזֵר, מֶֽלֶךְ פּוֹדֶה, מֶֽלֶךְ צַדִּיק, מֶֽלֶךְ צַח, מֶֽלֶךְ קָרוֹב, מֶֽלֶךְ רָם, מֶֽלֶךְ שׁוֹמֵר, מֶֽלֶךְ תָּם. לָֽמָּה לָּךְ אֵיבָה עִם זֶֽרַע אַבְרָהָם אוֹהֲבֶֽךָ, לָֽמָּה לָּךְ קִנְאָה עִם זֶֽרַע יִצְחָק עֲקֵדֶֽךָ, לָֽמָּה לָּךְ תַּחְרוּת עִם זֶֽרַע יַעֲקֹב תְּמִימֶֽךָ, כִּי קִנְיַן שָׁמַֽיִם וָאָֽרֶץ קָרָֽאתָ אוֹתָם. מִיַּד יֽוֹצְאֽוֹת קַרְנוֹת הוֹד מִתַּֽחַת כִּסֵּא הַכָּבוֹד וּמַכְרִיזוֹת וְאֽוֹמְרֽוֹת, אַשְׁרֵיכֶם מְלַמְּדֵי זְכוּת עַל בָּנַי, שֶֽׁבַח לָכֶם מְשַׁלְשְׁלֵי זְכוּת אָבוֹת, כִּי כָל־צְבָא הַשָּׁמַֽיִם וְהָאָֽרֶץ כָּבוֹד וּגְדֻלָּה יִתְּנוּ לָכֶם.
Act for Your sake if not for ours. Act for Your sake and save us.
Aseh lemaanakh im lo lemaanenu, aseh lemaanakh vehoshiyenu. Hoshiyenu vaanenu hayom uvekhal-yom vayom bitefilatenu, kiy tehilatenu atah.
עֲשֵׂה לְמַעֲנָךְ אִם לֹא לְמַעֲנֵֽנוּ, עֲשֵׂה לְמַעֲנָךְ וְהוֹשִׁיעֵֽנוּ. הוֹשִׁיעֵֽנוּ וַעֲנֵֽנוּ הַיּוֹם וּבְכָל־יוֹם וָיוֹם בִּתְפִלָּתֵֽנוּ, כִּי תְהִלָּתֵֽנוּ אָֽתָּה.
19 De'anei דְּעָנֵי לַעֲנִיֵּי
He who answers the poor, answer us. He who answers the oppressed, answer us. He who answers the brokenhearted, answer us. He who answers the lowly in spirit, answer us. He who answers the humble in spirit, answer us. He who answered Abraham our father on Mount Moriah, answer us. He who answered Isaac upon the altar, answer us. He who answered Jacob in Bethel, answer us. He who answered our ancestors at the Sea of Reeds, answer us. He who answered Moses at Horeb, answer us. He who answered Aaron with the incense, answer us. He who answered Elijah on Mount Carmel, answer us. He who answered Jonah in the belly of the fish, answer us. He who answered Daniel in the lions' den, answer us. He who answered Mordechai and Esther in Shushan, answer us. He who answers the righteous and the pious, answer us. O God of mercy, answer us.
Deaney laaniyey, aneynan. Deaney laashiykey, aneynan. Deaney liteviyrey liba, aneynan. Deaney leshifeley daeta, aneynan. Deaney lemakiykhey rucha, aneynan. Deaney leaveraham aviynu behar hamoriyah, aneynan. Deaney leitzechak al gabey madebecha, aneynan. Deaney leyaakov beveyt el, aneynan. Deaney leyosef beveyt asiyrey, aneynan. Deaney lemosheh vaavoteynu al yam suf, aneynan. Deaney leaharon bamachetah, aneynan. Deaney lefiynechas bashitiym, aneynan. Deaney liyhoshua bagilegal, aneynan. Deaney leeliy baramah, aneynan. Deaney lishemuel bamitzepah, aneynan. Deaney ledavid velishelomoh veno biyrushalaim, aneynan. Deaney leeliyahu behar hakaremel, aneynan. Deaney leeliysha biyriycho, aneynan. Deaney lechizekiyahu bachaloto, aneynan. Deaney leyonah bimeey hadagah, aneynan. Deaney lachananeyah miyshael vaazareyah bego atun nura yakideta, aneynan. Deaney ledaniyel beguba deareyavata, aneynan. Deaney lemaredekhay veeseter beshushan habiyrah, aneynan. Deaney leezera bagolah, aneynan. Deaney lechoniy bamaegal, aneynan. Deaney letzadiykey vachasiydey utemiymey diy vekhal-dar vedar, aneynan. Rachamana aneynan, rachamana sheziyv, rachamana perok, rachamana deitemeley rachamiyn rachem alana veal kal-aneshey veytana, veal kal-iserael achana, umechashokha linehora apekiynan bediyl shemakh raba.
דְּעָנֵי לַעֲנִיֵּי, עֲנֵינָֽן. דְּעָנֵי לַעֲשִׁיקֵי, עֲנֵינָֽן. דְּעָנֵי לִתְבִירֵֽי לִבָּא, עֲנֵינָֽן. דְּעָנֵי לְשִׁפְלֵי דַעְתָּא, עֲנֵינָֽן. דְּעָנֵי לְמַכִּיכֵי רוּחָא, עֲנֵינָֽן. דְּעָנֵי לְאַבְרָהָם אָבִֽינוּ בְּהַר הַמֹּרִיָּה, עֲנֵינָֽן. דְּעָנֵי לְיִצְחָק עַל גַּבֵּי מַדְבְּחָא, עֲנֵינָֽן. דְּעָנֵי לְיַעֲקֹב בְּבֵית אֵל, עֲנֵינָֽן. דְּעָנֵי לְיוֹסֵף בְּבֵית אֲסִירֵי, עֲנֵינָֽן. דְּעָנֵי לְמֹשֶׁה וַאֲבוֹתֵֽינוּ עַל יַם סוּף, עֲנֵינָֽן. דְּעָנֵי לְאַהֲרֹן בַּמַּחְתָּה, עֲנֵינָֽן. דְּעָנֵי לְפִֽינְחָֽס בַּשִּׁטִּים, עֲנֵינָֽן. דְּעָנֵי לִיהוֹשֻֽׁעַ בַּגִּלְגָּל, עֲנֵינָֽן. דְּעָנֵי לְעֵלִי בָּרָמָה, עֲנֵינָֽן. דְּעָנֵי לִשְׁמוּאֵל בַּמִּצְפָּה, עֲנֵינָֽן. דְּעָנֵי לְדָוִד וְלִשְׁלֹמֹה בְנוֹ בִּירוּשָׁלַֽיִם, עֲנֵינָֽן. דְּעָנֵי לְאֵלִיָּֽהוּ בְּהַר הַכַּרְמֶל, עֲנֵינָֽן. דְּעָנֵי לֶאֱלִישָׁע בִּירִיחוֹ, עֲנֵינָֽן. דְּעָנֵי לְחִזְקִיָּֽהוּ בַּחֲלוֹתוֹ, עֲנֵינָֽן. דְּעָנֵי לְיוֹנָה בִּמְעֵי הַדָּגָה, עֲנֵינָֽן. דְּעָנֵי לַחֲנַנְיָה מִישָׁאֵל וַעֲזַרְיָה בְּגוֹ אַתּוּן נוּרָא יָקִדְתָּא, עֲנֵינָֽן. דְּעָנֵי לְדָנִיֵּאל בְּגֻבָּא דְאַרְיָוָתָא, עֲנֵינָֽן. דְּעָנֵי לְמָרְדְּכַי וְאֶסְתֵּר בְּשׁוּשַׁן הַבִּירָה, עֲנֵינָֽן. דְּעָנֵי לְעֶזְרָא בַּגּוֹלָה, עֲנֵינָֽן. דְּעָנֵי לְחוֹנִי בַּמַּעְגָּל, עֲנֵינָֽן. דְּעָנֵי לְצַדִּיקֵי וַחֲסִידֵי וּתְמִימֵי דִּי בְכָל־דָּר וְדָר, עֲנֵינָֽן. רַחֲמָנָא עֲנֵינָֽן, רַחֲמָנָא שְׁזִיב, רַחֲמָנָא פְּרֹק, רַחֲמָנָא דְּיִתְמְלֵי רַחֲמִין רַחֵם עָלָֽנָא וְעַל כָּל־אַנְשֵׁי בֵיתָֽנָא, וְעַל כָּל־יִשְׂרָאֵל אָחָֽנָא, וּמֵחֲשׁוֹכָא לִנְהוֹרָא אַפְּקִינָֽן בְּדִיל שְׁמָךְ רַבָּא.
20 Yah Shema יָהּ שְׁמַע אֶבְיוֹנֶֽיךָ
בעשרת ימי תשובה נוהגים לומר פיוט זה. ויוצאי חאלב לא נהגו לאומרו.
During the Ten Days of Repentance, it is customary to recite this piyyut. Those from Aleppo did not have this custom.
O God, hear Your needy ones, who beseech Your countenance. Our Father, for Your children, do not hide Your ears.
Yah shema eveyoneykha, hamechaliym paneykha. Aviynu levaneykha, al taelem azeneykha.
יָהּ שְׁמַע אֶבְיוֹנֶֽיךָ, הַמְחַלִּים פָּנֶֽיךָ. אָבִֽינוּ לְבָנֶֽיךָ, אַל תַּעְלֵם אָזְנֶֽיךָ.
O God, a people cries out from the depths, from great distress. Please do not send them away empty from before You.
Yah am mimaamakiym, ikereu merov metzukiym, al-na teshiyvem rekiym, hayom milefaneykha. Yah shema eveyoneykha, hamechaliym paneykha. Aviynu levaneykha, al taelem azeneykha.
יָהּ עַם מִמַּעֲמַקִּים, יִקְרְאוּ מֵרֹב מְצוּקִים, אַל־נָא תְשִׁיבֵם רֵקִים, הַיּוֹם מִלְּפָנֶֽיךָ. יָהּ שְׁמַע אֶבְיוֹנֶֽיךָ, הַמְחַלִּים פָּנֶֽיךָ. אָבִֽינוּ לְבָנֶֽיךָ, אַל תַּעְלֵם אָזְנֶֽיךָ.
Their wrongs and their iniquity, erase, and their many willful sins. And if You will not act for their sake, act, O my Rock, for the sake of Your Name.
Hauotam vaavonam, mecheh verubey zedonam, veim lo taaseh lemaanam, aseh tzuriy lemaanekha. Yah shema eveyoneykha, hamechaliym paneykha. Aviynu levaneykha, al taelem azeneykha.
הַוּוֹתָם וַעֲוֹנָם, מְחֵה וְרֻבֵּי זְדוֹנָם, וְאִם לֹא תַעֲשֶׂה לְמַעֲנָם, עֲשֵׂה צוּרִֽי לְמַעֲנֶֽךָ. יָהּ שְׁמַע אֶבְיוֹנֶֽיךָ, הַמְחַלִּים פָּנֶֽיךָ. אָבִֽינוּ לְבָנֶֽיךָ, אַל תַּעְלֵם אָזְנֶֽיךָ.
And erase this day their debt, and accept their words as a gift. And prepare their hearts for You, and incline Your ear. Hearken to their cry and turn to their prayer.
Umecheh hayom chovam, uretzeh kemo shay niyvam, ulekha takhiyn libam, vegam takeshiyv azenekha. Yah shema eveyoneykha, hamechaliym paneykha. Aviynu levaneykha, al taelem azeneykha.
וּמְחֵה הַיּוֹם חוֹבָם, וּרְצֵה כְּמוֹ שַׁי נִיבָם, וּלְךָ תָּכִין לִבָּם, וְגַם תַּקְשִׁיב אָזְנֶֽךָ. יָהּ שְׁמַע אֶבְיוֹנֶֽיךָ, הַמְחַלִּים פָּנֶֽיךָ. אָבִֽינוּ לְבָנֶֽיךָ, אַל תַּעְלֵם אָזְנֶֽיךָ.
Attend to the tears upon their faces, and gather the wandering flock. And raise up for them a shepherd, and graciously visit Your flock.
Dimeat peneyhem tisheeh, veteesof eder toeh, vetakiym lekha roeh, ufekod betov tzonekha. Yah shema eveyoneykha, hamechaliym paneykha. Aviynu levaneykha, al taelem azeneykha.
דִּמְעַת פְּנֵיהֶם תִּשְׁעֶה, וְתֶאֱסֹף עֵֽדֶר תּוֹעֶה, וְתָקִים לְךָ רוֹעֶֽה, וּפְקֹד בְּטוֹב צֹאנֶֽךָ. יָהּ שְׁמַע אֶבְיוֹנֶֽיךָ, הַמְחַלִּים פָּנֶֽיךָ. אָבִֽינוּ לְבָנֶֽיךָ, אַל תַּעְלֵם אָזְנֶֽיךָ.
Those who walk in the upright way, grant them this day forgiveness. And in their heartfelt prayer, let them find Your grace.
Holekhey bederekh nekhochah, tevasrem hayom seliychah, uvitefilat hashachar, hametziyem chinekha. Yah shema eveyoneykha, hamechaliym paneykha. Aviynu levaneykha, al taelem azeneykha.
הֽוֹלְכֵֽי בְּדֶֽרֶךְ נְכוֹחָֽה, תְּבַשְּׂרֵם הַיּוֹם סְלִיחָה, וּבִתְפִלַּת הַשַּֽׁחַר, הַמְצִיאֵם חִנֶּֽךָ. יָהּ שְׁמַע אֶבְיוֹנֶֽיךָ, הַמְחַלִּים פָּנֶֽיךָ. אָבִֽינוּ לְבָנֶֽיךָ, אַל תַּעְלֵם אָזְנֶֽיךָ.
21 Im Afes אִם אָפֵֽס רֹֽבַע הַקֵּן
פיוט לרבי אפרים ברבי יצחק מרגנשבורג (וסימנו "אפרים בר רב יצחק חזק"(+.
A piyyut by Rabbi Ephraim son of Rabbi Yitzchak of Regensburg (its acrostic spells "Ephraim bar Rav Yitzchak chazak").
If the nest is depleted, if the tent of the Presence is emptied, please let us not be lost, for we have an aged Father.
Im afes rova haken, ohel shiken im riken, al-na novedah kiy al ken, yesh-lanu av zaken.
אִם אָפֵֽס רֹֽבַע הַקֵּן, אֹֽהֶל שִׁכֵּן אִם רִקֵּן, אַל־נָא נֹֽאבְדָֽה כִּי עַל כֵּן, יֶשׁ־לָֽנוּ אָב זָקֵן.
You will recognize His face, and recall His righteousness before You. Accept His precious son as an offering.
Paniym lo takiyr, vetzideko lefaneykha nazekiyr, kach-na ben yakiyr, venimetzah damo al kiyr.
פָּנִים לוֹ תַּכִּיר, וְצִדְקוֹ לְפָנֶֽיךָ נַזְכִּיר, קַח־נָא בֵּן יַקִּיר, וְנִמְצָה דָמוֹ עַל קִיר.
He ran to the youth to sanctify him, and his soul was bound to his soul. He adorned him with wood and fire; a crown of glory and splendor was upon his head.
Ratz el hanaar lehakediysho, venafesho keshurah venafesho, itero vaetziym veisho, nezer elohayv al rosho.
רָץ אֶל הַנַּֽעַר לְהַקְדִּֽישׁוֹ, וְנַפְשׁוֹ קְשׁוּרָה בְנַפְשׁוֹ, עִטְּרוֹ בָעֵצִים וְאִשּׁוֹ, נֵֽזֶר אֱלֹהָיו עַל רֹאשׁוֹ.
The only son was light as a gazelle. He answered and said: 'My father, here is the fire and the wood we bring, but where is the lamb for the offering?'
Yachiyd hukal katzeviy, anah veamar aviy, hineh haesh vehaetziym naviy, uteshurah eyn lehaviy.
יָחִיד הוּקַל כַּצְּבִי, עָנָה וְאָמַר אָבִֽי, הִנֵּה הָאֵשׁ וְהָעֵצִים נָבִיא, וּתְשׁוּרָה אֵין לְהָבִיא.
Words he answered so as not to alarm him, and he replied to him: 'My son, God will provide for Himself.' And He made known that the Lord our God is a consuming fire.
Miliym heshiyvo milehavehiylo, vayaan vayomer lo. Beniy, elohiym ireeh-lo, veyoda Adonai et asher lo.
מִלִּים הֱשִׁיבוֹ מִלְּהַבְהִילוֹ, וַיַּֽעַן וַיֹּֽאמֶר לֽוֹ. בְּנִי, אֱלֹהִים יִרְאֶה־לּוֹ, וְיוֹדַע יְהֹוָה אֶת אֲשֶׁר לוֹ.
In Your commandment they were both careful, and after You they did not doubt. They hurried and went swiftly, upon the mountain that God had said.
Bemitzevatekha sheneyhem nizehariym, veachareykha lo mehareheriym, chashu vehalekhu nimehariym, al achad hehariym.
בְּמִצְוָֽתְךָֽ שְׁנֵיהֶם נִזְהָרִים, וְאַחֲרֶֽיךָ לֹא מְהַרְהְרִים, חָֽשׁוּ וְהָֽלְכֽוּ נִמְהָרִים, עַל אַחַד הֶהָרִים.
They saw a lofty mound and hastened with the wood of the offering. Together in perfect love, they made a straight highway in the Arabah.
Rau ed telulah, miharu atzey olah, yachad beahavah khelulah, yashru baaravah mesilah.
רָאוּ אֵד תְּלוּלָה, מִהֲרוּ עֲצֵי עוֹלָה, יַֽחַד בְּאַהֲבָה כְלוּלָה, יַשְּׁרוּ בָּעֲרָבָה מְסִלָּה.
The only son saw that he was the one. The tested one spoke to his father: 'Father, make me like a lamb. Do not let your heart weaken.'
Raah yachiyd kiy hu haseh, naam lehoro hamenuseh, aviy otiy kakeves taaseh, lo tachemol velo tekhaseh.
רָאָה יָחִיד כִּי הוּא הַשֶּׂה, נָאַם לְהוֹרוֹ הַמְּנֻסֶּה, אָבִי אוֹתִי כַּכֶּֽבֶשׂ תַּעֲשֶׂה, לֹא תַחְמֹל וְלֹא תְכַסֶּה.
'In me He desires and longs; my heart is His to reveal. If You prevent me, in the end, my spirit and soul shall ascend to Him.'
Biy chafetz venikhesof, levaviy lo lachasof, im timenaeniy sof, ruchiy venishematiy elayv yeesof.
בִּי חָפֵץ וְנִכְסֹף, לְבָבִי לוֹ לַחֲשֹׂף, אִם תִּמְנָעֵֽנִי סוֹף, רוּחִי וְנִשְׁמָתִי אֵלָיו יֶאֱסֹף.
His hands and his feet he bound, and his sword upon him he appointed. He hastened to place him on the wood, and the fire consumed upon the altar.
Yadayv veragelayv akad, vecharebo alayv pakad, lesumo al haetziym shakad, vehaesh al-hamizebecha tukad.
יָדָיו וְרַגְלָיו עָקַד, וְחַרְבּוֹ עָלָיו פָּקַד, לְשׂוּמוֹ עַל הָעֵצִים שָׁקַד, וְהָאֵשׁ עַל־הַמִּזְבֵּחַ תּוּקַד.
He stretched forth his neck of his own will, and his father drew near to him to slaughter him for the sake of his Master. And behold, the Lord appeared and called from heaven.
Tzavar pashat meelayv, veaviyv nigash elayv, leshochato leshem bealayv, vehineh Adonai nitzav alayv.
צַוָּאר פָּשַׁט מֵאֵלָיו, וְאָבִיו נִגַּשׁ אֵלָיו, לְשֹׁחֲטוֹ לְשֵׁם בְּעָלָיו, וְהִנֵּה יְהֹוָה נִצָּב עָלָיו.
Examine all that he did: the father did not spare his son, and his heart he lifted to heaven. And he saw, and behold, a ram caught in the thicket by its horns.
Chakor et kal-asher asah, haav al beno lo chasah, velibo el kapaim nasa, vayare elohiym et kal-asher asah.
חֲקֹר אֶת כָּל־אֲשֶׁר עָשָׂה, הָאָב עַל בְּנוֹ לֹא חָסָה, וְלִבּוֹ אֶל כַּפַּֽיִם נָשָׂא, וַיַּֽרְא אֱלֹהִים אֶת כָּל־אֲשֶׁר עָשָׂה.
He called from the womb, from the dawn: 'In place of your chosen son, behold, a ram.' And do, do not delay.
Kara merechem mishechar, temur binekha hanivechar, vehineh ail achar, vaaseh al teachar.
קָרָא מֵרֶֽחֶם מִשְׁחָר, תְּמוּר בִּנְךָ הַנִּבְחָר, וְהִנֵּה אַֽיִל אַחַר, וַעֲשֵׂה אַל תְּאַחַר.
In place of his memorial offering, it shall be established as his incense, and its smoke shall ascend to You. And it shall be he and his substitute, both holy to the Lord.
Chaliyfey azekarato, tikon kehaketarato, vetaaleh lekha timerato, vehayah hu utemurato.
חֲלִיפֵי אַזְכָּרָתוֹ, תִּכּוֹן כְּהַקְטָרָתוֹ, וְתַעֲלֶה לְךָ תִּמְרָתוֹ, וְהָיָה הוּא וּתְמוּרָתוֹ.
22 Zikaron Lefanekha זִכָּרוֹן לְפָנֶֽיךָ בַּשַּֽׁחַק
A remembrance before You in the heavens, forever etched in the book: an eternal covenant never to be erased, of Abraham and of Isaac.
Zikaron lefaneykha bashachak, laad basefer yuchak, beriyt olam bal yumechak, et averaham veet itzechak.
זִכָּרוֹן לְפָנֶֽיךָ בַּשַּֽׁחַק, לָעַד בַּסֵּֽפֶר יוּחַק, בְּרִית עוֹלָם בַּל יֻמְחַק, אֶת אַבְרָהָם וְאֶת יִצְחָק.
Those who call You come to bow down. In distress, look upon the binding. Your flock, in mercy, remember.
Koreeykha baiym lakod, betzarah akedah tishekod, vetzonekha berachamiym tifekod, peney hatzon el akod.
קֽוֹרְאֶֽיךָ בָּאִים לָקוֹד, בְּצָרָה עֲקֵדָה תִשְׁקֹד, וְצֹאנְךָ בְּרַחֲמִים תִּפְקֹד, פְּנֵי הַצֹּאן אֶל עָקוֹד.
Awaken Your might to rouse the sleeping. For Your sake, redeem those who tremble. Strengthen Your beloved.
Orerah gevuratekha lehakiytz niredamiym, lemaanekha tifedeh charediym nidehamiym, lehameshiykh rachameykha vachasadeykha mishemey meromiym, el melekh yoshev al kise rachamiym.
עֽוֹרְרָֽה גְבוּרָֽתְךָֽ לְהָקִיץ נִרְדָּמִים, לְמַעַנְךָ תִּפְדֶּה חֲרֵדִים נִדְהָמִים, לְהַמְשִׁיךְ רַחֲמֶֽיךָ וַחֲסָדֶֽיךָ מִשְּׁמֵי מְרוֹמִים, אֵל מֶֽלֶךְ יוֹשֵׁב עַל כִּסֵּא רַחֲמִים.
The Thirteen Attributes of Mercy (י״ג מידות) are recited aloud by the congregation together.
God, King, who sits upon a throne of mercy, and acts with loving-kindness, forgiving the iniquities of His people, removing them one by one. Increasing forgiveness for sinners and pardon for transgressors. Dealing righteously with all flesh and spirit; not according to their evil does He requite them. God, You have taught us to recite the thirteen attributes; remember for us today the covenant of the thirteen attributes, as You made known of old to the humble one, and so it is written in Your Torah: And the Lord descended in the cloud and stood with him there, and proclaimed by the Name of the Lord. And there it is said:
El melekh yoshev al kise rachamiym, umitenaheg bachasiydut, mochel avonot amo, maaviyr rishon rishon. Marebeh mechiylah lachataiym, useliychah laposheiym. Oseh tzedakot im kal-basar verucha, lo kheraatam lahem gomel. El, horetanu lomar midot shelosh-esereh, zekhor lanu hayom beriyt shelosh-esereh, kemo shehodaeta leanav mikedem, vekhen katuv betoratakh: Vayered Adonai beanan vaiteyatzev imo sham vaikera veshem Adonai : Vesham neemar:
אֵֽל מֶֽלֶךְ יוֹשֵׁב עַל כִּסֵּא רַחֲמִים, וּמִתְנַהֵג בַּחֲסִידוּת, מוֹחֵל עֲוֹנוֹת עַמּוֹ, מַעֲבִיר רִאשׁוֹן רִאשׁוֹן. מַרְבֶּה מְחִילָה לַחַטָּאִֽים, וּסְלִיחָה לַפּֽוֹשְׁעִֽים. עוֹשֵׂה צְדָקוֹת עִם כָּל־בָּשָׂר וְרֽוּחַ, לֹא כְרָעָתָם לָהֶם גּוֹמֵל. אֵֽל, הוֹרֵתָֽנוּ לוֹמַר מִדּוֹת שְׁלֹשׁ־עֶשְׂרֵה, זְכֹר לָֽנוּ הַיּוֹם בְּרִית שְׁלֹשׁ־עֶשְׂרֵה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוֹדַֽעְתָּ לֶעָנָו מִקֶּֽדֶם, וְכֵן כָּתוּב בְּתוֹרָתָךְ: וַיֵּרֶד יְהוָֹה בֶּֽעָנָן וַיִּתְיַצֵּב עִמּוֹ שָׁם וַיִּקְרָא בְשֵׁם יְהוָֹֽה : וְשָׁם נֶאֱמַר:
And the Lord passed before him and proclaimed: The Lord, the Lord, God, compassionate and gracious, slow to anger, and abundant in kindness and truth. Preserving kindness for thousands of generations, forgiving iniquity, transgression, and sin, and He cleanses.
Vayaavor Adonai al-panayv vaikera Adonai Adonai el rachum vechanun erekh apaim verav-chesed veemet: Notzer chesed laalafiym nose avon vafesha vechataah venakeh.
וַיַּֽעֲבֹר יְהֹוָה | עַל־פָּנָיו וַיִּקְרָא יְהֹוָה | יְהֹוָה אֵל רַחוּם וְחַנּוּן אֶרֶךְ אַפַּיִם וְרַב־חֶסֶד וֶֽאֱמֶֽת: נֹצֵר חֶסֶד לָֽאֲלָפִים נֹשֵׂא עָוֹן וָפֶשַׁע וְחַטָּאָה וְנַקֵּֽה.
And forgive our iniquity and our sin, and take us as Your heritage.
Vesalacheta laavonenu ulechatatenu unechaletanu:
וְסָֽלַחְתָּ לַֽעֲוֹנֵנוּ וּלְחַטָּאתֵנוּ וּנְחַלְתָּֽנוּ:
23 Bezochri בְּזָכְרִי עַל מִשְׁכָּבִֽי
מנהג מרן הראש"ל שליט"א שלא לומר פיוטים אלו, אלא אומר מיד "רחום וחנון". וכן מנהג יוצאי בבל. ומנהג יוצאי חאלב לומר פיוט "למתדוה", ואחר כך אומרים "חצות לילה", שהוא הקטע האחרון של פיוט "בזכרי". ומנהג יוצאי מרוקו לומר רק פיוט "בזכרי".
The custom of Maran the Rishon LeTzion, may he live long, is not to recite these piyyutim, but to proceed directly to "Rachum VeChanun." This is also the custom of those from Babylonia. Those from Aleppo recite "Lemitvaddeh," then "Chatzot Laylah" (the final section of "Bezochri"). Those from Morocco recite only "Bezochri."
When I remember upon my bed, the willful sins of my heart. I rise and come to the house of my God. And I say as I lift my eyes: Let us fall into the hand of the Lord, for His mercies are great.
Bezakheriy al mishekaviy, zedon libiy vaashamayv. Akumah veavoah, el beyt Elohai vahadomayv. Vaomar benaseiy ain, betachanunay eley shamayv. Nipelah na veyad Adonai kiy rabiym rachamayv.
בְּזָכְרִי עַל מִשְׁכָּבִֽי, זְדוֹן לִבִּי וַאֲשָׁמָיו. אָקֽוּמָה וְאָבֽוֹאָה, אֶל בֵּית אֱלֹהַי וַהֲדוֹמָיו. וָאֹמַר בְּנָשְׂאִי עַֽיִן, בְּתַחֲנוּנַי אֱלֵי שָׁמָיו. נִפְּלָה נָּא בְיַד יְהֹוָה כִּי רַבִּים רַחֲמָיו.
To You, my God, Rock of my strength, my refuge in distress. In You is my hope and my trust. Let us fall into the hand of the Lord, for His mercies are great.
Lekha eliy tzur cheyliy, menusatiy betzaratiy. Bekha siveriy vetikevatiy, eyalutiy begalutiy. Lekha kal-mishealot libiy, venegedekha kal-taavatiy. Pedeh eved lekha tzoek, miyad rodayv vekamayv. Nipelah na veyad Adonai kiy rabiym rachamayv.
לְךָ אֵלִי צוּר חֵילִֽי, מְנוּסָתִי בְּצָרָתִי. בְּךָ שִׂבְרִי וְתִקְוָתִֽי, אֱיָלוּתִי בְּגָלוּתִֽי. לְךָ כָּל־מִשְׁאֲלוֹת לִבִּי, וְנֶגְדְּךָ כָּל־תַּאֲוָתִי. פְּדֵה עֶֽבֶד לְךָ צוֹעֵֽק, מִיַּד רוֹדָיו וְקָמָיו. נִפְּלָה נָּא בְיַד יְהֹוָה כִּי רַבִּים רַחֲמָיו.
Answer me, O Lord, answer me, when I call from the straits. Let it be known among the peoples that Your hand is not too short to save. Let us fall into the hand of the Lord, for His mercies are great.
Aneniy Adonai aneniy, bekareiy min hametzar. Veivada baamiym, kiy yadekha lo tiketzar. Veal tivezeh enut aniy, tzoek mitigerat tzar. Asher peshaayv lekha modeh, umitevadeh al alumayv. Nipelah na veyad Adonai kiy rabiym rachamayv.
עֲנֵֽנִי יְהֹוָה עֲנֵֽנִי, בְּקָרְאִי מִן הַמֵּצַֽר. וְיִוָּדַע בָּעַמִּים, כִּי יָֽדְךָֽ לֹא תִקְצַר. וְאַל תִּבְזֶה עֱנוּת עָֽנִי, צוֹעֵק מִתִּגְרַת צַר. אֲשֶׁר פְּשָׁעָיו לְךָ מוֹדֶֽה, וּמִתְוַדֶּה עַל עֲלוּמָיו. נִפְּלָה נָּא בְיַד יְהֹוָה כִּי רַבִּים רַחֲמָיו.
Why should a man complain and say, why should he speak and justify himself? A creature of clay, whose body shall return to dust. What is his advantage and what is his gain? Let us fall into the hand of the Lord, for His mercies are great.
Mah-iteonen veyomar, mah-yedaber veitzetadak. Yetziyr chomer asher tashuv, geviyato keavak dak. Mah-iten lekha haadam, kiy iresha vekhiy itzedak. Halo milayv umifealayv, ketuviym besefer yamayv. Nipelah na veyad Adonai kiy rabiym rachamayv.
מַה־יִּתְאוֹנֵן וְיֹאמַֽר, מַה־יְּדַבֵּר וְיִצְטַדָּֽק. יְצִיר חֹֽמֶר אֲשֶׁר תָּשׁוּב, גְּוִיָּתוֹ כְּאָבָק דַּק. מַה־יִּתֵּן לְךָ הָאָדָם, כִּי יִרְשַׁע וְכִי יִצְדַּק. הֲלֹא מִלָּיו וּמִפְעָלָיו, כְּתוּבִים בְּסֵֽפֶר יָמָיו. נִפְּלָה נָּא בְיַד יְהֹוָה כִּי רַבִּים רַחֲמָיו.
At midnight they arose for You, Your servants in their praise. The merit of the fathers, remember for them, and do not turn to their wickedness. Let us fall into the hand of the Lord, for His mercies are great.
Chatzot layelah lekha kamu, avadeykha bemahalalam. Zekhut avot lahem tizekor, veal tefen lemaalalam. Keneh adatekha kiymey kedem, kedosh yaakov goalam. Vehinase hael, oseh hashalom bimeromayv. Nipelah na veyad Adonai kiy rabiym rachamayv.
חֲצוֹת לַֽיְלָה לְךָ קָֽמוּ, עֲבָדֶֽיךָ בְּמַהֲלָלָם. זְכוּת אָבוֹת לָהֶם תִּזְכֹּר, וְאַל תֵּֽפֶן לְמַעֲלָלָם. קְנֵה עֲדָֽתְךָֽ כִּֽימֵי קֶֽדֶם, קְדוֹשׁ יַעֲקֹב גֹּאֲלָֽם. וְהִנָּשֵׂא הָאֵל, עוֹשֶׂה הַשָּׁלוֹם בִּמְרוֹמָיו. נִפְּלָה נָּא בְיַד יְהֹוָה כִּי רַבִּים רַחֲמָיו.
24 LeDavid (Psalm 25) לְדָוִד אֵלֶיךָ
Compassionate and gracious One, I have sinned before You. O Lord, full of mercy, have mercy upon me and accept my supplications.
Rachum vechanun chatanu lefaneykha rachem aleynu vehoshiyenu.
רַחוּם וְחַנּוּן חָטָֽאנוּ לְפָנֶֽיךָ רַחֵם עָלֵֽינוּ וְהוֹשִׁיעֵֽנוּ.
Of David: To You, O Lord, I lift my soul. My God, in You I trust; let me not be put to shame. Indeed, none who hope in You shall be ashamed; those who deal treacherously shall be shamed. Show me Your ways, O Lord; teach me Your paths. Guide me in Your truth and teach me. Remember Your mercies, O Lord, and Your kindnesses, for they are eternal. Do not remember the sins of my youth. Good and upright is the Lord; therefore He instructs sinners in the way. Who is the man who fears the Lord? He will instruct him in the way. Turn to me and be gracious to me. See my affliction and forgive all my sins. Guard my soul and rescue me; let me not be put to shame. Redeem Israel, O God, from all its troubles.
Ledavid eleykha Adonai nafeshiy esa: Elohai bekha batachtiy al-evoshah al-yaaletzu oyevay liy: Gam kal-kveykha lo yevoshu yevoshu habogediym reykam: Derakheykha Adonai hodiyeniy orechoteykha lamedeniy: Haderiykheniy vaamitekha velamedeniy kiy-atah elohey isheiy otekha kiviytiy kal-hayom: Zekhor-rachameykha Adonai vachasadeykha kiy meolam hemah: Chatovt neuray ufeshaay al-tizekor kechasedekha zekhar-liy-atah lemaan tuvekha Adonai: Tov-veyashar Adonai al-ken yoreh chataiym badarekh: Yaderekh anaviym bamishepat viylamed anaviym dareko: Kal-arechot Adonai chesed veemet lenotzerey veriyto veedotayv: Lemaan-shimekha Adonai vesalacheta laavoniy kiy rav-hu: Miy-zeh haiysh yere Adonai yorenu bederekh ivechar: Nafesho betov taliyn vezar-o iyrash aretz: Sod Adonai liyreayv uveriyto lehodiyam: Eynay tamiyd el-Adonai kiy hu-yotziy mereshet ragelay: Peneh-elay vechaneniy kiy-yachiyd veaniy aniy: Tzarot levaviy hirechiyvu mimetzukotay hotziyeniy: Reeh aneiy vaamaliy vesho lekhal-chatovtay: Reeh-oyevay kiy-rabu vesineat chamas seneuniy: Shamerah nafeshiy vehatziyleniy al-evosh kiy-chasiytiy vakh: Tom-vayosher itzeruniy kiy kiviytiykha: Pedeh elohiym et-iserael mikol tzarotayv: Vehu ifedeh et-iserael mikol avonotayv:
לְדָוִד אֵלֶיךָ יְהֹוָה נַפְשִׁי אֶשָֹּֽׂא: אֱֽלֹהַי בְּךָ בָּטַחְתִּי אַל־אֵבוֹשָׁה אַל־יַֽעַלְצוּ אֽוֹיְבַי לִֽי: גַּם כָּל־קוֶֹיךָ לֹא יֵבֹשׁוּ יֵבֹשׁוּ הַבּֽוֹגְדִים רֵיקָֽם: דְּרָכֶיךָ יְהֹוָה הֽוֹדִיעֵנִי אֹרְחוֹתֶיךָ לַמְּדֵֽנִי: הַדְרִיכֵנִי בַֽאֲמִתֶּךָ | וְֽלַמְּדֵנִי כִּֽי־אַתָּה אֱלֹהֵי יִשְׁעִי אֽוֹתְךָ קִוִּיתִי כָּל־הַיּֽוֹם: זְכֹֽר־רַֽחֲמֶיךָ יְהֹוָה וַֽחֲסָדֶיךָ כִּי מֵֽעוֹלָם הֵֽמָּה: חַטֹּאות נְעוּרַי | וּפְשָׁעַי אַל־תִּזְכֹּר כְּחַסְדְּךָ זְכָר־לִי־אַתָּה לְמַעַן טֽוּבְךָ יְהֹוָֽה: טֽוֹב־וְיָשָׁר יְהֹוָה עַל־כֵּן יוֹרֶה חַטָּאִים בַּדָּֽרֶךְ: יַדְרֵךְ עֲנָוִים בַּמִּשְׁפָּט וִֽילַמֵּד עֲנָוִים דַּרְכּֽוֹ: כָּל־אָרְחוֹת יְהֹוָה חֶסֶד וֶֽאֱמֶת לְנֹֽצְרֵי בְרִיתוֹ וְעֵֽדֹתָֽיו: לְמַֽעַן־שִׁמְךָ יְהֹוָה וְֽסָלַחְתָּ לַֽעֲוֹנִי כִּי רַב־הֽוּא: מִי־זֶה הָאִישׁ יְרֵא יְהֹוָה יוֹרֶנּוּ בְּדֶרֶךְ יִבְחָֽר: נַפְשׁוֹ בְּטוֹב תָּלִין וְזַרְעוֹ יִירַשׁ אָֽרֶץ: סוֹד יְהֹוָה לִֽירֵאָיו וּבְרִיתוֹ לְהֽוֹדִיעָֽם: עֵינַי תָּמִיד אֶל־יְהֹוָה כִּי הֽוּא־יוֹצִיא מֵרֶשֶׁת רַגְלָֽי: פְּנֵֽה־אֵלַי וְחָנֵּנִי כִּֽי־יָחִיד וְעָנִי אָֽנִי: צָרוֹת לְבָבִי הִרְחִיבוּ מִמְּצֽוּקוֹתַי הֽוֹצִיאֵֽנִי: רְאֵה עָנְיִי וַֽעֲמָלִי וְשָֹא לְכָל־חַטֹּאותָֽי: רְאֵֽה־אֹֽיְבַי כִּי־רָבּוּ וְשִׂנְאַת חָמָס שְׂנֵאֽוּנִי: שָׁמְרָה נַפְשִׁי וְהַצִּילֵנִי אַל־אֵבוֹשׁ כִּֽי־חָסִיתִי בָֽךְ: תֹּֽם־וָיֹשֶׁר יִצְּרוּנִי כִּי קִוִּיתִֽיךָ: פְּדֵה אֱלֹהִים אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵל מִכֹּל צָֽרוֹתָֽיו: וְהוּא יִפְדֶּה אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵל מִכֹּל עֲוֹֽנֹתָֽיו:
O Lord, God of Israel, turn from Your fierce anger and relent concerning the evil against Your people.
Adonai elohey iserael, shuv mecharon apekha vehinachem al-haraah leamekha:
יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל, שׁוּב מֵֽחֲרוֹן אַפֶּךָ וְהִנָּחֵם עַל־הָֽרָעָה לְעַמֶּֽךָ:
25 Daily Supplications תחינות דבר יום ביומו
מנהג מרן הראש"ל שליט"א להמשיך "אתאנו לחלות פניך". וכן מנהג יוצאי בבל. ויש נוהגים לומר סדר תחינות דבר יום ביומו, אשר נתחברו על ידי גדולי הדורות וכן מנהג יוצאי חאלב ודמשק ומרוקו.
The custom of Maran the Rishon LeTzion, may he live long, is to continue with "Atanu Lechalot Panekha." This is also the custom of those from Babylonia. Some have the custom to recite daily supplications, each corresponding to a day of the week, composed by the great sages of the generations. This is the custom of those from Aleppo, Damascus, and Morocco.
תחינה ליום ראשון (סימן שמואל חזק(+.
Supplication for Sunday (acrostic: Shmuel chazak).
O Lord, hearken to the cry of Your people, and perform with us a sign for good; and who is like Your people Israel, a unique nation. Deliver them from between the teeth of lions, and gather them from the four winds, and bring them near, one to another. And comfort the mourners of Zion, and have mercy on wandering Oholibah, to join the tent that it may be one. Edom and Moab have thirsted in the pit for my life; with disgrace they have struck my cheeks, one from this side and one from that. At my feet they have hidden snare and pitfall, and they have whispered against me together; there is none who does good, not even one. Strengthen Your people, O Dweller of the heavens of the earth, and may Your enemies be for destruction and ruin; and the Lord shall be King over all the earth; on that day the Lord shall be one and His Name one.
Adonai shaveat amekha hakeshiyvah, vaaseh imanu ot letovah, umiy keamekha iserael goy echad. Mibeyn shiney arayot techaletzem, umeareba ruchot tekabetzem, vekarev otam echad el echad. Veet aveley tziyon tenachem, veet aholiyvah hanedudah terachem, lechaber et haohel liheyot echad. Edom umoav tzametu vabor chayay, becherepah hiku lechayay, mizeh echad umizeh echad. Leragelay tamenu pach vafachat, vaitelachashu alay yachad, eyn oseh-tov eyn gam echad. Chazek amekha shokhen shemey eretz, veoyeveykha iheyu lekhalah vacheretz, vehayah Adonai lemelekh al-kal-haaretz, bayom hahu iheyeh Adonai echad ushemo echad.
יְהֹוָה שַׁוְעַת עַמְּךָ הַקְשִֽׁיבָה, וַעֲשֵׂה עִמָּֽנוּ אוֹת לְטוֹבָה, וּמִי כְּעַמְּךָ יִשְׂרָאֵל גּוֹי אֶחָד. מִבֵּין שִׁנֵּי אֲרָיוֹת תְּחַלְּצֵם, וּמֵאַרְבַּֽע רוּחוֹת תְּקַבְּצֵם, וְקָרֵב אוֹתָם אֶחָד אֶל אֶחָד. וְאֶת אֲבֵלֵי צִיּוֹן תְּנַחֵם, וְאֶת אָהֳלִיבָֽה הַנְּדוּדָה תְּרַחֵם, לְחַבֵּר אֶת הָאֹֽהֶל לִהְיוֹת אֶחָד. אֱדוֹם וּמוֹאָב צָֽמְתֽוּ בַבּוֹר חַיַּי, בְּחֶרְפָּה הִכּוּ לְחָיַי, מִזֶּה אֶחָד וּמִזֶּה אֶחָד. לְרַגְלַי טָֽמְנֽוּ פַּח וָפַֽחַת, וַיִּתְלַחֲשׁוּ עָלַי יַֽחַד, אֵין עֹֽשֵׂה־טֽוֹב אֵין גַּם אֶחָד. חַזֵּק עַמְּךָ שׁוֹכֵן שְׁמֵי עֶֽרֶץ, וְאֽוֹיְבֶֽיךָ יִהְיוּ לְכָלָה וָחֶֽרֶץ, וְהָיָה יְהֹוָה לְמֶֽלֶךְ עַל־כָּל־הָאָֽרֶץ, בַּיּוֹם הַהוּא יִהְיֶה יְהֹוָה אֶחָד וּשְׁמוֹ אֶחָד.
תחינה ליום שני (סימן יהודה חזק(+.
Supplication for Monday (acrostic: Yehuda chazak).
O Lord, Unique One who searches the hearts of all humankind, who does great things beyond searching—and there was evening and there was morning, a second day. Attend to the speech of Your servant and his utterance, who bears exile upon his shoulder, and on the day of his falling, raise him up; there is One and there is no second. And remember the covenant of Your beloved ones, for a people crying out before You, the children of Jacob Your servants—and He commanded also the second. My Beloved, attend to the supplications, and reckon their prayers as portions, by the merit of those who offer offerings—Nethanel son of Zuar on the second day. Incline Your ear to the prayer of Your people, and send us the anointed of Your righteousness, that we may offer offerings before You—one lamb in the morning and the second lamb. Strengthen my heart, my King, and hasten to illuminate my darkness; if my way is hidden, prosper, pray, Your servant on the second day.
Adonai yachiyd libot kal-beney adam choker, oseh gedolot ad eyn cheker, vayehiy erev vayehiy voker yom sheniy. Hakeshev siychat avedekha uneumo, sovel galut al shikhemo, veyom nafelo lahakiymo, yesh echad veeyn sheniy. Uzekhor beriyt yediydeykha, leam tzoakiym negedekha, beney yaakov avadeykha, vayetzav gam et hasheniy. Dodiy hakeshev techinot, vachashov tefilotam kemanot, bizekhut makeriyviym karebanot, netaneel ben tzuar bayom hasheniy. Hat azenekha litefilat amekha, ushelach lanu meshiycha tzidekekha, venakeriyv karebanot lefaneykha, et hakeves echad baboker veet hakeves hasheniy. Chazek libiy malekiy, umaher lehaiyr cheshekiy, im niseterah darekiy, hatzeliychah na leavedekha bayom hasheniy.
יְהֹוָה יָחִיד לִבּוֹת כָּל־בְּנֵי אָדָם חוֹקֵר, עוֹשֵׂה גְדוֹלוֹת עַד אֵין חֵֽקֶר, וַיְהִי עֶֽרֶב וַיְהִי בֹֽקֶר יוֹם שֵׁנִי. הַקְשֵׁב שִׂיחַת עַבְדְּךָ וּנְאוּמוֹ, סוֹבֵל גָּלוּת עַל שִׁכְמוֹ, וְיוֹם נָפְלוֹ לַהֲקִימוֹ, יֵשׁ אֶחָד וְאֵין שֵׁנִי. וּזְכֹר בְּרִית יְדִידֶֽיךָ, לְעַם צוֹעֲקִים נֶגְדֶּֽךָ, בְּנֵי יַעֲקֹב עֲבָדֶֽיךָ, וַיְצַו גַּם אֶת הַשֵּׁנִי. דּוֹדִי הַקְשֵׁב תְּחִנּוֹת, וַחֲשֹׁב תְּפִלּוֹתָם כְּמָנוֹת, בִּזְכוּת מַקְרִיבִים קָרְבָּנוֹת, נְתַנְאֵל בֶּן צוּעָר בַּיּוֹם הַשֵּׁנִי. הַט אָזְנְךָ לִתְפִלַּת עַמֶּֽךָ, וּשְׁלַח לָֽנוּ מְשִׁיחַ צִדְקֶֽךָ, וְנַקְרִיב קָרְבָּנוֹת לְפָנֶֽיךָ, אֶת הַכֶּֽבֶשׂ אֶחָד בַּבֹּֽקֶר וְאֶת הַכֶּֽבֶשׂ הַשֵּׁנִי. חַזֵּק לִבִּי מַלְכִּי, וּמַהֵר לְהָאִיר חֶשְׁכִּי, אִם נִסְתְּרָה דַּרְכִּי, הַצְלִֽיחָה נָּא לְעַבְדְּךָ בַּיּוֹם הַשֵּׁנִי.
תחינה ליום שלישי (סימן דויד בר אלעזר(+.
Supplication for Tuesday (acrostic: David bar Elazar).
O Lord, my eyes grow faint looking upward; in my distress I call, O Lord. And my spirit is overwhelmed within me from the abundance of my anger and my complaint, and I pour out my soul before the Lord. Yah, attend to my utterance, and perform a sign for good with me; many things have You done, You, O Lord. I am brought low, and He will save me—righteous God and Savior; for Your salvation I have hoped, O Lord. When my enemies rise against me, my heart shall not fear, and I have trusted in You, O Lord. My hand is raised in You, my stronghold, and not by my sword—for not by sword and spear does the Lord save. My enemies have prevailed, and they have vaunted themselves over me; will You restrain Yourself over these, O Lord? Why do You hide Your face, that the enemy should crown himself over me, O jealous and avenging God, O Lord? I will set counsels in my soul, and with my heart I will murmur: Will the Lord cast off forever? The arrogant contend with me; my thoughts answer me back—for the Lord will not cast off forever. I have hoped for Your mercies, for in You, O Lord, I have waited; You will answer, O Lord my God.
Adonai dalu eynay lamarom, batzar-liy ekera Adonai. Vatiteatef alay ruchiy, merov kaesiy vesiychiy, vaeshepokh et nafeshiy lifeney Adonai. Yah hakeshev neumiy, vaaseh ot letovah imiy, rabot asiyta atah Adonai. Dalotiy veliy yehoshiya, el tzadiyk umoshiya, liyshuatekha kiviytiy Adonai. Bekum alay oyeviy, lo iyra libiy, vaaniy aleykha batachtiy Adonai. Ramah vekha yadiy misegabiy, velo vecharebiy, kiy-lo vecherev uvachaniyt yehoshiya Adonai. Oyevay gavaru, vealay hiteamaru, haal eleh titeapak Adonai. Lamah faneykha tasetiyr, veoyev alay yakhetiyr, el kano venokem Adonai. Etzot benafeshiy asiymah, veim levaviy ahiymah, haleolamiym izenach Adonai. Zediym yeriyvuniy, seiyfay yeshiyvuniy, kiy lo izenach leolam Adonai. Rachameykha sibaretiy, kiy lekha Adonai hochaletiy, atah taaneh adonay Elohai.
יְהֹוָה דַּלּֽוּ עֵינַי לַמָּרוֹם, בַּצַּר־לִי אֶקְרָא יְהֹוָה. וַתִּתְעַטֵּף עָלַי רוּחִי, מֵרֹב כַּעְסִי וְשִׂיחִי, וָאֶשְׁפֹּךְ אֶת נַפְשִׁי לִפְנֵי יְהֹוָה. יָהּ הַקְשֵׁב נְאוּמִי, וַעֲשֵׂה אוֹת לְטוֹבָה עִמִּי, רַבּוֹת עָשִֽׂיתָ אַתָּה יְהֹוָה. דַּלּוֹתִֽי וְלִי יְהוֹשִֽׁיעַ, אֵל צַדִּיק וּמוֹשִֽׁיעַ, לִישׁוּעָֽתְךָֽ קִוִּֽיתִי יְהֹוָה. בְּקוּם עָלַי אֽוֹיְבִֽי, לֹא יִירָא לִבִּי, וַאֲנִי עָלֶֽיךָ בָּטַֽחְתִּי יְהֹוָה. רָמָֽה בְךָ יָדִי מִשְׂגַּבִּי, וְלֹא בְחַרְבִּי, כִּי־לֹא בְחֶֽרֶב וּבַחֲנִית יְהוֹשִֽׁיעַ יְהֹוָה. אֽוֹיְבַֽי גָּבָֽרוּ, וְעָלַי הִתְאַמָּֽרוּ, הַעַל אֵֽלֶּה תִּתְאַפַּק יְהֹוָה. לָֽמָּה פָנֶֽיךָ תַסְתִּיר, וְאוֹיֵב עָלַי יַכְתִּיר, אֵל קַנּוֹא וְנוֹקֵם יְהֹוָה. עֵצוֹת בְּנַפְשִׁי אָשִֽׂימָה, וְעִם לְבָבִי אָהִֽימָה, הַלְעוֹלָמִים יִזְנַח יְהֹוָה. זֵדִים יְרִיבֽוּנִי, סְעִיפַי יְשִׁיבֽוּנִי, כִּי לֹא יִזְנַח לְעוֹלָם יְהֹוָה. רַחֲמֶֽיךָ שִׂבַּֽרְתִּי, כִּי לְךָ יְהֹוָה הוֹחַֽלְתִּי, אַתָּה תַעֲנֶה אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהָי.
תחינה ליום רביעי (סימן אני דויד קטן(+.
Supplication for Wednesday (acrostic: Ani David katan).
O Lord, if my iniquity is too great to bear, and I have acted foolishly, do not enter into judgment with Your servant. Purify the afflictions of my heart, and pass over the sins of my youth; also restrain Your servant from the willful. My sorrows have prevailed, and my sighs have broken my heart; gladden the soul of Your servant. Those who seek my harm have magnified themselves against me; they rejoice at my ruin—do not hide Your face from Your servant. And how long shall I cry out with no judgment? When will You execute judgment upon my pursuers? How many are the days of Your servant? Let Your anger turn to have mercy upon me; let Your kindness be, pray, to comfort me, according to Your word to Your servant. I have recounted my ways, and upon You from the womb I have leaned; let Your face shine upon Your servant. I am too small for all the kindnesses, O Rock who settles the solitary, which You have done with Your servant. Cleanse me from my sin; do not hide Your ear from my relief, from my cry—pray, O Lord, for I am Your servant. Show me wonders; do not hide Your face from me; do not turn aside in anger against Your servant.
Adonai im gadal avoniy mineso, vehisekaletiy asoh, al tavo vemishepat et avedekha. Nigeey levaviy haver, vechatot neuray haaver, gam mizediym chasokh avedekha. Yegonotay gaveru, veanechotay libiy shaveru, samecha nefesh avedekha. Doreshey raatiy alay higediylu, lehavatiy yagiylu, al taseter paneykha meavedekha. Vead an ashavea veeyn mishepat, matay taaseh verodefay mishepat, kamah yemey avedekha. Yashuv apekha lerachameniy, yehiy na chasedekha lenachameniy, keimeratekha leavedekha. Derakhay siparetiy, vealeykha mibeten nisemakhetiy, haiyrah faneykha al avedekha. Katonetiy mikal-hachasadiym, tzur moshiyv yechiydiym, asher asiyta et avedekha. Tahareniy mechatatiy, al taelem azenekha leravechatiy leshaveatiy, ana Adonai kiy aniy avedekha. Nifelaot hareeniy, al taseter paneykha mimeniy, al tat beaf avedekha.
יְהֹוָה אִם גָּדַל עֲוֹנִי מִנְּשׂוֹא, וְהִסְכַּֽלְתִּי עֲשֹׂה, אַל תָּבוֹא בְמִשְׁפָּט אֶת עַבְדֶּֽךָ. נִגְעֵי לְבָבִי הָבֵר, וְחַטֹּאת נְעוּרַי הַעֲבֵר, גַּם מִזֵּדִים חֲשֹׂךְ עַבְדֶּֽךָ. יְגוֹנוֹתַי גָּֽבְרֽוּ, וְאַנְחוֹתַי לִבִּי שָֽׁבְרֽוּ, שַׂמֵּֽחַ נֶֽפֶשׁ עַבְדֶּֽךָ. דּֽוֹרְשֵֽׁי רָעָתִי עָלַי הִגְדִּֽילוּ, לְהַוָּתִי יָגִֽילוּ, אַל תַּסְתֵּר פָּנֶֽיךָ מֵעַבְדֶּֽךָ. וְעַד אָן אֲשַׁוֵּֽעַ וְאֵין מִשְׁפָּט, מָתַי תַּעֲשֶׂה בְרֽוֹדְפַֽי מִשְׁפָּט, כַּמָּֽה יְמֵי עַבְדֶּֽךָ. יָשׁוּב אַפְּךָ לְרַחֲמֵֽנִי, יְהִי נָא חַסְדְּךָ לְנַחֲמֵֽנִי, כְּאִמְרָֽתְךָֽ לְעַבְדֶּֽךָ. דְּרָכַי סִפַּֽרְתִּי, וְעָלֶֽיךָ מִבֶּֽטֶן נִסְמַֽכְתִּי, הָאִֽירָה פָנֶֽיךָ עַל עַבְדֶּֽךָ. קָטֹֽנְתִּי מִכָּל־הַחֲסָדִים, צוּר מוֹשִׁיב יְחִידִים, אֲשֶׁר עָשִֽׂיתָ אֶת עַבְדֶּֽךָ. טַהֲרֵֽנִי מֵחַטָּאתִי, אַל תַּעְלֵם אָזְנְךָ לְרַוְחָתִי לְשַׁוְעָתִי, אָֽנָּֽא יְהֹוָה כִּי אֲנִי עַבְדֶּֽךָ. נִפְלָאוֹת הַרְאֵֽנִי, אַל תַּסְתֵּר פָּנֶֽיךָ מִמֶּֽנִּי, אַל תַּט בְּאַף עַבְדֶּֽךָ.
תחינה ליום חמישי (סימן שמואל(+.
Supplication for Thursday (acrostic: Shmuel).
O Lord, heed the wanderer from his nest, and awaken the sleep of his eye—and he arose at midnight. Because of the shame of his guilt, his tear has become for him bread by day and by night. And it was, in his captivity, that he was driven about, and from mountain to hill he was pursued—by heat by day and by frost by night. Enemies oppress me, and against me they take counsel, and they conspire all the night. Let my prayer come before You, O Lord, God of my salvation; by day I have cried out in the night.
Adonai sheeh noded mikino, veorer shenat eyno, vayakam bachatziy halayelah. Mipeney boshet ashemato, hayetah lo dimeato, lechem yomam valayelah. Vayehiy beshiveyo nehedaf, umehar legiveah niredaf, lechorev bayom ulekerach balayelah. Oyeviym otiy ilechatzu, vealay iteyaatzu, vaitechareshu kal-halayelah. Lefaneykha tavo tefilatiy, Adonai elohey yeshuatiy, yom tzaaketiy balayelah.
יְהֹוָה שְׁעֵה נוֹדֵד מִקִּנּוֹ, וְעוֹרֵר שְׁנַת עֵינוֹ, וַיָּֽקָם בַּחֲצִי הַלָּֽיְלָה. מִפְּנֵי בֹּֽשֶׁת אַשְׁמָתוֹ, הָֽיְתָה לּוֹ דִמְעָתוֹ, לֶֽחֶם יוֹמָם וָלָֽיְלָה. וַיְהִי בְּשִׁבְיוֹ נֶהְדָּף, וּמֵהַר לְגִבְעָה נִרְדָּף, לְחֹֽרֶב בַּיּוֹם וּלְקֶֽרַח בַּלָּֽיְלָה. אֽוֹיְבִֽים אוֹתִי יִלְחָֽצוּ, וְעָלַי יִתְיָעָֽצוּ, וַיִּתְחָֽרְשֽׁוּ כָּל־הַלָּֽיְלָה. לְפָנֶֽיךָ תָּבֹא תְפִלָּתִי, יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵי יְשׁוּעָתִי, יוֹם צָעַֽקְתִּי בַּלָּֽיְלָה.
תחינה ליום ששי (סימן אברהם(+.
Supplication for Friday (acrostic: Avraham).
O Lord God, who settles the solitary, heed those who stand in the house of the Lord by nights. In You I have trusted, and Your Name I have remembered upon my bed by nights. Have mercy on him who goes and weeps, a man with his sword upon his thigh, from fear by nights. Behold, I go bowed down; I have found no rest, days as well as nights. Deliver him who cries out before You, to declare Your kindness in the morning, and Your faithfulness by nights.
Adonai elohiym moshiyv yechiydiym, sheeh leomediym, beveyt Adonai baleylot. Bekha batachtiy, veshimekha zakharetiy. Al mishekaviy baleylot. Rachem al holekh uvakhoh, iysh charebo al yerekho. Mipachad baleylot. Hineniy holekh shechocha, lo matzatiy manocha, yamiym gam leylot. Malet tzoek negedekha, lehagiyd baboker chasedekha, veemunatekha baleylot.
יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהִים מוֹשִׁיב יְחִידִים, שְׁעֵה לְעֽוֹמְדִֽים, בְּבֵית יְהֹוָה בַּלֵּילוֹת. בְּךָ בָּטַֽחְתִּי, וְשִׁמְךָ זָכַֽרְתִּי. עַל מִשְׁכָּבִי בַּלֵּילוֹת. רַחֵם עַל הוֹלֵךְ וּבָכֹה, אִישׁ חַרְבּוֹ עַל יְרֵכוֹ. מִפַּֽחַד בַּלֵּילוֹת. הִנְנִי הוֹלֵךְ שְׁחֽוֹחַ, לֹא מָצָֽאתִי מָנֽוֹחַ, יָמִים גַּם לֵילוֹת. מַלֵּט צוֹעֵק נֶגְדֶּֽךָ, לְהַגִּיד בַּבֹּֽקֶר חַסְדֶּֽךָ, וֶאֱמוּנָֽתְךָֽ בַּלֵּילוֹת.
Turn from Your fierce anger, and relent of the evil against Your people.
Shuv mecharon apekha vehinachem al-haraah leamekha:
שׁוּב מֵֽחֲרוֹן אַפֶּךָ וְהִנָּחֵם עַל־הָֽרָעָה לְעַמֶּֽךָ:
Do not be exceedingly angry, O Lord, and do not remember iniquity forever. Look, please — we are all Your people. Do not despise us for Your Name’s sake; do not disgrace the throne of Your glory. Remember, do not annul Your covenant with us. If our iniquities testify against us, O Lord, act for Your Name’s sake. For our backslidings have multiplied — we have sinned against You. Master, forgive us.
Al-tiketzof Adonai ad-meod veal-laad tizekor avon hen habet-na amekha khulanu: Al-tineatz lemaan shimekha al-tenabel kise khevodekha zekhor al-tafer beriytekha itanu: Im-avoneynu anu vanu Adonai ashoh lemaan shemekha. Kiy rabu meshuvoteynu lekha chatanu. Adon selach lanu.
אַל־תִּקְצֹף יְהֹוָה עַד־מְאֹד וְאַל־לָעַד תִּזְכֹּר עָוֹן הֵן הַבֶּט־נָא עַמְּךָ כֻלָּֽנוּ: אַל־תִּנְאַץ לְמַעַן שִׁמְךָ אַל־תְּנַבֵּל כִּסֵּא כְבוֹדֶךָ זְכֹר אַל־תָּפֵר בְּרִֽיתְךָ אִתָּֽנוּ: אִם־עֲוֹנֵינוּ עָנוּ בָנוּ יְהֹוָה עֲשֵֹה לְמַעַן שְׁמֶךָ. כִּי רַבּֽוּ מְשׁוּבוֹתֵֽינוּ לְךָ חָטָֽאנוּ. אָדוֹן סְלַח לָֽנוּ.
If we have been guilty, reddened like crimson, O Lord, send forth the abundance of Your mercies first, for the sake of the father saved from the Chaldeans, whose might was in the Valley of Siddim. Remember his love today for us, for our backslidings have multiplied — we have sinned against You; Master, forgive us.
Im ashamenu katola heediym, Adonai et hamon rachameykha takediym, lemaan av mutzal mikasediym, gibareto beemek hasidiym. Ahavato zekhor hayom lanu, kiy rabu meshuvoteynu lekha chatanu, adon selach lanu.
אִם אֲשָׁמֵֽנוּ כַּתּוֹלַע הֶאֱדִים, יְהֹוָה אֶת הֲמוֹן רַחֲמֶֽיךָ תַּקְדִּים, לְמַֽעַן אָב מֻצָּל מִכַּשְׂדִּים, גִּבַּרְתּוֹ בְּעֵֽמֶק הַשִּׂדִּים. אַהֲבָתוֹ זְכֹר הַיּוֹם לָֽנוּ, כִּי רַבּֽוּ מְשׁוּבוֹתֵֽינוּ לְךָ חָטָֽאנוּ, אָדוֹן סְלַח לָנוּ.
If we have sinned and multiplied willful rebellion, O Lord, reveal to us a time of favor, for the sake of the distinguished one who was bound like a sheep, and a ram was set as his atonement for favor. Remember his binding today for us, for our backslidings have multiplied — we have sinned against You; Master, forgive us.
Im chatanu vehirebiynu latzon, Adonai galeh lanu et ratzon, lemaan dagul haneekad katzon, veail hushat kafero leratzon. Akedato zekhor hayom lanu, kiy rabu meshuvoteynu lekha chatanu, adon selach lanu.
אִם חָטָֽאנוּ וְהִרְבִּֽינוּ לָצוֹן, יְהֹוָה גַּלֵּה לָֽנוּ עֵת רָצוֹן, לְמַֽעַן דָּגוּל הַנֶּעֱקַד כַּצֹּאן, וְאַֽיִל הוּשַׁת כָּפְרוֹ לְרָצוֹן. עֲקֵדָתוֹ זְכֹר הַיּוֹם לָֽנוּ, כִּי רַבּֽוּ מְשׁוּבוֹתֵֽינוּ לְךָ חָטָֽאנוּ, אָדוֹן סְלַח לָֽנוּ.
If we have multiplied crime and transgression, O Lord, be favorable through our prayer, for the sake of the wholehearted one who beheld a ladder and was afraid, and lodged in the place that is awesome. Remember his integrity today for us, for our backslidings have multiplied — we have sinned against You; Master, forgive us.
Im hirebiynu pesha vaaverah, Adonai hiteratzeh baatiyrah, lemaan tam chazah sulam vaiyra, velan bemakom mah-nora. Tumato zekhor hayom lanu, kiy rabu meshuvoteynu lekha chatanu, adon selach lanu.
אִם הִרְבִּֽינוּ פֶּֽשַׁע וַעֲבֵרָה, יְהֹוָה הִתְרַצֵּה בַּעֲתִירָה, לְמַֽעַן תָּם חָזָה סֻלָּם וַיִּֽירָא, וְלָן בְּמָקוֹם מַה־נּוֹרָא. תֻּמָּתוֹ זְכֹר הַיּוֹם לָֽנוּ, כִּי רַבּֽוּ מְשׁוּבוֹתֵֽינוּ לְךָ חָטָֽאנוּ, אָדוֹן סְלַח לָֽנוּ.
26 Atanu Lechalot אָתָֽאנוּ לְחַלּוֹת פָּנֶֽיךָ
We have come to beseech Your countenance, for kindness and truth go before Your face. Please do not put us to shame; do not send us away empty. Forgive us and send us salvation and mercy from Your dwelling.
Atanu lechalot paneykha, kiy chesed veemet yekademu faneykha. Na al teviyshenu, na al teshiyvenu reykam milefaneykha. Selach lanu, ushelach lanu, yeshuah verachamiym mimeonekha.
אָתָֽאנוּ לְחַלּוֹת פָּנֶֽיךָ, כִּי חֶֽסֶד וֶאֱמֶת יְקַדְּמוּ פָנֶֽיךָ. נָא אַל תְּבִישֵֽׁנוּ, נָא אַל תְּשִׁיבֵֽנוּ רֵיקָם מִלְּפָנֶֽיךָ. סְלַח לָֽנוּ, וּשְׁלַח לָֽנוּ, יְשׁוּעָה וְרַחֲמִים מִמְּעוֹנֶֽךָ.
We have come to seek atonement from You, O awesome and fearful One, stronghold in times of trouble.
Atanu levakesh mimekha kaparah, ayom venora, misegav leitot batzarah. Techayeynu, techanenu, uveshimekha nikera. Selach lanu, ushelach lanu, yeshuah verachamiym mimeonekha.
אָתָֽאנוּ לְבַקֵּשׁ מִמְּךָ כַּפָּרָֽה, אָיֹם וְנוֹרָא, מִשְׂגָּב לְעִתּוֹת בַּצָּרָֽה. תְּחַיֵּֽינוּ, תְּחָנֵּֽנוּ, וּבְשִׁמְךָ נִקְרָא. סְלַח לָֽנוּ, וּשְׁלַח לָֽנוּ, יְשׁוּעָה וְרַחֲמִים מִמְּעוֹנֶֽךָ.
Our Master in heaven, to You we supplicate as a servant supplicates his master. Grant us a heart for repentance, and send us complete healing.
Marana devishemaya, lakh mitechanenan keaveda demitechanen lemareh, hav lan liba letiyuveta, vela nehedar reykam min kamakh.
מָרָֽנָא דְבִשְׁמַיָּא, לָךְ מִתְחַנְּנָן כְּעַבְדָּא דְּמִתְחַנֵּן לְמָרֵהּ, הַב לָן לִבָּא לְתִיּוּבְתָּא, וְלָא נֶהְדַּר רֵיקָם מִן קַמָּךְ.
Our Master in heaven, to You we supplicate as a captive supplicates his master. All the captives cry from the depths of their captivity. Hear the voice of their prayers and redeem them from their bondage.
Marana devishemaya, lakh mitechanenan kevar shiveya demitechanen lemareh. Kulehon beney shiveya bekhasepa miteparekiyn, veamakh beyt iserael bitzelotiyn uvetachanuniyn. Arem yemiynakh veatzemach purekanakh, sivera dechayaya umetaya.
מָרָֽנָא דְבִשְׁמַיָּא, לָךְ מִתְחַנְּנָן כְּבַר שִׁבְיָא דְּמִתְחַנֵּן לְמָרֵהּ. כֻּלְּהוֹן בְּנֵי שִׁבְיָא בְּכַסְפָּא מִתְפָּֽרְקִֽין, וְעַמָּךְ בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל בִּצְלוֹתִין וּבְתַחֲנוּנִין. אָרֵם יְמִינָךְ וְאַצְמַח פֻּרְקָנָךְ, סִבְרָא דְחַיָּיָא וּמֵתַיָּא.
You who are appeased by mercy and conciliated by supplication, be appeased to an afflicted generation, for there is no other help. Our Father, our King, be gracious to us and answer us; deal with us in charity and kindness, and save us.
Miteratzeh berachamiym, umitepayes betachanuniym, hiteratzeh vehitepayes leam aniy umeduledal. Perokana demealema meshezevana demilekademiyn, perok yaakov mearea rechiyka, veasiyk zareeh mearea deshiveya.
מִתְרַצֶּה בְּרַחֲמִים, וּמִתְפַּיֵּס בְּתַחֲנוּנִים, הִתְרַצֵּה וְהִתְפַּיֵּס לְעַם עָנִי וּמְדֻלְדָּל. פְּרוֹקָֽנָא דְמֵעָֽלְמָא מְשֵׁזְבָֽנָא דְּמִלְּקַדְמִין, פְּרֹק יַעֲקֹב מֵאַרְעָא רְחִיקָֽא, וְאַסִּיק זַרְעֵהּ מֵאַרְעָא דְשִׁבְיָא.
27 Machei Umasei מַחֵי וּמַסֵּי
He strikes and He heals; He brings death and gives life; He raises from the grave to eternal life. When a son sins, his father punishes him; the father who has compassion heals his pain. A servant who rebels is put in chains; when he returns to his master, the chains are broken. We are Your children and we have sinned before You. Return us to You, O Lord, and we shall return; renew our days as of old.
Machey umasey, memiyt umechey, masiyk min sheol lechayey alema. Bera kad chatey, avuhiy lakeyeh, avuhiy dechaiys, asey lekheveh. Aveda demariyd, venafiyk bekolar, lemareh taiyv, itebar kolareh. Berakh bukherakh anan, vechateynan kamakh, ha raveya nafeshiyn, begidiyn umeradiyn. Ha avedakh anan, umeradenan kamakh, ha bevizeta veshiveya, veha bemalekiyuta. Bevau minakh, bematu minakh, berachamakh dinefiyshiyn, asey lekheviyn, deitekafu alan, ad dela nehevey, gemiyra veshiveya. Machey umasey, memiyt umechey, masiyk min sheol lechayey alema.
מַחֵי וּמַסֵּי, מֵמִית וּמֵחֵי, מַסִּיק מִן שְׁאוֹל לְחַיֵּי עָֽלְמָא. בְּרָא כַּד חָטֵי, אָבֽוּהִי לַקְיֵהּ, אָבֽוּהִי דְחַיִּיס, אַסֵּי לְכֵאבֵהּ. עַבְדָּא דְמָרִיד, וְנָפִיק בְּקוֹלָר, לְמָרֵהּ תָּאִיב, יִתְבַּר קוֹלָרֵהּ. בְּרָךְ בֻּכְרָךְ אֲנָן, וְחַטֵּינָן קַמָּךְ, הָא רַוְיָא נַפְשִׁין, בְּגִדִּין וּמְרָדִין. הָא עַבְדָּךְ אֲנָן, וּמְרַדְּנָן קַמָּךְ, הָא בְּבִזְתָּא וְשִׁבְיָא, וְהָא בְּמַלְקִיּוּתָֽא. בְּבָעֽוּ מִנָּךְ, בְּמָטֽוּ מִנָּךְ, בְּרַחֲמָךְ דִּנְפִישִׁין, אַסֵּי לְכֵאבִין, דְּאִתְּקָפוּ עֲלָן, עַד דְּלָא נֶהֱוֵי, גְּמִירָא בְשִׁבְיָא. מַחֵי וּמַסֵּי, מֵמִית וּמֵחֵי, מַסִּיק מִן שְׁאוֹל לְחַיֵּי עָֽלְמָא.
28 Rachamim Peshutim רַחֲמִים פְּשׁוּטִים
מנהג מרן הראש"ל שליט"א לחבר לאחד את שני הקטעים דלהלן, ואומר: "רחמים פשוטים וחיים טובים בקשנו ושאלנו ממך, כי רבים רחמים ומקור חיים עמך, צדקה וחסד עשה עמנו" וכו'
The custom of Maran the Rishon LeTzion, may he live long, is to combine the following two passages into one, saying: "Simple mercies and a good life we have sought and requested from You, for abundant are Your mercies and the source of life is with You; perform righteousness and kindness with us," etc.
Simple mercies we have sought from You, for many mercies are with You; act with us in righteousness and kindness for the sake of Your Name. Pray, O Lord our God and God of our fathers, remove from us and from our houses, and from the houses of all Your people the house of Israel wherever they are: pestilence, and sword, and evil, and famine, and captivity, and plunder, and destruction, and plague, and the Adversary, and the evil inclination, and evil diseases, and distress—from us and from Your people.
Rachamiym peshutiym bikashenu mimakh, kiy rabiym rachamiym imakh, tzedakah vachesed aseh imanu lemaan shemekha. Ana Adonai eloheynu velohey avoteynu, haser mimenu umibateynu, umibatey kal-amekha beyt iserael bekhal-makom shehem dever, vecherev, veraah, veraav, usheviy, uvizah, umashechiyt, umagefah, vesatan, veyetzer hara, vacholaiym raiym, vetzar mimenu umeamakh.
רַחֲמִים פְּשׁוּטִים בִּקַּֽשְׁנוּ מִמָּךְ, כִּי רַבִּים רַחֲמִים עִמָּךְ, צְדָקָה וָחֶֽסֶד עֲשֵׂה עִמָּֽנוּ לְמַֽעַן שְׁמֶֽךָ. אָֽנָּֽא יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ וֵאלֹהֵי אֲבוֹתֵֽינוּ, הָסֵר מִמֶּֽנּוּ וּמִבָּתֵּֽינוּ, וּמִבָּתֵּי כָּל־עַמְּךָ בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּכָל־מָקוֹם שֶׁהֵם דֶּֽבֶר, וְחֶֽרֶב, וְרָעָה, וְרָעָב, וּשְׁבִי, וּבִזָּה, וּמַשְׁחִית, וּמַגֵּפָה, וְשָׂטָן, וְיֵֽצֶר הָרָע, וָחֳלָאִים רָעִים, וְצַר מִמֶּֽנּוּ וּמֵעַמָּךְ.
A good life we have asked of You, for the source of life is with You; act with us in righteousness and kindness for the sake of Your Name. Pray, O Lord our God and God of our fathers, remove from us and from our houses, and from the houses of all Your people the house of Israel wherever they are: pestilence, and sword, and evil, and famine, and captivity, and plunder, and destruction, and plague, and the Adversary, and the evil inclination, and evil diseases, and distress—from us and from Your people.
Chaiym toviym shaalenu mimakh, kiy mekor chaiym imakh, tzedakah vachesed aseh imanu lemaan shemekha. Ana Adonai eloheynu velohey avoteynu, haser mimenu umibateynu, umibatey kal-amekha beyt iserael bekhal-makom shehem dever, vecherev, veraah, veraav, usheviy, uvizah, umashechiyt, umagefah, vesatan, veyetzer hara, vacholaiym raiym, vetzar mimenu umeamakh.
חַיִּים טוֹבִים שָׁאַֽלְנוּ מִמָּךְ, כִּי מְקוֹר חַיִּים עִמָּךְ, צְדָקָה וָחֶֽסֶד עֲשֵׂה עִמָּֽנוּ לְמַֽעַן שְׁמֶֽךָ. אָֽנָּֽא יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ וֵאלֹהֵי אֲבוֹתֵֽינוּ, הָסֵר מִמֶּֽנּוּ וּמִבָּתֵּֽינוּ, וּמִבָּתֵּי כָּל־עַמְּךָ בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּכָל־מָקוֹם שֶׁהֵם דֶּֽבֶר, וְחֶֽרֶב, וְרָעָה, וְרָעָב, וּשְׁבִי, וּבִזָּה, וּמַשְׁחִית, וּמַגֵּפָה, וְשָׂטָן, וְיֵֽצֶר הָרָע, וָחֳלָאִים רָעִים, וְצַר מִמֶּֽנּוּ וּמֵעַמָּךְ.
29 Elekha Adonai אֵלֶֽיךָ יְהֹוָה נָשָֽׂאתִי עֵינַֽי
To You, O Lord, I have lifted my eyes. Hear the voice of my supplications, in the greatness of Your kindness.
Eleykha Adonai nasatiy eynay, shema kol tachanunay, kegodel chasedekha.
אֵלֶֽיךָ יְהֹוָה נָשָֽׂאתִי עֵינַֽי, שְׁמַֽע קוֹל תַּחֲנוּנַֽי, כְּגֹֽדֶל חַסְדֶּֽךָ.
In Your Name I have trusted, and my palms I have spread. Words I have taken, and I have come before You.
Beshimekha batachtiy, vekhapay shitachetiy, devariym lakachetiy, uvatiy adeykha.
בְּשִׁמְךָֽ בָּטַֽחְתִּי, וְכַפַּי שִׁטַּֽחְתִּי, דְּבָרִֽים לָקַֽחְתִּי, וּבָֽאתִי עָדֶֽיךָ.
My sorrows have increased, and my sighs have multiplied, for all my iniquities are set before You.
Gaveru yegonotay, vairebu anechotay, kiy khal-avonotay, shatah lenegedekha.
גָּֽבְרֽוּ יְגוֹנוֹתַֽי, וַיִּרְבּֽוּ אַנְחוֹתַֽי, כִּי כָל־עֲוֹנוֹתַֽי, שַׁתָּֽה לְנֶגְדֶּֽךָ.
My soul drips from sorrow. Woe is me, for my transgressions have overtaken me.
Dalefah mitugah, nafeshiy haanugah, vechaletz mideagah, nefesh avedekha.
דָּֽלְפָֽה מִתּוּגָֽה, נַפְשִֽׁי הָעֲנוּגָֽה, וְחַלֵּֽץ מִדְּאָגָֽה, נֶֽפֶשׁ עַבְדֶּֽךָ.
Remove my sin, O God of my salvation, and also forgive my transgression.
Haaver chatatiy, elohey yeshuatiy, vegam et-dimeatiy, siymah benodekha.
הַעֲבֵֽר חַטָּֽאתִי, אֱלֹהֵֽי יְשׁוּעָתִֽי, וְגַם אֶת־דִּמְעָתִֽי, שִׂימָֽה בְּנֹאדֶֽךָ.
And in anger, remember mercy. Remember and relent, and gladden Your people.
Uverogez rachem, zakherah vehinachem, vesamecha venachem, nefesh avedekha.
וּבְרֹֽגֶז רַחֵֽם, זָכְרָֽה וְהִנָּֽחֵם, וְשַׂמֵּֽחַ וְנַחֵֽם, נֶֽפֶשׁ עַבְדֶּֽךָ.
Remember my affliction and wandering, the wormwood and the gall.
Zekhor aneiy umerudiy, laanah meodiy, ad matay lenegediy, techadesh edeykha.
זְכֹר עָנְיִֽי וּמְרוּדִֽי, לַעֲנָֽה מֵעוֹדִֽי, עַד מָתַֽי לְנֶגְדִּֽי, תְּחַדֵּֽשׁ עֵדֶֽיךָ.
My enemies have scorned me. Attend to my cry, O Lord.
Cherefuniy tzoreray, oyevay veshoreray, hakeshev-na amaray, bevoiy befachedekha.
חֵֽרְפֽוּנִי צֽוֹרְרַֽי, אֽוֹיְבַֽי וְשֽׁוֹרְרַֽי, הַקְשֶׁב־נָא אֲמָרַֽי, בְּבֹאִֽי בְּפַחְדֶּֽךָ.
The wicked have laid snares and traps for me; protect me, O Lord.
Tamenu liy zediym, pachiym umetzudiym, uvahem nilekadiym, paamey yediydeykha.
טָֽמְנוּ לִֽי זֵדִֽים, פַּחִֽים וּמְצוּדִֽים, וּבָהֶֽם נִלְכָּדִֽים, פַּעֲמֵֽי יְדִידֶֽיךָ.
They set traps for me and speak falsehood against me.
Yesiymun liy metzudiym, vesheker biy meiydiym, bealuniy zediym, tzur bileadeykha.
יְשִׂימֽוּן לִי מְצוּדִֽים, וְשֶֽׁקֶר בִּי מְעִידִֽים, בְּעָלֽוּנִי זֵדִֽים, צוּר בִּלְעָדֶֽיךָ.
Reveal Your glory, O Rock, to the forsaken people. Arise and shine.
Kevodekha galeh tzur, leam azuv veatzur, vetuvekha hanatzur, tenah liseriydeykha.
כְּבֽוֹדְךָֽ גַּלֵּֽה צוּר, לְעַם עָזֽוּב וְעָצֽוּר, וְטוּבְךָֽ הַנָּצֽוּר, תְּנָֽה לִשְׂרִידֶֽיךָ.
To those pressed with great hardship, do not be distant from them.
Lechutziym berov dochak, mehem al tirechak, zekhor leaveraham leitzechak, uleiserael avadeykha.
לְחוּצִֽים בְּרֹב דֹּֽחַק, מֵהֶֽם אַל תִּרְחַֽק, זְכֹר לְאַבְרָהָֽם לְיִצְחָֽק, וּלְיִשְׂרָאֵֽל עֲבָדֶֽיךָ.
O Most High, from the heights, have mercy on the sorrowful.
Marom mimeromiym, rachem al agumiym, veal zera rechumiym, yeliydey yediydeykha.
מָרֽוֹם מִמְּרוֹמִֽים, רַחֵֽם עַל עֲגוּמִֽים, וְעַל זֶֽרַע רְחוּמִֽים, יְלִידֵֽי יְדִידֶֽיךָ.
Those who groan in the pit of exile have placed their hope in You.
Noakiym bevor galut, bekha samu eyalut, veharem mishifelut, geza chasiydeykha.
נוֹאֲקִֽים בְּבוֹר גָּלֽוּת, בְּךָ שָֽׂמוּ אֱיָלֽוּת, וְהָרֵֽם מִשִּׁפְלֽוּת, גֶּֽזַע חֲסִידֶֽיךָ.
Over those who suffer, rejoice, and their enemies confound.
Soveley ol tetzahel, veoyeveyhem tevahel, ukhekedem tenahel, digeley gedudeykha.
סֽוֹבְלֵי עֹל תְּצַהֵֽל, וְאֽוֹיְבֵיהֶֽם תְּבַהֵֽל, וּכְקֶֽדֶם תְּנַהֵֽל, דִּגְלֵֽי גְדוּדֶֽיךָ.
Answer their supplication and support their cause.
Aneh et atiyratam, semokh et nefiylatam, vehashev et shevutam, meafesey cheledekha.
עֲנֵה אֶת עֲתִירָתָֽם, סְמֹךְ אֶת נְפִילָתָֽם, וְהָשֵֽׁב אֶת שְׁבוּתָֽם, מֵאַפְסֵֽי חֶלְדֶּֽךָ.
Redeem a people devoted to You, those who are silent in prayer.
Pedeh am lekha homeh, ukheilem domeh, begalut zeh khameh, saveu nedudeykha.
פְּדֵֽה עַם לְךָ הוֹמֶֽה, וּכְאִלֵּֽם דּוֹמֶֽה, בְּגָלֽוּת זֶה כַמֶּֽה, שָֽׂבְעֽוּ נְדוּדֶֽיךָ.
The Righteous One in His deeds, to You all actions are directed.
Tzadiyk bemifealot, lekha nitekenu aliylot, delenu mimetzulot, lemaan chasadeykha.
צַדִּֽיק בְּמִפְעָלֽוֹת, לְךָ נִתְכְּנֽוּ עֲלִילֽוֹת, דְּלֵֽנוּ מִמְּצוּלֽוֹת, לְמַֽעַן חֲסָדֶֽיךָ.
Gather the scattered, those imprisoned in chains of the oppressor.
Kabetz mefuzariym, bekhaveley tzar seguriym, belaum akhezariym, sarefu moadeykha.
קַבֵּֽץ מְפֻזָּרִֽים, בְּכַבְלֵֽי צַר סְגוּרִֽים, בְּלָעֽוּם אַכְזָרִֽים, שָֽׂרְפֽוּ מוֹעֲדֶֽיךָ.
O Merciful One, be a support to the people saturated with suffering.
Rachum heyeh lemishean, leam raveh laan, vaaseh na lemaan, sheloshet avadeykha.
רַחֽוּם הֱיֵֽה לְמִשְׁעָֽן, לְעַם רָוֶֽה לַֽעַן, וַעֲשֵֽׂה נָא לְמַֽעַן, שְׁלֹֽשֶׁת עֲבָדֶֽיךָ.
Almighty, You are exalted in all that You have done. For great is Your kindness toward me.
Shaday nisegaveta, bekhal-asher paaleta, kiy tzadiyk atah, vetzedek madeykha.
שַׁדַּֽי נִשְׂגַּֽבְתָּ, בְּכָל־אֲשֶׁר פָּעַֽלְתָּ, כִּי צַדִּֽיק אַתָּֽה, וְצֶֽדֶק מַדֶּֽיךָ.
Atone for my iniquity, for my hope is in You. Blot out my transgressions.
Tekhaper avoniy, kiy siveriy bekha malekiy, vekhonen et darekiy, Elohai leavedekha.
תְּכַפֵּֽר עֲוֹנִֽי, כִּי שִׂבְרִֽי בְּךָ מַלְכִּֽי, וְכוֹנֵֽן אֶת דַּרְכִּֽי, אֱלֹהַֽי לְעָבְדֶּֽךָ.
Attend to my prayer and to my song of joy. For in You, O Lord, I have hoped.
Tisheeh litefilatiy, ulesiycha rinatiy, kiy atah tikevatiy, umiy lo yeiydekha.
תִּשְׁעֶֽה לִתְפִלָּתִֽי, וּלְשִֽׂיחַ רִנָּתִֽי, כִּי אַתָּֽה תִקְוָתִֽי, וּמִי לֹא יְעִידֶֽךָ.
To You, O Lord, I have lifted my eyes. Hear the voice of my supplications, in the greatness of Your kindness.
Eleykha Adonai nasatiy eynay, shema kol tachanunay, kegodel chasedekha.
אֵלֶֽיךָ יְהֹוָה נָשָֽׂאתִי עֵינַֽי, שְׁמַֽע קוֹל תַּחֲנוּנַֽי, כְּגֹֽדֶל חַסְדֶּֽךָ.
30 Avinu Av HaRachaman אָבִֽינוּ אָב הָרַחֲמָן
Our Father, merciful Father, save us for the sake of Your Name.
Aviynu av harachaman, hoshiyenu lemaan shemekha.
אָבִֽינוּ אָב הָרַחֲמָן, הוֹשִׁיעֵֽנוּ לְמַֽעַן שְׁמֶֽךָ.
Our God and God of our ancestors, save us for the sake of Your Name.
Eloheynu velohey avoteynu, hoshiyenu lemaan shemekha.
אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ וֵאלֹהֵי אֲבוֹתֵֽינוּ, הוֹשִׁיעֵֽנוּ לְמַֽעַן שְׁמֶֽךָ.
In our distress we have called upon You; save us for the sake of Your Name.
Batzar lanu keranukha, vehoshiyenu lemaan shemekha.
בַּצָּר לָֽנוּ קְרָאנֽוּךָ, וְהוֹשִׁיעֵֽנוּ לְמַֽעַן שְׁמֶֽךָ.
Cause the multitude of Your mercies to flow upon us; save us for the sake of Your Name.
Galegel aleynu hamon rachameykha, vehoshiyenu lemaan shemekha.
גַּלְגֵּל עָלֵֽינוּ הֲמוֹן רַחֲמֶֽיךָ, וְהוֹשִׁיעֵֽנוּ לְמַֽעַן שְׁמֶֽךָ.
We have sought You; be found by us. Save us for the sake of Your Name.
Derashenukha himatze lanu, vehoshiyenu lemaan shemekha.
