דְּרַשְׁנֽוּךָ הִמָּֽצֵא לָֽנוּ, וְהוֹשִׁיעֵֽנוּ לְמַֽעַן שְׁמֶֽךָ.
Answer us today and every day in our prayer; save us for the sake of Your Name.
Heater-lanu hayom uvekhal-yom vayom bitefilatenu, vehoshiyenu lemaan shemekha.
הֵעָֽתֶר־לָֽנוּ הַיּוֹם וּבְכָל־יוֹם וָיוֹם בִּתְפִלָּתֵֽנוּ, וְהוֹשִׁיעֵֽנוּ לְמַֽעַן שְׁמֶֽךָ.
Do not put us to shame; save us for the sake of Your Name.
Veal teviyshenu misivrenu, vehoshiyenu lemaan shemekha.
וְאַל תְּבִישֵֽׁנוּ מִשִּׂבְרֵֽנוּ, וְהוֹשִׁיעֵֽנוּ לְמַֽעַן שְׁמֶֽךָ.
Remember us with a favorable memory; save us for the sake of Your Name.
Zakherenu bezikheron tov milefaneykha, vehoshiyenu lemaan shemekha.
זָכְרֵֽנוּ בְּזִכְרוֹן טוֹב מִלְּפָנֶֽיךָ, וְהוֹשִׁיעֵֽנוּ לְמַֽעַן שְׁמֶֽךָ.
Have pity and mercy upon us; save us for the sake of Your Name.
Chus verachem aleynu, vehoshiyenu lemaan shemekha.
חוּס וְרַחֵם עָלֵֽינוּ, וְהוֹשִׁיעֵֽנוּ לְמַֽעַן שְׁמֶֽךָ.
Purify us from the impurity of our sins; save us for the sake of Your Name.
Taharenu mitumeot avoneynu, vehoshiyenu lemaan shemekha.
טַהֲרֵֽנוּ מִטֻּמְאוֹת עֲוֹנֵֽינוּ, וְהוֹשִׁיעֵֽנוּ לְמַֽעַן שְׁמֶֽךָ.
Let Your mercies be aroused upon us; save us for the sake of Your Name.
Yehemu-na rachameykha aleynu, vehoshiyenu lemaan shemekha.
יֶֽהֱמוּ־נָֽא רַחֲמֶֽיךָ עָלֵֽינוּ, וְהוֹשִׁיעֵֽנוּ לְמַֽעַן שְׁמֶֽךָ.
Have compassion upon Your people and mercy upon Your inheritance.
Chamol al amakh, verachem al nachalatakh, chusah na kerov rachameykha, chanenu malekenu vaanenu.
חֲמֹל עַל עַמָּךְ, וְרַחֵם עַל נַחֲלָתָךְ, חֽוּסָה נָּֽא כְּרֹב רַחֲמֶֽיךָ, חָנֵּֽנוּ מַלְכֵּֽנוּ וַעֲנֵֽנוּ.
31 Avinu Malkeinu אָבִֽינוּ מַלְכֵּֽנוּ אָבִֽינוּ אָֽתָּה
מרן הראש"ל שליט"א אינו נוהג לומר פיוט זה, וממשיך הקטע שלאחריו, "אבינו מלכנו אבינו אתה, אבינו מלכנו אין לנון מלך אלא אתה" וכו'.
Maran the Rishon LeTzion, may he live long, does not recite this piyyut, and continues with the next section: "Avinu Malkenu, You are our Father; Avinu Malkenu, we have no king but You," etc.
Our Father, our King, You are our Father. Our Father, our King, we have no King but You. Our Father, our King, have mercy upon us. Our Father, our King, be gracious to us and answer us, for we have no deeds; act with us in righteousness and kindness for the sake of Your great Name, and save us. And we do not know what we shall do, for our eyes are upon You. Remember Your mercies, O Lord, and Your kindnesses, for they are from everlasting. Let Your kindness, O Lord, be upon us, as we have hoped in You. Do not remember against us the iniquities of the first ones; let Your mercies quickly precede us, for we are brought very low. Our help is in the Name of the Lord, Maker of heaven and earth. Be gracious to us, O Lord, be gracious to us, for we are greatly filled with contempt. In wrath, remember mercy. In wrath, remember love. In wrath, remember the Binding. In wrath, remember sincerity. O Lord, save; may the King answer us on the day we call. For He knows our impulse; He remembers that we are dust. Help us, O God of our salvation, for the sake of the glory of Your Name; deliver us and atone for our sins for the sake of Your Name.
Aviynu malekenu aviynu atah. Aviynu malekenu eyn lanu ela atah. Aviynu malekenu rachem aleynu.
אָבִֽינוּ מַלְכֵּֽנוּ אָבִֽינוּ אָֽתָּה. אָבִֽינוּ מַלְכֵּֽנוּ אֵין לָֽנוּ אֶלָּֽא אָֽתָּה. אָבִֽינוּ מַלְכֵּֽנוּ רַחֵם עָלֵֽינוּ.
If we have done good, You are our Father. If we have transgressed, You are our Father. If we have spoken much, You are our Father.
Im hetavenu foal aviynu atah. Veim heraenu maal eyn lanu ela atah. Aviynu malekenu rachem aleynu.
אִם הֵטַֽבְנוּ פֹֽעַל אָבִֽינוּ אָֽתָּה. וְאִם הֵרַֽעְנוּ מַֽעַל אֵין לָֽנוּ אֶלָּֽא אָֽתָּה. אָבִֽינוּ מַלְכֵּֽנוּ רַחֵם עָלֵֽינוּ.
Though our sins have prevailed, You are our Father.
Im gavar pisheenu aviynu atah. Atah tzur isheenu eyn lanu ela atah. Aviynu malekenu rachem aleynu.
אִם גָּבַֽר פִּשְׁעֵֽנוּ אָבִֽינוּ אָֽתָּה. אַתָּֽה צוּר יִשְׁעֵֽנוּ אֵין לָֽנוּ אֶלָּֽא אָֽתָּה. אָבִֽינוּ מַלְכֵּֽנוּ רַחֵם עָלֵֽינוּ.
Though we have multiplied speech, You are our Father. Remember that we are clay; we have none but You. Our Father, our King, have mercy upon us.
Im hirebiynu omer aviynu atah. Zekhor kiy anachenu chomer eyn lanu ela atah. Aviynu malekenu rachem aleynu.
אִם הִרְבִּֽינוּ אֹֽמֶר אָבִֽינוּ אָֽתָּה. זְכֹר כִּי אֲנַֽחְנוּ חֹֽמֶר אֵין לָֽנוּ אֶלָּֽא אָֽתָּה. אָבִֽינוּ מַלְכֵּֽנוּ רַחֵם עָלֵֽינוּ.
If to You we have sinned, You are our Father.
Im lekha chatanu aviynu atah. Adon selach lanu, eyn lanu ela atah. Aviynu malekenu rachem aleynu.
אִם לְךָ חָטָֽאנוּ אָבִֽינוּ אָֽתָּה. אָדוֹן סְלַח לָֽנוּ, אֵין לָֽנוּ אֶלָּֽא אָֽתָּה. אָבִֽינוּ מַלְכֵּֽנוּ רַחֵם עָלֵֽינוּ.
To You, O Lord, we have hoped, You are our Father.
Lekha Adonai kiviynu, aviynu atah. Kiy atah aviynu, eyn lanu ela atah. Aviynu malekenu rachem aleynu.
לְךָ יְהֹוָה קִוִּֽינוּ, אָבִֽינוּ אָֽתָּה. כִּי אַתָּֽה אָבִֽינוּ, אֵין לָֽנוּ אֶלָּֽא אָֽתָּה. אָבִֽינוּ מַלְכֵּֽנוּ רַחֵם עָלֵֽינוּ.
To You we have spread our palms, You are our Father.
Lekha perasenu shaal, aviynu atah. Hametziy lanu taal, eyn lanu ela atah. Aviynu malekenu rachem aleynu.
לְךָ פֵּרַֽשְׂנוּ שַֽׁעַל, אָבִֽינוּ אָֽתָּה. הַמְצִֽיא לָֽנוּ תַּֽעַל, אֵין לָֽנוּ אֶלָּֽא אָֽתָּה. אָבִֽינוּ מַלְכֵּֽנוּ רַחֵם עָלֵֽינוּ.
Hurry, send the anointed one, You are our Father.
Chushah shelach mashiycha, aviynu atah. Vetziytz yesha taferiycha, eyn lanu ela atah. Aviynu malekenu rachem aleynu.
חֽוּשָׁה שְׁלַח מָשִֽׁיחַ, אָבִֽינוּ אָֽתָּה. וְצִיץ יֶֽשַׁע תַּפְרִֽיחַ, אֵין לָֽנוּ אֶלָּֽא אָֽתָּה. אָבִֽינוּ מַלְכֵּֽנוּ רַחֵם עָלֵֽינוּ.
Our Father, our King, You are our Father. Our Father, our King, we have no King but You. Our Father, our King, have mercy upon us. Our Father, our King, be gracious to us and answer us, for we have no deeds; act with us in righteousness and kindness for the sake of Your great Name, and save us. And we do not know what we shall do, for our eyes are upon You. Remember Your mercies, O Lord, and Your kindnesses, for they are from everlasting. Let Your kindness, O Lord, be upon us, as we have hoped in You. Do not remember against us the iniquities of the first ones; let Your mercies quickly precede us, for we are brought very low. Our help is in the Name of the Lord, Maker of heaven and earth. Be gracious to us, O Lord, be gracious to us, for we are greatly filled with contempt. In wrath, remember mercy. In wrath, remember love. In wrath, remember the Binding. In wrath, remember sincerity. O Lord, save; may the King answer us on the day we call. For He knows our impulse; He remembers that we are dust. Help us, O God of our salvation, for the sake of the glory of Your Name; deliver us and atone for our sins for the sake of Your Name.
Aviynu malekenu aviynu atah. Aviynu malekenu eyn lanu melekh ela atah. Aviynu malekenu rachem aleynu.
אָבִֽינוּ מַלְכֵּֽנוּ אָבִֽינוּ אָֽתָּה. אָבִֽינוּ מַלְכֵּֽנוּ אֵין לָֽנוּ מֶֽלֶךְ אֶלָּֽא אָֽתָּה. אָבִֽינוּ מַלְכֵּֽנוּ רַחֵם עָלֵֽינוּ.
Our Father, our King, You are our Father. Our Father, our King, we have no King but You. Our Father, our King, have mercy upon us. Our Father, our King, be gracious to us and answer us, for we have no deeds; act with us in righteousness and kindness for the sake of Your great Name, and save us. And we do not know what we shall do, for our eyes are upon You. Remember Your mercies, O Lord, and Your kindnesses, for they are from everlasting. Let Your kindness, O Lord, be upon us, as we have hoped in You. Do not remember against us the iniquities of the first ones; let Your mercies quickly precede us, for we are brought very low. Our help is in the Name of the Lord, Maker of heaven and earth. Be gracious to us, O Lord, be gracious to us, for we are greatly filled with contempt. In wrath, remember mercy. In wrath, remember love. In wrath, remember the Binding. In wrath, remember sincerity. O Lord, save; may the King answer us on the day we call. For He knows our impulse; He remembers that we are dust. Help us, O God of our salvation, for the sake of the glory of Your Name; deliver us and atone for our sins for the sake of Your Name.
Aviynu malekenu aviynu atah. Aviynu malekenu eyn lanu melekh ela atah. Aviynu malekenu rachem aleynu. Aviynu malekenu chanenu vaanenu kiy eyn banu maasiym. Aseh imanu tzedakah vachesed lemaan shimekha hagadol vehoshiyenu. Vaanachenu lo neda mah-naaseh kiy aleykha eyneynu: Zekhor-rachameykha Adonai vachasadeykha kiy meolam hemah: Yehiy-chasedekha Adonai aleynu kaasher ichalenu lakh: Al-tizekar-lanu avonot rishoniym maher yekademunu rachameykha kiy dalonu meod: Ezerenu beshem Adonai oshoh shamaim vaaretz: Chanenu Adonai chanenu kiy-rav savaenu vuz: Berogez rachem tizekor: Berogez ahavah tizekor. Berogez akedah tizekor. Berogez temiymut tizekor. Adonai hoshiyah hamelekh yaanenu veyom-kareenu: Kiy-hu yada itzerenu zakhur kiy-afar anachenu: Azerenu elohey isheenu al-devar kevod-shemekha vehatziylenu vekhaper al-chatoteynu lemaan shemekha:
אָבִֽינוּ מַלְכֵּֽנוּ אָבִֽינוּ אָֽתָּה. אָבִֽינוּ מַלְכֵּֽנוּ אֵין לָֽנוּ מֶֽלֶךְ אֶלָּֽא אָֽתָּה. אָבִֽינוּ מַלְכֵּֽנוּ רַחֵם עָלֵֽינוּ. אָבִֽינוּ מַלְכֵּֽנוּ חָנֵּֽנוּ וַעֲנֵֽנוּ כִּי אֵין בָּֽנוּ מַעֲשִׂים. עֲשֵׂה עִמָּֽנוּ צְדָקָה וָחֶֽסֶד לְמַֽעַן שִׁמְךָ הַגָּדוֹל וְהוֹשִׁיעֵֽנוּ. וַֽאֲנַחְנוּ לֹא נֵדַע מַֽה־נַּֽעֲשֶׂה כִּי עָלֶיךָ עֵינֵֽינוּ: זְכֹֽר־רַֽחֲמֶיךָ יְהֹוָה וַֽחֲסָדֶיךָ כִּי מֵֽעוֹלָם הֵֽמָּה: יְהִֽי־חַסְדְּךָ יְהֹוָה עָלֵינוּ כַּֽאֲשֶׁר יִחַלְנוּ לָֽךְ: אַֽל־תִּזְכָּר־לָנוּ עֲוֹנֹת רִֽאשֹׁנִים מַהֵר יְקַדְּמוּנוּ רַֽחֲמֶיךָ כִּי דַלּוֹנוּ מְאֹֽד: עֶזְרֵנוּ בְּשֵׁם יְהֹוָה עֹשֵֹה שָׁמַיִם וָאָֽרֶץ: חָנֵּנוּ יְהֹוָה חָנֵּנוּ כִּי־רַב שָׂבַעְנוּ בֽוּז: בְּרֹגֶז רַחֵם תִּזְכּֽוֹר: בְּרֹֽגֶז אַהֲבָה תִּזְכּֽוֹר. בְּרֹֽגֶז עֲקֵדָה תִּזְכּֽוֹר. בְּרֹֽגֶז תְּמִימוּת תִּזְכּֽוֹר. יְהֹוָה הוֹשִׁיעָה הַמֶּלֶךְ יַֽעֲנֵנוּ בְיוֹם־קָרְאֵֽנוּ: כִּי־הוּא יָדַע יִצְרֵנוּ זָכוּר כִּֽי־עָפָר אֲנָֽחְנוּ: עָזְרֵנוּ | אֱלֹהֵי יִשְׁעֵנוּ עַל־דְּבַר כְּבֽוֹד־שְׁמֶךָ וְהַצִּילֵנוּ וְכַפֵּר עַל־חַטֹּאתֵינוּ לְמַעַן שְׁמֶֽךָ:
32 Shomer Yisrael שׁוֹמֵר יִשְׂרָאֵל
Guardian of Israel, guard the remnant of Israel, and let not Israel perish, who declare: 'Hear, O Israel.'
Shomer iserael, shemor sheeriyt iserael, veal yovad iserael, haomeriym bekhal-yom: Shema iserael.
שׁוֹמֵר יִשְׂרָאֵל, שְׁמֹר שְׁאֵרִית יִשְׂרָאֵל, וְאַל יֹאבַד יִשְׂרָאֵל, הָאֽוֹמְרִֽים בְּכָל־יוֹם: שְׁמַע יִשְׂרָאֵל.
Guardian of a unique nation, guard the remnant of the unique nation, and let not the unique nation perish, who declare: 'Hear, O Israel, the Lord our God, the Lord is One.'
Shomer goy echad, shemor sheeriyt goy echad, veal yovad goy echad, haomeriym bekhal-yom: Shema iserael, Adonai eloheynu Adonai echad.
שׁוֹמֵר גּוֹי אֶחָד, שְׁמֹר שְׁאֵרִית גּוֹי אֶחָד, וְאַל יֹאבַד גּוֹי אֶחָד, הָאֽוֹמְרִֽים בְּכָל־יוֹם: שְׁמַע יִשְׂרָאֵל, יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ יְהֹוָה אֶחָד.
Guardian of a holy nation, guard the remnant of the holy nation, and let not the holy nation perish, who thrice sanctify the Holy One.
Shomer goy kadosh, shemor sheeriyt goy kadosh, veal yovad goy kadosh, haomeriym bekhal-yom: Kadosh, kadosh, kadosh.
שׁוֹמֵר גּוֹי קָדוֹשׁ, שְׁמֹר שְׁאֵרִית גּוֹי קָדוֹשׁ, וְאַל יֹאבַד גּוֹי קָדוֹשׁ, הָאֽוֹמְרִֽים בְּכָל־יוֹם: קָדוֹשׁ, קָדוֹשׁ, קָדוֹשׁ.
Guardian of a great nation, guard the remnant of the great nation, and let not the great nation perish, who declare 'Blessed be the Name of the glory of His kingdom forever and ever.'
Shomer goy raba, shemor sheeriyt goy raba, veal yovad goy raba, haomeriym bekhal-yom: Amen yehe shemeyh raba.
שׁוֹמֵר גּוֹי רַבָּא, שְׁמֹר שְׁאֵרִית גּוֹי רַבָּא, וְאַל יֹאבַד גּוֹי רַבָּא, הָאֽוֹמְרִֽים בְּכָל־יוֹם: אָמֵן יְהֵא שְׁמֵיהּ רַבָּא.
33 Kaddish קדיש תענו ותעתרו
ואומר השליח ציבור קדיש תענו ותעתרו:
The prayer leader recites the Kaddish "Ta'anu VeTe'atru":
May His great Name be exalted and sanctified in the world He created according to His will. May He establish His kingdom, cause His salvation to sprout, and bring near His anointed, in your lifetime and in your days, speedily and soon; and say Amen. May His great Name be blessed forever and ever. Blessed, praised, glorified, exalted, extolled, honored, elevated, and lauded be the Name of the Holy One, blessed is He, above all blessings, songs, praises, and consolations uttered in the world; and say Amen.
Itegadal veitekadash shemeh raba. Amen. Bealema diy vera kireuteh, veyameliykh malekhuteh, veyatzemach purekaneh, viykarev meshiycheh. Amen. Bechayeykhon uveyomeykhon uvechayey dekhal-beyt iserael, baagala uvizeman kariyv, veimeru amen. Amen. Yehe shemeh raba mevarakh lealam ulealemey alemaya itebarakh, veishetabach, veitepaar, veiteromam, veitenase, veitehadar, veitealeh, veitehalal, shemeh dekudesha beriykh hu. Amen. Leela min kal-birekhata, shiyrata, tishebechata venechamata, daamiyran bealema, veimeru amen. Amen.
יִתְגַּדַּל וְיִתְקַדַּשׁ שְׁמֵהּ רַבָּא. אמן. בְּעָֽלְמָא דִי בְרָא כִּרְעוּתֵהּ, וְיַמְלִיךְ מַלְכוּתֵהּ, וְיַצְמַח פּוּרְקָנֵהּ, וִיקָרֵב מְשִׁיחֵהּ. אמן. בְּחַיֵּיכוֹן וּבְיוֹמֵיכוֹן וּבְחַיֵּי דְכָל־בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל, בַּעֲגָלָא וּבִזְמַן קָרִיב, וְאִמְרוּ אָמֵן. אמן. יְהֵא שְׁמֵהּ רַבָּא מְבָרַךְ לְעָלַם וּלְעָֽלְמֵי עָֽלְמַיָּֽא יִתְבָּרַךְ, וְיִשְׁתַּבַּח, וְיִתְפָּאַר, וְיִתְרוֹמַם, וְיִתְנַשֵּׂא, וְיִתְהַדָּר, וְיִתְעַלֶּה, וְיִתְהַלָּל, שְׁמֵהּ דְּקוּדְשָׁא בְּרִיךְ הוּא. אמן. לְעֵֽלָּא מִן כָּל־בִּרְכָתָא, שִׁירָתָא, תִּשְׁבְּחָתָא וְנֶחָמָתָא, דַּאֲמִירָן בְּעָֽלְמָא, וְאִמְרוּ אָמֵן. אמן.
May you be answered and may your pleas be accepted with mercy from heaven; may your cry be received; may your prayer be heard with favor; and may the voice of your petition be answered; and may there be fulfilled in you the verse that is written: The Lord, God of your fathers, increase you a thousandfold as you are, and bless you as He has spoken to you. And so may it be His will, and let us say Amen. Amen.
Teanu veteateru berachamiym min hashamayim, tekubal tzaakatekhem, tishama tefilatekhem beratzon, veyeaneh kol atiyratekhem, viykayem bakhem mikera shekatuv: Adonai elohey avotekhem yosef aleykhem kakhem elef peamiym viyvarekh etekhem kaasher diber lakhem: Vekhen yehiy ratzon venomar amen. Amen.
תֵּעָנֽוּ וְתֵעָֽתְרֽוּ בְּרַחֲמִים מִן הַשָּׁמַֽיִם, תְּקֻבַּל צַעֲקַתְכֶם, תִּשָּׁמַע תְּפִלַּתְכֶם בְּרָצוֹן, וְיֵעָנֶה קוֹל עֲתִירַתְכֶם, וִיקַיֵּם בָּכֶם מִקְרָא שֶׁכָּתוּב: יְהֹוָה אֱלֹהֵי אֲבֽוֹתֵכֶם יֹסֵף עֲלֵיכֶם כָּכֶם אֶלֶף פְּעָמִים וִֽיבָרֵךְ אֶתְכֶם כַּֽאֲשֶׁר דִּבֶּר לָכֶֽם: וְכֵן יְהִי רָצוֹן וְנֹאמַר אָמֵן. אמן.
May the prayers and supplications of all Israel be accepted before their Father in heaven; and say, Amen.
Titekabal tzelotana uvautana, im tzelotehon uvautehon dekhal-beyt iserael, kodam avuna devishemaya, veimeru amen. Amen.
תִּתְקַבַּל צְלוֹתָֽנָא וּבָעוּתָֽנָא, עִם צְלֽוֹתְהֽוֹן וּבָעֽוּתְהֽוֹן דְּכָל־בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל, קֳדָם אֲבֽוּנָא דְבִשְׁמַיָּא, וְאִמְרוּ אָמֵן. אמן.
May there be abundant peace from heaven, and good life upon us and upon all Israel; and say, Amen.
Yehe shelama raba min shemaya, chaiym, vesava, viyshuah, venechamah, vesheyzava, urefuah, ugeulah, useliychah, vekhaparah, verevach vehatzalah, lanu ulekhal-amo iserael, veimeru amen. Amen.
יְהֵא שְׁלָמָא רַבָּא מִן שְׁמַיָּא, חַיִּים, וְשָׂבָע, וִישׁוּעָה, וְנֶחָמָה, וְשֵׁיזָבָא, וּרְפוּאָה, וּגְאֻלָּה, וּסְלִיחָה, וְכַפָּרָה, וְרֶֽוַח וְהַצָּלָה, לָֽנוּ וּלְכָל־עַמּוֹ יִשְׂרָאֵל, וְאִמְרוּ אָמֵן. אמן.
נכון שיפסע שלש פסיעות לאחוריו קודם שיאמר "עושה שלום".
It is proper to take three steps backward before saying "Oseh Shalom" (He who makes peace).
He who makes peace in His heights, may He make peace upon us and upon all Israel; and say, Amen.
Oseh shalom bimeromayv, hu berachamayv yaaseh shalom aleynu, veal kal-amo iserael, veimeru amen. Amen.
עֹשֶֽׂה שָׁלוֹם בִּמְרוֹמָֽיו, הוּא בְּרַחֲמָיו יַעֲשֶׂה שָׁלוֹם עָלֵֽינוּ, וְעַל כָּל־עַמּוֹ יִשְׂרָאֵל, וְאִמְרוּ אָמֵן. אמן.
34 Shir HaMa'alot (Psalm 130) שִׁיר הַֽמַּֽעֲלוֹת מִמַּֽעֲמַקִּים
A Song of Ascents. From the depths I call You, O Lord. O Lord, hear my voice; let Your ears be attentive to the voice of my supplications. If You preserved iniquities, O Lord, who could stand? For with You there is forgiveness, that You may be feared. I have hoped in the Lord; my soul has hoped, and for His word I have waited. My soul yearns for the Lord, more than watchmen for the morning. Let Israel hope in the Lord, for with the Lord there is kindness, and with Him is abundant redemption. And He will redeem Israel from all its iniquities.
Shiyr hamaalot mimaamakiym keratiykha Adonai: Adonay shimeah vekoliy tiheyeynah azeneykha kashuvot lekol tachanunay: Im-avonot tishemar-yah adonay miy yaamod: Kiy-imekha haseliychah lemaan tivare: Kiviytiy Adonai kivetah nafeshiy velidevaro hochaletiy: Nafeshiy ladonay mishomeriym laboker shomeriym laboker: Yachel iserael el-Adonai kiy-im-Adonai hachesed veharebeh imo fedut: Vehu ifedeh et-iserael mikol avonotayv:
שִׁיר הַֽמַּֽעֲלוֹת מִמַּֽעֲמַקִּים קְרָאתִיךָ יְהֹוָֽה: אֲדֹנָי שִׁמְעָה בְקוֹלִי תִּֽהְיֶינָה אָזְנֶיךָ קַשֻּׁבוֹת לְקוֹל תַּֽחֲנוּנָֽי: אִם־עֲוֹנוֹת תִּשְׁמָר־יָהּ אֲדֹנָי מִי יַֽעֲמֹֽד: כִּֽי־עִמְּךָ הַסְּלִיחָה לְמַעַן תִּוָּרֵֽא: קִוִּיתִי יְהֹוָה קִוְּתָה נַפְשִׁי וְֽלִדְבָרוֹ הוֹחָֽלְתִּי: נַפְשִׁי לַֽאדֹנָי מִשֹּֽׁמְרִים לַבֹּקֶר שֹֽׁמְרִים לַבֹּֽקֶר: יַחֵל יִשְׂרָאֵל אֶל־יְהֹוָה כִּֽי־עִם־יְהֹוָה הַחֶסֶד וְהַרְבֵּה עִמּוֹ פְדֽוּת: וְהוּא יִפְדֶּה אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵל מִכֹּל עֲוֹֽנֹתָֽיו:
ואומרים קדיש "יהא שלמא"
The Kaddish "Yehei Shelama" is recited.
May His great Name be exalted and sanctified in the world He created according to His will. May He establish His kingdom, cause His salvation to sprout, and bring near His anointed, in your lifetime and in your days, speedily and soon; and say Amen. May His great Name be blessed forever and ever. Blessed, praised, glorified, exalted, extolled, honored, elevated, and lauded be the Name of the Holy One, blessed is He, above all blessings, songs, praises, and consolations uttered in the world; and say Amen.
Itegadal veitekadash shemeh raba. Amen. Bealema diy vera kireuteh, veyameliykh malekhuteh, veyatzemach purekaneh, viykarev meshiycheh. Amen. Bechayeykhon uveyomeykhon uvechayey dekhal-beyt iserael, baagala uvizeman kariyv, veimeru amen. Amen. Yehe shemeh raba mevarakh lealam ulealemey alemaya itebarakh, veishetabach, veitepaar, veiteromam, veitenase, veitehadar, veitealeh, veitehalal, shemeh dekudesha beriykh hu. Amen. Leela min kal-birekhata, shiyrata, tishebechata venechamata, daamiyran bealema, veimeru amen. Amen.
יִתְגַּדַּל וְיִתְקַדַּשׁ שְׁמֵהּ רַבָּא. אמן. בְּעָֽלְמָא דִי בְרָא כִּרְעוּתֵהּ, וְיַמְלִיךְ מַלְכוּתֵהּ, וְיַצְמַח פּוּרְקָנֵהּ, וִיקָרֵב מְשִׁיחֵהּ. אמן. בְּחַיֵּיכוֹן וּבְיוֹמֵיכוֹן וּבְחַיֵּי דְכָל־בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל, בַּעֲגָלָא וּבִזְמַן קָרִיב, וְאִמְרוּ אָמֵן. אמן. יְהֵא שְׁמֵהּ רַבָּא מְבָרַךְ לְעָלַם וּלְעָֽלְמֵי עָֽלְמַיָּֽא יִתְבָּרַךְ, וְיִשְׁתַּבַּח, וְיִתְפָּאַר, וְיִתְרוֹמַם, וְיִתְנַשֵּׂא, וְיִתְהַדָּר, וְיִתְעַלֶּה, וְיִתְהַלָּל, שְׁמֵהּ דְּקוּדְשָׁא בְּרִיךְ הוּא. אמן. לְעֵֽלָּא מִן כָּל־בִּרְכָתָא, שִׁירָתָא, תִּשְׁבְּחָתָא וְנֶחָמָתָא, דַּאֲמִירָן בְּעָֽלְמָא, וְאִמְרוּ אָמֵן. אמן.
May there be abundant peace from heaven, and good life upon us and upon all Israel; and say, Amen.
Yehe shelama raba min shemaya, chaiym, vesava, viyshuah, venechamah, vesheyzava, urefuah, ugeulah, useliychah, vekhaparah, verevach vehatzalah, lanu ulekhal-amo iserael, veimeru amen. Amen.
יְהֵא שְׁלָמָא רַבָּא מִן שְׁמַיָּא, חַיִּים, וְשָׂבָע, וִישׁוּעָה, וְנֶחָמָה, וְשֵׁיזָבָא, וּרְפוּאָה, וּגְאֻלָּה, וּסְלִיחָה, וְכַפָּרָה, וְרֶֽוַח וְהַצָּלָה, לָֽנוּ וּלְכָל־עַמּוֹ יִשְׂרָאֵל, וְאִמְרוּ אָמֵן. אמן.
נכון שיפסע שלש פסיעות לאחוריו קודם שיאמר "עושה שלום".
It is proper to take three steps backward before saying "Oseh Shalom" (He who makes peace).
He who makes peace in His heights, may He make peace upon us and upon all Israel; and say, Amen.
Oseh shalom bimeromayv, hu berachamayv yaaseh shalom aleynu, veal kal-amo iserael, veimeru amen. Amen.
עֹשֶֽׂה שָׁלוֹם בִּמְרוֹמָֽיו, הוּא בְּרַחֲמָיו יַעֲשֶׂה שָׁלוֹם עָלֵֽינוּ, וְעַל כָּל־עַמּוֹ יִשְׂרָאֵל, וְאִמְרוּ אָמֵן. אמן.
May our prayers ascend before the Throne of Glory, and may we be inscribed and sealed in the Book of Life.
Seder HaSelichot · Sephardic Rite
